### [148] [mcp-proto-okn: Natural-language access to open scientific knowledge graphs through the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30283)
*Peter W. Rose,Benjamin M. Good,Amanda M. Saravia-Butler,Charlotte A. Nelson,James P. Balhoff,Yaphet Kebede,Patricia L. Whetzel,Christopher Bizon,Andrew I. Su,Sergio E. Baranzini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: mcp-proto-okn 是一个基于 Python 的模型上下文协议服务器，通过自然语言使 AI 助手能够发现、查询和整合科学知识图谱，降低了跨领域知识图谱分析的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低生物医学和科学用户进行跨领域知识图谱分析的门槛，使 AI 助手能够通过自然语言与科学知识图谱进行交互。

Method: 使用 Python 和 FastMCP 框架实现，提供图路由、模式检查、SPARQL 执行、本体扩展、多图查询和转录生成等功能。

Result: 实现了一个功能完整的 MCP 服务器，支持通过自然语言发现、检查、查询和整合科学知识图谱，代码已在 GitHub 上开源。

Conclusion: mcp-proto-okn 有效降低了科学知识图谱的访问和分析门槛，为生物医学和科学用户提供了一种通过自然语言与知识图谱交互的新方式。

Abstract: MCP Server Proto-OKN (mcp-proto-okn) is a Python-based Model Context Protocol server that enables AI assistants to discover, inspect, query and integrate scientific knowledge graphs through natural language. The server provides graph routing, schema inspection, SPARQL execution, ontology expansion, multi-graph querying, and transcript generation, lowering the barrier to cross-domain knowledge graph analysis for biomedical and scientific users.
  mcp-proto-okn is implemented in Python using the FastMCP framework and is available at https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn. Documentation, client configuration instructions, and example analysis transcripts are provided in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [149] [ProjectionBench: Evaluating Scientific Hypothesis Generation in LLMs Under Progressive Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30284)
*A. J. Lew,Y. Cao,M. J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个渐进式信息揭示的基准框架，用于评估LLM在科研发现中的创新推理能力，通过语义相似度将模型生成假设与原文结论对比。在45篇论文上评估GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 pro和Gemini 3.1 pro，发现GPT-5.4在最小信息下仍能保持0.7 F1与真实结论对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多通过多跳检索评估LLM的深度研究能力，但缺乏对其创新性推理能力（科学发现核心）的系统测试。

Method: 构建渐进式信息揭示框架：模型从仅接收论文主题和研究问题开始，逐步获得技术细节，在每一阶段生成假设，通过原子断言的自动语义相似度与原文结论对比，衡量从创新性到基础推理能力的渐进表现。

Result: GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 pro优于前代；GPT-5.4在最小上下文下仍保持0.7 F1与真实结论的对齐。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统评估LLM的科学推理与发现能力提供了基础，对下一代AI科学家/协同科学家系统的开发至关重要。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is an inherently creative and uncertain process, requiring reasoning beyond the recall of known knowledge. While many benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on deep research tasks via multi-hop retrieval, their innovative reasoning abilities essential for true scientific discovery remain largely untested. We introduce a benchmark framework for evaluating model performance in scientific discovery and reasoning, building up from a raw problem to the classical null hypothesis test. In our framework, models initially receive only the topic and research question from a recent paper, with technical details progressively revealed. At each stage of information disclosure, the model is tasked with generating hypotheses that address the research question, which is compared with the conclusions from the original paper and evaluated via automated semantic similarity of constituent atomic claims. This progressive evaluation of semantic divergence from ground-truth conclusions enables assessment of a model's innovativeness (under minimal information) to grounded reasoning capabilities (under full experimental details), both critical for using LLMs for scientific discovery purposes. Our framework provides a foundation for systematically evaluating scientific reasoning and discovery capabilities in LLMs, crucial for advancing the development of next-generation AI scientist/co-scientist systems. Specifically, here we evaluate GPT-5, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 pro, and Gemini 3.1 pro preview across 45 papers spanning bioactive materials, mechanical materials, and nanomaterials. We find that GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 pro outperform their previous generation counterparts as expected, and GPT-5.4 in particular maintains 0.7 F1 score alignment with ground truth conclusions even under minimal context.

</details>


### [150] [MIRA: Mid-training Rubric Anchoring for Source-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30288)
*Haowen Wang,Yaxin Du,Jian Yang,Jiajun Wu,Shukai Liu,Yuxuan Zhang,Pingjie Wang,Siheng Chen,Tuney Zheng,Ming Zhou,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIRA框架，通过自锚定规则发现实现面向代码中训练的源感知数据过滤，在仅使用一半token的情况下匹配全语料性能并超越多种选择基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中训练阶段的数据选择问题具有独特性：数据在预训练规模下优化但面向下游能力，且来自异构源。现有方法要么可扩展但信号隐式，要么语义强但假设固定规则或标准化格式，无法同时满足可扩展性和源自适应语义标准。

Method: 提出MIRA（源感知过滤框架），基于自锚定规则发现。首先为每个源组自动发现应评估什么规则，然后将这些判断蒸馏到可扩展的学生评分器中，用于全语料过滤。

Result: 在面向代码的中训练场景（21个数据源、5个源组）上，MIRA在九个代码基准测试中超越多种选择基线，并在仅使用一半token的情况下匹配全语料训练的性能。

Conclusion: 将规则构建纳入数据选择过程本身，通过先发现各源组评估指标再蒸馏为可扩展评分器的方式，有效解决了中训练数据选择中可扩展性与语义自适应性之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Mid-training has become an important stage in modern LLM development, using large-scale curated mixtures to strengthen capabilities before final post-training. Its data selection problem is distinct: the data are optimized under a pretraining-style objective at near-pretraining scale, but are curated toward downstream capabilities and drawn from heterogeneous sources with different formats and training roles. As a result, effective selection requires both scalability and source-adaptive semantic criteria. Existing model-based methods scale well, but provide only implicit quality signals. Semantic selection methods offer stronger judgments, but usually assume fixed rubrics or standardized data formats. To address this mismatch, we propose MIRA, a source-aware filtering framework based on self-anchored rubric discovery. The key idea is to make rubric construction part of data selection: MIRA first discovers what should be evaluated for each source group, then distills those judgments into scalable student scorers for full-corpus filtering. On code-oriented mid-training with 21 sources and 5 source groups, MIRA outperforms selection baselines across nine code benchmarks and matches the full-corpus run while using only half the tokens.

</details>


### [151] [Demystifying Data Organization for Enhanced LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30334)
*Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Tongshen Yang,Yonghan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wenshan Wu,Qihao Zhao,Hao Li,Yuanyuan Gao,Kim-Hui Yap,Scarlett Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了数据组织方式对LLM训练的影响，提出了四项优化准则（边界锐化、循环调度、课程连贯性、局部多样性）及两种新排序方法（STR和SAW），实验证明能提升训练稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM训练中，数据选择（data selection）已得到广泛研究，但数据组织（data organization）这一直接影响训练效果的环节却鲜有探索。尤其是LLM通常只训练一个或少量epoch，数据顺序对学习效果的影响更为显著。本文旨在以最小额外计算开销，系统探究并优化数据组织策略。

Method: 利用预计算好的样本级分数（原用于数据效率），提出四项准则：边界锐化（Boundary Sharpening）、循环调度（Cyclic Scheduling）、课程连贯性（Curriculum Continuity）、局部多样性（Local Diversity）。基于这些准则设计了两种数据排序方法STR和SAW。在多个模型规模和数据量下，对预训练和SFT阶段进行了实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的四项准则有效，STR和SAW两种排序方法能显著提升LLM训练的稳定性和性能，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 数据组织是提升LLM训练效率的重要维度，本文提出的准则和方法为低开销、易部署的训练优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized various fields, yet their training efficiency is heavily reliant on effective data curation. While data selection has been widely studied, the strategic data organization for enhanced training remains an underexplored area, particularly since current LLMs are often trained for only one or a few epochs. This paper systematically explores the influence of data organization on LLM training by reusing pre-computed sample-level scores originally generated for data efficiency, thereby incurring minimal additional computational overhead. We identify and formalize four key guidelines for optimizing data organization: Boundary Sharpening, Cyclic Scheduling, Curriculum Continuity, and Local Diversity. Guided by them, we introduce two novel data ordering methods termed STR and SAW. Extensive experiments across different model scales and data sizes, encompassing both pre-training and SFT stages, validate the effectiveness of our summarized guidelines. They also demonstrate the robustness of our proposed data ordering methods in enhancing the stability and performance of LLM training. Github Link: https://github.com/microsoft/data-efficacy/

</details>


### [152] [Locally Coherent, Globally Incoherent: Bounding Compositional Incoherence in Multi-Component LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30335)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了多组件LLM智能体中"局部一致、全局不一致"的概率组合失效问题，提出组合残差ε*、乘积结构二分法、瑞利商预测、Boyle-Dykstra投影修复和任意时间有效e-过程等理论工具与算法，在1,876个集成团上实证了该问题的普遍性（33-94%出现非零残差）及三种LLM端缓解策略的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组件LLM智能体各部分仅观察联合问题的局部，各自内部概率一致但组合后违反基本概率公理，现有工作缺乏对此类失效的形式化刻画与可计算方法。

Method: 1) 形式化定义组合残差ε*（组合输出到联合一致多面体的L2距离）；2) 乘积结构二分法刻画局部一致性保证全局一致的条件；3) 瑞利商预测残差大小；4) 层级Boyle-Dykstra投影进行确定性修复；5) 任意时间有效e-过程实现顺序一致性监控；6) 在1,876个集成团上实证并测试三种LLM端缓解策略。

Result: ε*在33-94%的集成团上非零，在比例分配规则下每注产生+0.115 nats的遗憾；当采用自身一致化的投注者时增益降至+0.006 nats。三种LLM端缓解策略（检索、分区感知提示、聚合器LLM）均失败或导致性能倒退。

Conclusion: 多组件LLM智能体的概率组合不连贯性是普遍且顽固的问题，现有直观缓解策略无效，本文提出的形式化框架与修复算法为后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Multi-component LLM agents assemble probabilistic claims from components that each see only part of a joint problem; the composition can violate basic probability axioms even when every component is locally coherent. We formalise this locally coherent, globally incoherent failure via the compositional residual eps*, the L2 distance from the composed quote to the joint coherent polytope, computable at runtime from system output and the declared cross-component coupling constraints. A product-structure dichotomy characterises when local coherence suffices, and a Rayleigh-quotient prediction matches the observed residual within 7% on three of four relation classes. A hierarchical Boyle-Dykstra projection repairs the composition deterministically; an anytime-valid e-process gives sequential coherence monitoring. Across 1,876 ensemble cliques on a four-LLM mid-tier panel (frontier-panel rerun in Section 5.5), eps* > 0 on 33-94% of cliques, translating to +0.115 nats per bet of regret on 1,770 resolved bets under the proportional allocation rule (the gain collapses to +0.006 under bettors that themselves coherentise). Three intuitive LLM-side mitigations(retrieval, partition-aware prompting, aggregator-LLM) each fail or regress.

</details>


### [153] [Tiny but Trusted: Efficient Vision-Language Reasoning for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30344)
*Xiaona Zhou,Muntasir Wahed,Tianjiao Yu,Constantin Brif,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型在时序异常检测中缺乏可解释性问题，构建了VisAnomBench基准数据集（基于公开时序数据+大模型生成的高质量解释），并微调得到参数高效的VisAnomReasoner模型。实验表明，该方法在异常定位精度和F1分数上分别提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点，并在跨基准测试中展现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在时序异常检测中表现不佳，且公开异常检测基准仅提供区间标注而无自然语言解释，难以微调VLM产生可解释的决策。

Method: 构建VisAnomBench基准，从公开时序数据集中筛选并利用多个大型VLM生成高质量异常解释；在此基础上微调参数高效的VisAnomReasoner模型。

Result: VisAnomReasoner在VisAnomBench上精确率和F1分别提升至少21.23和23.87个百分点；在TSB-AD-U跨基准测试中，精确率和F1分别提升9.57和13.39个百分点。

Conclusion: VisAnomReasoner实现了更准确的异常定位，一致优于所有基线方法，并具备良好的跨基准泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance across many tasks, yet prior studies report unsatisfactory performance when applying large language or multimodal models to finding abnormal patterns in sequential data. Public anomaly detection benchmarks typically provide interval annotations but not natural-language rationales, making it difficult to fine-tune VLMs to produce grounded, interpretable decisions. To address this gap, we construct VisAnomBench, a curated benchmark built from public time-series datasets and augmented with high-quality anomaly explanations selected from multiple large VLMs using fine-grained, task-specific rewards. Through fine-tuning on this benchmark, we develop VisAnomReasoner, a parameter-efficient VLM for time-series anomaly detection. Experimental results on VisAnomBench show that VisAnomReasoner achieves more accurate anomaly localization and consistently outperforms all baselines, with improvements of at least 21.23 and 23.87 percentage points in precision and F1, respectively. Additional experiments on the TSB-AD-U benchmark demonstrate strong cross-benchmark generalization, with VisAnomReasoner improving precision and F1 by 9.57 and 13.39 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [154] [SchGen: PCB Schematic Generation with Semantic-Grounded Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30345)
*Qinpei Luo,Ruichun Ma,Xinyu Zhang,Lili Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SchGen，首个能够根据自然语言请求生成可编辑PCB原理图的大语言模型。核心贡献在于设计了一种语义化的代码表征方式（基于相对布局和引脚名称连线），并构建了大规模数据集，实验证明其在连线准确性和功能正确性上显著优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB原理图设计仍高度依赖人工且需要专业知识，虽然生成式AI已应用于IC设计，但从自然语言直接生成PCB原理图这一方向尚属空白。

Method: 1) 提出语义化的代码表征：将几何驱动的问题转化为语义驱动的匹配任务，包含相对布局和基于引脚名的连线原语。2) 构建大规模数据集：通过人机协作流程将开源硬件设计转换为该表征。3) 训练SchGen大语言模型。

Result: SchGen在连线准确性和功能正确性上显著优于替代表征方法甚至更大规模的通用的LLM。

Conclusion: 表征设计是使生成模型胜任复杂硬件设计任务的关键因素。

Abstract: Printed circuit board (PCB) schematic design defines nearly all electronic hardware, but it remains manual and expertise-intensive. While generative AI has advanced digital and analog IC design, PCB schematic generation from natural-language intent is largely unexplored. This paper presents SchGen, the first large language model that generates editable PCB schematics from natural-language requests. The key challenge lies in the lack of an LLM-suited representation and a large-scale dataset. Current schematic formats are dominated by verbose, tool-specific syntax and geometry-heavy descriptions, making them difficult to generate reliably. We introduce a semantically grounded code representation that encodes schematic editing primitives with relative placement and pin-name-based wiring, transforming a geometry-driven generation problem into a semantics-driven matching task amenable to LLMs. We further construct a large-scale dataset of PCB schematics paired with user prompts via a human-agent collaborative pipeline that converts open-source hardware designs into our representation. Experiments show that SchGen significantly outperforms alternative representations and even larger general-purpose LLMs on wire connectivity accuracy and functional correctness. Our results highlight the critical role of representation design in enabling generative models for complex hardware design tasks.

</details>


### [155] [Physics Is All You Need? A Case Study in Physicist-Supervised AI Development of Scientific Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30353)
*Nhat-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一位物理学家在12个工作日内监督AI编码智能体（Claude Code）构建可微分的单圈微扰论模块（CLAX-PT），记录了15个监督事件。智能体能自主解决10个问题，但无法解决3个未被oracle测试捕获的问题——其共同特征是智能体将症状缓解当作根本原因解决。研究发现，监督设计而非模型能力决定了输出是否可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化评估AI智能体在理论物理编码中的自主性边界与监督需求，检验现有智能体是否具备区分预测充分性与解释正确性的能力，以及能否跳出给定架构提出替代方案。

Method: N=1案例研究：一名物理学家在12个工作日、57个会话中监督Claude Code（Sonnet和Opus模型）编写CLAX-PT代码，记录并分类15个干预事件，分析其解决路径与失败模式。

Result: 智能体自主解决10个事件，2个需物理学家领域知识介入；3个无法解决的事件中，智能体将症状缓解当作根本原因，33/57的会话用于调整错误架构中的系数，无法重新评估分支选择。另发现校准后的fudge因子能通过所有oracle测试但在其他宇宙学参数下预测错误。

Conclusion: 三种监督实践（多样参数点测试、共享变更日志、禁止非物理数值补丁）对捕获oracle测试遗漏的错误至关重要。当前智能体缺乏提出架构替代方案和区分预测充分性与解释正确性的能力，这些无法通过单纯规模扩展解决。

Abstract: Are AI agents tools, co-authors, or researchers? We present a quantified case study ($N=1$): a physicist supervising an AI coding agent (Claude Code, Sonnet and Opus models) over 12 work days and 57 sessions to build CLAX-PT, a differentiable one-loop perturbation theory module in JAX. We documented and classified 15 supervision events by intervention level.
  The agent resolved ten autonomously by iterating against oracle tests. Two more by the physicist's domain knowledge. The three it could not -- all evaded oracle detection -- share a common property: the agent treated symptom reduction as root-cause resolution. It spent 33 of the 57 sessions adjusting coefficients within a code architecture that could not represent the target physics, and could not re-evaluate its CLASS-PT branch choice even when prompted to reconsider; only an injected physics concept (anisotropic BAO damping) triggered the redesign. Separately, the agent committed a calibrated correction that passed all oracle tests but corresponded to no quantity in the theory, predicting wrong values at any other cosmology.
  The fudge factor was caught and replaced within the same session. Three supervision practices proved critical for catching what oracle tests missed: testing at diverse parameter points beyond the fiducial calibration; shared changelogs that surfaced stalled exploration across sessions; and an explicit rule against unphysical numerical patches. In this case, supervision design, not model capability, determined whether the agent's output was trustworthy. Closing the gap would require agents that propose architectural alternatives rather than optimize within a given structure, and distinguish predictive adequacy from explanatory correctness -- capabilities not exhibited here, not obviously addressed by scaling alone. [Abridged.]

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Dynamics of Stochastic Momentum with Sparse Updates in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28961)
*Katie Everett,Elliot Paquette*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了动量方法在稀疏更新场景下的动力学，发现动量与SGD的等价性、不稳定性和经典重球动力学的三种相态取决于学习与保留两个时间尺度的比值，并揭示了不同token稀疏度下动量值的谱冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动量理论假设梯度均匀到达每个参数，但实际中重尾数据分布和现代架构违反了这一假设，需要理解稀疏更新下动量的真实行为。

Method: 分析了两个可处理模型（稀疏输入最小二乘、稀有类逻辑回归）的闭式二阶矩动力学，在高维极限下刻画了稀疏度、批大小和动量衰减三个标度指数的相结构。

Result: 相结构由两个时间尺度比决定：动量保留时间尺度（缓冲存活多少活跃更新）与学习时间尺度（减少平方误差所需活跃更新数）。学习远慢于保留时等价于SGD，学习更快时不稳定，时间尺度相当时恢复经典重球动力学。不同token稀疏度下振荡动力学对应的动量值不同，导致全局动量存在谱冲突。

Conclusion: 动量方法在稀疏更新场景下存在复杂的相变行为，全局动量无法同时适配不同频率的token，为理解和改进实际中的动量使用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Existing theory of momentum assumes that gradients arrive at every parameter at a roughly constant rate, an assumption violated in practice by heavy-tailed data distributions and modern architectures. We theoretically analyze the dynamics of two tractable models of momentum under sparse updates: a least squares model with sparse inputs and a logistic regression model with a rare class. Both admit exact closed-form second-moment dynamics whose high-dimensional limits we characterize across three scaling exponents for sparsity, batch size, and momentum decay. The phase structure on both problems is governed by the ratio of two intrinsic timescales: a momentum retention timescale (how many active updates the buffer survives) and a learning timescale (how many active updates it takes to reduce the squared error). When learning is much slower than retention, the limit matches SGD; when learning is faster, the system is unstable; where the timescales coincide, we recover classical heavy-ball dynamics. The oscillatory dynamics occur at different momentum values for different token sparsity, creating a spectral conflict for global momentum across token frequencies.

</details>


### [157] [Anytime-Valid Federated Conformal RAG for LLM Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29139)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Anytime-FC-RAG，将联邦共形检索增强生成（FC-RAG）从固定水平扩展到任意时刻有效的序贯覆盖保证，通过可累积的校准偏差预算和截断赌博e过程，在无需额外假设的前提下实现时间一致报警、Hoeffding拼接累积误覆盖包络、可预测自适应控制下的安全性以及训练侧误差传播控制，实验验证了14-57%的带宽节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FC-RAG仅在固定水平上提供分布自由的覆盖保证，无法保证在任意停止时间（序贯监测场景）下的有效性。朴素序贯组合因边际覆盖界导致赌博e过程在不利校准样本上不是超鞅，无法调用Ville不等式，需要新的理论框架。

Method: 1) 构建可累积的逐步校准偏差预算，将边际覆盖界转化为校准良好事件上的严格条件界；2) 设计截断赌博e过程，使其在全概率空间上为非负超鞅；3) 组合上述两个成分获得四重保证；4) 引入可预测自适应控制器，当e过程超过警告阈值时才升级检索带宽。

Result: 在GPT-2-small + MiniLM群组上对MMLU、DBpedia、AG News数据集的实验验证了理论预测的报警率、检测延迟、包络覆盖率，并实现了14-57%的带宽节省；报警仅在覆盖真正失效时触发。

Conclusion: Anytime-FC-RAG成功将联邦共形序贯预测扩展到任意时间有效的场景，在不增加假设成本的前提下提供了更强的理论保证和实际通信效率提升，为弱语言模型群组的自适应部署提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Federated Conformal RAG (FC-RAG) provides distribution-free coverage for a bandwidth-limited swarm of weak language models, but only at a fixed horizon. We extend it to anytime-valid sequential coverage: validity at every stopping time, preserved under predictable adaptive control (recalibration, per-node bandwidth escalation, distilled-student refresh), at no extra cost in assumptions over fixed-horizon FC-RAG. Naive composition fails because FC-RAG's marginal coverage bound makes the betting e-process a non-supermartingale on adverse calibration draws, and Ville's inequality cannot be invoked. We give Anytime-FC-RAG, a sequential extension built on a summable per-step calibration-deviation budget that converts the marginal bound into a strict conditional bound on a calibration-good event, paired with a truncated betting e-process that is a nonnegative supermartingale on the entire probability space. From these two ingredients, we obtain four guarantees: time-uniform alarm validity $\mathbb{P}(\sup_t E_t \ge 1/δ_e) \le δ_e + δ_{\mathrm{cal}}$, a Hoeffding-stitched cumulative-miscoverage envelope at the same total budget, safety under any predictable controller (recalibration, bandwidth escalation, student refresh), and training-side error propagation across an unbounded sequence of Federated Probe-Logit Distillation (FPLD) refreshes via a summable training budget. As a practical consequence, an adaptive controller that escalates retrieval bandwidth only when the e-process crosses a warning threshold matches the alarm rate of a fixed-high-bandwidth schedule at substantially lower communication cost. Experiments on a GPT-2-small + MiniLM swarm across MMLU, DBpedia, and AG News verify the predicted alarm rate, detection delay, envelope coverage, and $14$-$57\%$ bandwidth savings; the alarm fires when and only when coverage genuinely breaks.

</details>


### [158] [Deep Optimal Individualized Treatment Rules for Bivariate Survival Outcomes via Adaptive Prediction-Powered Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29464)
*Kun Ren,Yifan Cui,Wen Su*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对多治疗随机试验中的双变量生存结局，提出了一种基于深度神经网络的个性化治疗规则优化方法，通过随机策略和边际加速失效时间模型联合链接函数建模双变量依赖性，并引入自适应预测增强方法提升决策鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多治疗随机试验中，双变量生存结局为决策制定带来了显著分析挑战，现有方法难以有效优化个性化治疗规则以最大化联合生存概率。

Method: 通过深度神经网络建模治疗规则为随机策略，利用链接函数耦合边际加速失效时间模型以捕捉双变量依赖性，并引入自适应预测增强方法利用机器学习模型的辅助预测来提升鲁棒性和有效性。

Result: 提出了一个新颖的框架，能够处理删失数据，并有效利用辅助预测信息增强决策效果。

Conclusion: 该方法为多治疗双变量生存结局下的个性化治疗规则优化提供了有效解决方案，兼顾了双变量依赖性和右删失问题。

Abstract: In randomized trials involving multiple treatments, bivariate survival outcomes present significant analytical challenges for making decisions. This paper addresses the problem of deriving optimal individualized treatment rules to maximize the joint survival probability beyond fixed time points $(t_1, t_2)$ through deep neural networks, while accounting for right censoring. We propose a novel approach that models treatment rules via stochastic policies, coupling marginal accelerated failure time models via link function to capture bivariate dependence. To enhance robustness and effectiveness of decision making, we introduce an adaptive prediction-powered method that leverages auxiliary predictions from machine learning models.

</details>


### [159] [Eigen-Spike Emergence and Quadratic Equivalents for Conjugate Kernels on Nonlinearly Separable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29669)
*Collin Cranston,Zhichao Wang,Todd Kemp,Michael W. Mahoney*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用随机矩阵理论中的确定性等价方法，研究了前馈神经网络的共轭核矩阵在XOR非线性可分数据下的信息特征值涌现与相变条件，为线性分类在非线性特征空间中的可行性提供了精确的理论刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性等价方法虽然能将复杂非线性随机矩阵简化为线性代理模型，但这一理想化的线性等价在处理非线性可分数据（如XOR问题）时是否仍然有效尚不清楚。作者旨在弥合RMT理论工具与机器学习实际需求之间的差距。

Method: 以XOR问题为非线性可分数据集，研究前馈NN的共轭核矩阵；构建稳健的二次型等价矩阵替换尖峰CK矩阵；通过分析信息性离群特征值及其特征向量与XOR标签的渐近对齐程度，刻画样本复杂度、信噪比、非线性激活函数选择和预训练特征等场景下的BBP型相变。

Result: 在样本复杂度、信噪比、非线性激活选择及预训练特征等不同场景下，推导出精确的BBP型相变条件，明确了线性分类通过CK特征向量变得可行的临界点。

Conclusion: 将RMT中的确定性等价工具扩展到对ML实践中具有非线性可分数据的问题分析之中，为理解非线性特征映射中的可学习性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Recent work in random matrix theory (RMT) has developed the notion of deterministic equivalents: typically linear surrogate models that approximate the spectral behavior of large nonlinear random matrices, such as nonlinear feature maps in neural networks (NNs). On the one hand, these deterministic equivalents make theoretical predictions tractable by reducing a complex model to a simpler model with properties that fall under the umbrella of classical RMT tools. However, this leaves open the question of whether this idealized linear equivalence remains meaningful when dealing with high-dimensional nonlinearly separable data, such as performing clssification on nonlinearly separable data. Motivated by this, we consider the conjugate kernel (CK), which is the nonlinear feature map of a feedforward NN, under a canonical nonlinearly separable dataset, the XOR problem; and we use the study of informative outlier eigenvalues in the CK and whether their corresponding eigenvectors asymptotically align with XOR labels as a proxy for nonlinear learnability. We develop a robust quadratic equivalent to the spiked CK matrix that enables a precise analysis of emergent informative spikes, as one modifies various knobs common in ML practice: sample complexity, signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), nonlinear activation choice, and pretrained features. In each of these scenarios, we derive a precise BBP-type phase transition in which linear classification via the CK eigenvectors becomes possible. Our analysis helps translate the power of deterministic equivalence tools in RMT to study problems of practical relevance in ML.

</details>


### [160] [Instance-dependent Stochastic Lipschitz bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29748)
*Marius Potfer,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对Lipschitz bandit问题，提出了一种新的算法和分析框架，通过积分次优性间隙来刻画遗憾界，从而获得比传统zooming维度方法更细粒度的实例依赖界，并在最大化器集具有正维度时取得严格改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Lipschitz bandit的遗憾界要么是最坏情形界，要么是基于zooming维度的部分实例依赖界。然而zooming维度仅依赖于近最优水平集的渐近增长，无法捕捉函数f更精细的结构性质。作者旨在提供一种能适应水平集局部增长而非仅渐近行为的更细粒度遗憾分析。

Method: 提出一种新算法，通过积分f在其水平集上的次优性间隙来刻画遗憾；利用水平集的局部增长特性而非仅渐近行为进行自适应分析；并将分析扩展到完全信息设置（Lipschitz experts）。

Result: 当最大化器集维度d^*>0时，得到改进的自适应速率阶为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{d_z+1/\max(d_z,d^*)+2})$，严格优于经典zooming界。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了比zooming维度更精细的实例依赖遗憾界，能适应水平集的局部增长，并在最大化器集有正维度时取得理论上的严格改进；此外还扩展到了完全信息设置并放宽了部分正则性假设。

Abstract: We study the Lipschitz bandit problem, where a learner sequentially maximizes an unknown Lipschitz function $f$ over a domain $\mathcal{X} \subset [0,1]^d$ using noisy pointwise evaluations. Existing regret bounds are either worst-case, scaling as $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d+1/d+2}\right )$, or adaptive via the zooming dimension $d_z$, yielding $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d_z+1/d_z+2}\right )$. However, such zooming-based guarantees are only partially instance-dependent, as they depend solely on the asymptotic growth of near-optimal level sets and fail to capture finer structural properties of $f$. We provide an analysis and an algorithm that characterizes the regret through integrals of the suboptimality gap of $f$ over its level sets. This yields regret bounds that adapt to the local growth of level sets, rather than only their asymptotic behavior. As a corollary, when the set of maximizers has dimension $d^\star>0$, we obtain improved adaptive rates of order $\tilde{\mathcal{O}} \left ( T^{d_z+1 / \max(d_z,d^\star)+2}\right )$ strictly improving over classical zooming bounds in this regime. Finally, we extend our analysis to the full-information setting (Lipschitz experts) and show how some of the regularity assumptions can be relaxed.

</details>


### [161] [Joint Model and Data Sparsification via the Marginal Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29908)
*Alexander Timans,Thomas Möllenhoff,Christian A. Naesseth,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合学习特征和样本相关性的对称稀疏贝叶斯学习方法，通过单一贝叶斯目标同时实现模型和数据稀疏化，在保持共轭性和闭式更新的同时，显著提升了鲁棒回归性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏贝叶斯学习（SBL）依赖同方差噪声模型，对异常值和噪声误设敏感，导致模型拟合和预测性能下降。

Method: 提出联合学习个体特征和样本相关性（Joint ARD），在单一贝叶斯目标下同时实现模型和数据双重稀疏化。该方法保持共轭性，支持标准优化过程的闭式更新，并与鲁棒回归和影响函数视角一致。

Result: 在多种回归任务上的实证结果表明，联合ARD方法一致地生成了既稀疏又鲁棒的预测模型。

Conclusion: 对称剪枝（同时剪枝模型和数据）是扩展ARD的自然方式，能有效提升稀疏贝叶斯学习对数据污染的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Sparse recovery in linear systems underpins applications from signal processing to high-dimensional regression. Sparse Bayesian Learning, grounded in the principle of automatic relevance determination (ARD), offers a practical Bayesian mechanism for feature sparsity via marginal likelihood optimization. Yet, its reliance on a homoscedastic noise model renders it sensitive to data contaminations such as outliers or misspecified noise, harming model fit and predictions. Instead, we propose jointly learning individual feature and sample relevancies, enabling simultaneous model and data sparsification via a single Bayesian objective. This symmetric pruning of model and data offers a natural extension that preserves conjugacy, admits closed-form updates for standard optimization procedures, and aligns with perspectives from robust regression and influence functions. Empirical results across diverse regression tasks affirm that a joint ARD approach consistently yields both sparse and robust prediction models.

</details>


### [162] [Diffusion Models Are Statistically Optimal for Learning Low-Dimensional Multi-Modal Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30153)
*Jingda Wu,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在低维流形结构上的统计样本复杂度，证明其仅需约$\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$个样本即可在1-Wasserstein距离下达到$\varepsilon$误差，其中$k$为内在维度，该收敛率近最优且仅依赖于内在维度，显著优于传统遭受维度诅咒的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型理论依赖于强正则性假设（如均匀有界密度或全局光滑得分函数），无法捕捉低维和多模态这类内在结构。本文旨在为扩散模型在具有低维子空间联合支撑的分布上的统计效率提供理论基础，解释其在高维复杂学习任务中的成功。

Method: 假设每个子空间内的数据分布为次高斯分布，分析了扩散模型学习联合低维子空间支撑分布的样本复杂度，基于1-Wasserstein距离进行误差度量。分析不依赖光滑性、有界密度或对数凹性等假设。

Result: 扩散模型达到$\varepsilon$误差所需的样本复杂度为$\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$，其中$k$为内在维度。该收敛率仅依赖于内在维度，是近最优的，显著改进了现有受维度诅咒的理论保证。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够统计自适应于内在低维结构，同时自然适应多模态数据，为其在复杂高维学习任务中的成功提供了严格的理论依据。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable empirical success in learning high-dimensional distributions, particularly those exhibiting low-dimensional and multi-modal structures. However, theoretical understanding of their statistical efficiency remains limited. Existing theories typically rely on strong regularity assumptions, such as uniformly bounded densities or globally smooth score functions, which fail to capture such intrinsic structures. In this work, we study the sample complexity of diffusion models for learning distributions supported on a union of low-dimensional subspaces. Assuming that the data distribution within each subspace is subgaussian, we show that diffusion models require at most $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$ samples to achieve $\varepsilon$ error in 1-Wasserstein distance, where $k$ is the intrinsic dimension. This near-optimal convergence rate depends only on the intrinsic dimension and significantly improves upon prior theoretical guarantees that suffer from the curse of dimensionality. Notably, our analysis applies to a broad collection of distributions without imposing smoothness, bounded-density, or log-concavity assumptions. Overall, our results show that diffusion models can statistically adapt to intrinsic low-dimensional structure while naturally accommodating multi-modal data, offering a rigorous theoretical justification for their success in complex high-dimensional learning tasks.

</details>


### [163] [Visual Spatial Learning: Single-Field Spatial Interpolation Using Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30167)
*Daniel Tinoco,Raquel Menezes,Carlos Baquero,Alexandra Silva*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CNN的模型，仅利用单个部分观测场进行空间插值，无需外部数据或先验场，在单实例稀疏监督下学习预测未观测点，作为经典地统计方法（如Kriging）的实用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典插值方法（如Kriging）依赖高斯过程假设和变差函数，在非平稳设置中效果有限且需要大量领域专业知识，亟需一种无需显式协方差建模、能灵活捕捉局部空间模式的数据驱动方法。

Method: 采用卷积神经网络(CNN)架构，在单个部分观测场上训练和推理，以观测位置为监督信号，在用户定义网格上预测未观测点，无需外部数据或先验场。

Result: 不需要显式协方差建模或变差函数估计，能够以数据驱动方式灵活捕捉局部空间模式，在稀疏监督下实现单实例空间插值。

Conclusion: CNN方法可作为经典地统计方法的实用替代方案，将CNN的应用扩展到新的问题领域，证明了CNN在单实例空间插值中的潜力。

Abstract: Predicting a complete spatially correlated field from sparse observations is a fundamental challenge in spatial statistics and environmental modelling. Classical interpolation methods such as Kriging rely on Gaussian process assumptions and variography, which can limit their effectiveness in non-stationary settings and require substantial domain expertise. In this work, we leverage an architecture based on convolutional neural networks (CNNs) for spatial interpolation that is trained and applied on a single partially observed field, without access to external data or prior fields. The model is supervised directly on the observed locations and learns to predict values at unobserved points on the user defined grid. Unlike Kriging, our method does not require explicit covariance modelling or variogram estimation, and it can flexibly capture local spatial patterns in a data-driven manner. This work demonstrates the potential of CNNs for single-instance spatial interpolation under sparse supervision, offering a practical alternative to classical geostatistical methods, and extending the use of CNNs to a new problem domain.

</details>


### [164] [Wasserstein Contraction of Coordinate Ascent Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30253)
*Rocco Caprio,Adrien Corenflos,Sam Power*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了坐标上升变分推断算法在Wasserstein距离下的收缩性，证明了在满足固定点处的传输-信息不等式和函数光滑性条件时算法具有收缩性。该结果具有一般性和精确性，支持局部收敛保证，适用于一般光滑流形及某些非光滑空间。应用包括贝叶斯高斯混合模型、高维贝叶斯Probit回归以及使用Pólya-Gamma随机变量的逻辑回归（即Jaakkola-Jordan算法）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析坐标上升变分推断算法在Wasserstein距离下的收敛性质，填补现有理论分析的空白，提供更一般化和精确的收敛保证。

Method: 利用传输-信息不等式（transport-information inequality）和函数光滑性条件，在Wasserstein距离框架下推导CAVI算法的收缩性，并将理论扩展到光滑流形和非光滑空间。

Result: 得到了CAVI算法在Wasserstein距离下的局部收敛保证，结果具有一般性和精确性，适用于多种模型和空间结构。

Conclusion: 该理论分析为CAVI算法提供了更严格的收敛性理解，并验证了其在多个经典贝叶斯模型中的适用性。

Abstract: We study the contraction in Wasserstein distance of the coordinate ascent variational inference algorithm. This is shown to hold under a transport-information inequality at the fixed points and a functional smoothness condition. The results are general and sharp, allow for local convergence guarantees, hold for general smooth manifolds, and also in some non-smooth spaces. We consider applications to Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Models, and high-dimensional Bayesian Probit Regression, and Logistic Regression with Pólya-Gamma random variables (i.e. Jaakkola-Jordan's algorithm).

</details>


### [165] [Leave a Window Out: Modifying the Jackknife for Predictive Inference in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30292)
*Hanyang Jiang,Rina Foygel Barber,Ashwin Pananjady,Yao Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出留窗法（leave-a-window-out, LWO）来克服标准留一法在时间序列中覆盖率失效的问题，在保证有效覆盖的同时比分裂保形预测产生更窄的预测区间，并引入循环可交换性偏离度量来理论证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准保形预测要求数据可交换且预测器无记忆，这在时间序列中不现实；分裂保形预测虽鲁棒但样本分裂会降低准确性，因此需要研究不依赖数据分裂的预测推断方法在时间序列中的可靠性。

Method: 提出留窗法（LWO），对每个待预测点，去除包含该点及其前后邻近点的窗口来拟合模型，基于模型稳定性条件证明其覆盖有效性；引入循环可交换性偏离系数来量化时间依赖性对保形推断的影响。

Result: 理论证明标准留一法在轻度时间依赖的时间序列中覆盖率可任意差；实验表明LWO在标准留一法失效时仍能保持有效覆盖，且区间宽度远窄于分裂保形预测。

Conclusion: LWO为时间序列数据提供了一种无需数据分裂、兼顾覆盖有效性与区间紧致性的保形预测方法，拓展了保形推断在非可交换环境下的适用性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction methods enjoy strong theoretical and empirical predictive inference performance, provided the data is exchangeable, and predictors are trained in a memoryless fashion. However, these assumptions and constraints are impractical in many real-data settings, such as time series (where temporal dependence violates exchangeability, and where memoryless predictors will inevitably have poor predictive accuracy). Recent work shows that the split conformal prediction method is robust to these issues of memory-based predictors and deviations from exchangeability that are common features of time-series data. However, since using sample splitting can lead to lower accuracy, this motivates asking whether other predictive inference methods (that do not rely on data splitting) could also be reliably used in the time series setting.
  In this work, we show that the vanilla leave-one-out jackknife can suffer an arbitrary loss of coverage even in canonical time series models with mild temporal dependence. As a remedy, we propose a careful modification tailored to such settings, which we term the \emph{leave-a-window-out} (LWO) method, and show that it can achieve valid coverage provided that the model-fitting procedure satisfies mild stability properties. Our proofs are based on quantifying the degree to which the data departs from \emph{cyclic exchangeability}, and we introduce new coefficients to measure the extent of this departure. Experiments on time series data demonstrate that our LWO method often enjoys valid coverage when the vanilla jackknife fails to cover, while producing much narrower intervals than split conformal prediction.

</details>


### [166] [Improved Guarantees for Heterogeneous Treatment-Effect Estimation via Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30319)
*Anay Mehrotra,Phuc Tran,Van H. Vu,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在面板数据中估计异质性处理效应的新方法，通过将问题转化为矩阵补全，并在低秩假设下实现了行方向上的精确估计，推导出了首个尖锐的行-wise ℓ2 扰动界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代因果推断的核心目标之一是估计异质性处理效应（HTE），即理解干预如何影响每个个体单元，而不仅仅是平均效应。现有的矩阵补全方法虽可用于估计平均处理效应，但无法给出有意义的每行（每个单元）上的估计保证，因此需要新的理论和方法来突破这一局限。

Method: 将面板数据下的异质性处理效应估计问题形式化为矩阵补全问题，其中数据矩阵由所有单元-时间点的处理效应组成，目标是对矩阵每行的平均值进行估计。作者提出了一种简单、计算高效的估计器，在未知倾向性、标准低秩性和正则性假设下，实现了行-wise ℓ2 误差为 Õ(√(1/n + n/m²))。分析中首次建立了低秩近似的尖锐行-wise ℓ2 扰动界，补充了现有的谱范数、Frobenius 范数和逐元素扰动理论。

Result: 推导出行-wise ℓ2 误差上界为 Õ(√(1/n + n/m²))，该界在理论上是最优的（sharp），并且不需要知道倾向性得分。这是首个针对异质性处理效应估计的每行级别的非渐近保证。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了异质性处理效应估计在行级别理论保证上的空白，所提出的锐利行-wise 扰动界对低秩矩阵近似理论具有独立意义，为面板数据因果推断提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: A central goal of modern causal inference is estimating heterogeneous treatment effects to answer questions like "how does an intervention affect each unit," rather than only on average. We study this problem with panel-data where we observe $n$ units across $m$ times under unknown, non-uniform treatment assignments. The data in this setting is naturally represented as a matrix of all unit--time treatment effects. Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects can then be expressed as obtaining a good estimation of each row's average in this matrix. This allows us to formulate the problem as matrix completion, which can be solved under natural low-rankness assumptions. However, existing matrix-completion guarantees are not powerful enough to get meaningful bounds for the per-row guarantee required for estimating the heterogeneous treatment effect; roughly speaking, they are only useful for estimating average treatment effect bounds, as also illustrated in a recent line of work. We give a simple, computationally efficient estimator that, without knowledge of the propensities and under standard low-rankness and regularity assumptions, achieves a row-wise $\ell_2$ error of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$. Technically, our analysis establishes the first sharp row-wise $\ell_2$-perturbation bound for low-rank approximation, complementing existing spectral-, Frobenius-, and entrywise perturbation theory.

</details>


### [167] [Prediction-Powered Inference Across Many Tasks for AI Evaluation & Social Science Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29249)
*Nicolas Emmenegger,Ellery Stahler,Chara Podimata*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了多任务预测驱动推断（Multi-task PPI）框架，通过跨任务重校准共享代理-真实标签间的结构信息，在每任务标签稀缺时提升统计推断效率。理论证明线性跨任务重校准与原始代理渐进等价，仅非线性结构能带来效率提升。在2024美国大选的语言模型审计中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPI方法将各任务独立处理，未能利用相关任务间的共享结构信息，这在每任务仅有少量高质量标签的场景下尤为受限。

Method: 提出多任务PPI框架：(1) 跨任务重校准共享代理-真实标签关系中的结构信息；(2) 保留任务内矫正与功率调优；(3) 理论上证明了线性跨任务重校准与原始代理渐进等价，仅非线性结构能带来效率提升。

Result: 理论证明效率提升仅当代理-真实标签关系存在非线性结构时成立；在合成、半合成数据集及2024美国大选语言模型审计实验中，跨任务重校准在标签稀缺时显著缩减置信区间宽度。

Conclusion: 多任务PPI框架能有效利用跨任务共享结构提升标签稀缺下的统计推断效率，但增益来源于非线性结构；线性重校准与原始代理等价。

Abstract: Many applications require statistically valid inference across many related tasks, while using only a handful of high-quality labels per hypothesis. In AI evaluation, these tasks may correspond to model behaviors across prompts, subgroups, or hypotheses; in social science surveys, they may correspond to related questions, populations, or measurement conditions. Prediction-powered inference (PPI) uses abundant but inexpensive proxy measurements to improve inference from limited, ground-truth labels, but commonly used methods treat tasks independently and therefore fail to exploit shared structure across related tasks. This limitation is especially important in settings where only a small number of labels are available per task. To address this issue, we introduce a multi-task prediction-powered inference framework that uses labeled data from related tasks to improve power while preserving task-specific inference. Our methods exploit the shared structure in the proxy-ground-truth relationship through cross-task recalibration, while retaining within-task rectification and power tuning to construct accurate point estimates and confidence intervals. We prove that efficiency gains beyond power-tuned PPI are only possible when the proxy-ground-truth relationship contains nonlinear structure; affine cross-task recalibrations are asymptotically equivalent to using the original proxy. We complement our theoretical findings with experiments on synthetic and semi-synthetic datasets, as well as a case study auditing language models on election-related information during the 2024 U.S. presidential election. Using a large human-annotation study, we show that cross-task recalibration can substantially reduce confidence interval widths when labels are scarce.

</details>


### [168] [Matching Rates and Optimal Allocation for Federated Probe-Logit Distillation under Heterogeneous Bandwidth Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29642)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦语言建模中基于探针集蒸馏的通信效率问题，证明了标量量化logit蒸馏的单轮通信下界 Ω(K⁻¹·2^{-2B/V}) 与上界匹配，并扩展至多轮顺序压缩（速率 O(K⁻¹·2^{-2TB/V})）及异构带宽场景，提出了对数倾斜注水最优分配规则及其自适应变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦探针-logit蒸馏（FPLD）方法虽给出了KL散度上界，但其带宽项 O(K⁻¹·2^{-2B/V}) 是否紧致（下界）未知，且异构带宽场景下的最优分配策略未解。

Method: (1) 利用抖动量化构造匹配的单轮下界 Ω(K⁻¹·2^{-2B/V})；(2) 提出嵌套/缩放残差量化实现T轮顺序压缩，速率 O(K⁻¹·2^{-2TB/V})；(3) 导出异构带宽下的闭合最优分配 B_i^* = B_tot/K + (V/2) log₂(w_i/̄w_g)（对数倾斜注水规则），并设计基于预热阶段的自适应变体。

Result: (1) 单轮带宽项紧致性证明（Θ(K⁻¹·2^{-2B/V})）；(2) T轮压缩的指数级加速；(3) 异构带宽最优分配公式；(4) 自适应变体相对次优性 1+O(√(log(K/δ)/(mT₀)))；(5) n-gram仿真验证理论界和分配策略有效性。

Conclusion: 证明了FPLD带宽项的最优性并推广到多轮和异构场景，为联邦蒸馏的通信效率提供了完整的信息论下界和分配策略。

Abstract: In federated language modeling, $K$ nodes each hold $n$ samples but cannot pool data or exchange full-precision gradients or weights. We study the minimax rate at which a conditional distribution over $V$ tokens can be estimated when each node may upload at most $B$ bits per query in a public probe set. In federated probe-logit distillation (FPLD), each node transmits a scalar-quantized logit vector on the probe set, and an aggregator distills a global parametric student. Prior work (Dubey and Huo, 2026) establishes a high-probability KL rate $O(d/(Kn) + ρ\sqrt{V \log V / m} + K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ plus optimization slack, with the bandwidth term in its trace-sharpened form. Whether this bandwidth-term rate is tight, and how the upper bound generalizes to heterogeneous per-node bandwidths, are left open.
  We close both gaps. First, the dithered FPLD construction has a matching single-round lower bound $Ω(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ under non-degeneracy, pinning the bandwidth-axis rate at $Θ(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$. $T$-round sequential refinement with nested/scaled residual quantizers achieves $O(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2TB/V})$; vanilla FPLD's $T$-independent bandwidth term is suboptimal for every $T > 1$. Second, we establish a heterogeneous-bandwidth upper bound for per-node budgets $B_i$, paired with a closed-form optimal allocation $B_i^* = B_{\mathrm{tot}}/K + (V/2) \log_2(w_i / \bar{w}_g)$, a log-tilted water-filling rule that is the per-node analogue of reverse water-filling for distortion-rate optimization. A plug-in adaptive variant estimates the weights from a short warm-up phase and attains $1 + O(\sqrt{\log(K/δ)/(m T_0)})$ relative suboptimality. Synthetic n-gram simulations confirm that empirical KL is bracketed by the upper and lower bounds and that the optimal allocation strictly dominates uniform and inverse-weighted baselines under heterogeneous clipping.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法尽管修改了不同的权重，但都依赖于一个共同的功能子空间，即通过抑制而非覆盖知识来实现编辑；该机制通过消除后层过度注意力起作用，且是编辑成功的必要条件，解释了编辑无法传播到相关事实的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑方法（如ROME、MEMIT）主要通过修改MLP权重来更新Transformer模型中的事实关联，但现有评估仅关注输出行为，对其内部机制理解不足。本文旨在探究不同编辑是否共享统一的底层机制。

Method: 在编辑后的权重上训练一个紧凑的二值掩码，用于逆转编辑效果；通过掩码识别编辑共享的关键权重子集，并分析其功能作用。

Result: 掩码在训练集上逆转80%的编辑，测试集上超过70%；注入掩码导致编辑成功率从98%降至38%；掩码通过消除后层过度注意力来逆转编辑。

Conclusion: ROME和MEMIT共享一个共同的功能子空间，编辑通过抑制而非覆盖知识的方式实现；这解释了编辑无法传播到相关事实的原因，并为检测和防御恶意编辑提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [170] [Representation Signatures and Risk-Feedback Alignment in LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28850)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过TradeArena测试平台，研究了LLM智能体在金融决策环境中的行为对齐与表征动态，发现了预失败信号（规划嵌入漂移、流形有效秩收缩等），并通过压力测试验证了理性层和无理性层条件下的不同表现，最后揭示了51只股票日内交易中的相关性盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在金融决策中是否会出现对齐失败、漂移或崩溃，以及能否通过可审计的风险反馈和表征轨迹来检测这些失败信号。

Method: 构建TradeArena交易智能体测试平台，包含风险报告、执行模拟、记忆和可回放轨迹；使用80个滚动失败锚点、多种嵌入方法（哈希、LSA、Transformer、白盒隐藏状态探针）；进行压力测试（无CoT权重、词汇控制、OHLCV噪声、虚假审计报告）；开展51只股票日内交易实验。

Result: 发现规划嵌入漂移、融合表示分离正常与预回撤状态、有效秩收缩等预失败信号；理性层收缩在无理性时消失，但意图空间收缩可能保留；结构化风险反馈可作为外部对齐信号，但非通用性能提升器；发现了相关性盲点（LLM合理化集中持仓耦合资产）。

Conclusion: 可审计的风险反馈和表征轨迹能够揭示LLM金融推理何时在对齐、漂移或失败，研究主张的是可检测性而非盈利性。

Abstract: We study behavioral alignment and representation dynamics of large language model (LLM) agents in financial decision environments. Using TradeArena, an auditable trading-agent testbed with risk reports, execution simulation, memory, and replayable trajectories, we analyze how rationales, positions, and interventions evolve under market stress. We find measurable pre-failure signatures: planning embeddings drift from normal-state centroids, fused plan-risk representations separate normal from pre-drawdown states, and manifold diagnostics show effective-rank contraction before failures. To address small-sample and embedding-choice concerns, we use 80 rolling failure anchors across eight LLM trajectories and show that contraction persists across hash, LSA, Transformer, and white-box hidden-state probes. Stress tests with CoT-free target weights, lexical controls, OHLCV noise, and false-audit reports indicate that rationale-level contraction can vanish without rationales, while intent-space contraction may remain; lexical diversity does not collapse; and fused signatures remain informative under noise. We also find that structured risk feedback can act as an external alignment signal without fine-tuning, but not as a universal performance enhancer: true audit feedback improves calibration for some models, return and drawdown for others, and reveals cases where hidden or placebo feedback has higher short-horizon return but weaker alignment diagnostics. Finally, a 51-stock intraday experiment reveals a correlation blind spot: LLM rationales often justify concentrated exposure to coupled assets that the risk layer repeatedly clips, with a rolling Markowitz baseline as a covariance reference. These results support a research claim rather than a profitability claim: auditable risk feedback and representation trajectories reveal when LLM financial reasoning is aligning, drifting, or failing.

</details>


### [171] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出差异电路脆弱性指标，从机制层面比较RL和SFT对LLM内部计算电路的破坏程度，发现SFT适应任务更快但导致更严重的电路破坏和灾难性遗忘，而RL更好保留原始电路但任务适应较慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有行为层面研究表明RL比SFT更能缓解灾难性遗忘，但其背后的机制原因尚不明确。本文旨在从神经机制层面探究RL与SFT对LLM内部计算电路的不同影响，以解释RL在保持先前能力方面的优势。

Method: 提出差异电路脆弱性（Differential Circuit Vulnerability），一种以注意力头为单位的度量指标，用于衡量微调过程中电路退化的程度。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型上分别用RL和SFT微调科学问答任务，比较两种方法对电路完整性的影响。

Result: SFT对目标任务适应更快，但导致更严重的电路破坏和先前能力的灾难性遗忘；RL保留更大比例的原始电路，但任务适应速度较慢。两者之间存在明确的机制层面权衡。

Conclusion: 电路保留程度可能是解释RL相比SFT更不易灾难性遗忘的关键机制因素。代码已开源。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [172] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种轨迹感知的大语言模型推理代理，将分子先导优化中的工具选择建模为顺序决策问题，通过前瞻性策略提升ADMET性质优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子先导优化方法多采用一步式分子优化，忽略了序列设计决策的长期影响，无法有效处理需要多步调整的复杂ADMET优化任务。

Method: TRACE将分子优化工具的选择形式化为动作轨迹上的顺序决策问题，利用大语言模型的推理能力，在保持关键分子子结构约束下做出前瞻性的工具选择决策。

Result: 在多项ADMET优化任务上，TRACE相比基线模型实现了更高的优化成功率、更大的性质改善幅度和更高的分子有效性，同时保持了分子相似性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的顺序决策策略能有效提升分子先导优化的性能，为AI驱动的药物发现提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [173] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在四人不完美信息卡牌游戏Big 2中，系统比较了策略梯度与值函数逼近方法。PPO在随机、贪心和启发式对手下均优于MC Q近似、SARSA和Q学习。适度的熵正则化和当前策略自对弈能显著提升PPO性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不完美信息多人游戏（Big 2）作为深度强化学习受控实验平台的可行性，系统评估不同RL算法在隐藏信息、稀疏奖励和非平稳对手环境中的表现。

Method: 构建自对弈RL框架，在统一环境、输入表示、训练预算和评估协议下，比较PPO、MC Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning在Big 2中的表现，并研究熵正则化和不同自对弈策略的影响。

Result: PPO在所有对手类型上均优于其他算法；适度熵正则化防止策略过度确定化；当前策略自对弈比检查点自对弈或固定对手训练在有限预算下效果更好。

Conclusion: Big 2可作为研究不完美信息、多人交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集深度强化学习的有效受控实验环境。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [174] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于VAE的世界模型在无语言监督的随机具身探索中，其潜在空间会自发发展出反映物理几何结构的空间语义组织，且预测性能与语义对齐共同发展，验证了物理世界几何是表征的组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究世界模型在没有任何语言监督的情况下，仅通过物理探索能学到什么，以及其潜在表征的组织原则是什么。

Method: 训练基于VAE的世界模型进行随机具身探索，分析潜在空间的空间语义结构；通过20个时间检查点分析预测性能与语义对齐的共变关系；采用双敲除实验（调整KL正则化beta参数）验证共享驱动假设。

Result: 方向准确率0.677±0.029（对比随机编码器的0.547），位置RSA 0.192±0.047（对比随机编码器的0.029，提升6.6倍）；预测性能与语义对齐共改进（Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004）；强KL正则化（beta=0.1）导致两者同时崩溃，降低beta至0.001可恢复。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则，为设计具有语义基础的具身智能体提供了直接启示。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [175] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COM策略，通过在初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束来保留时间序列token的连续性和有序性，从而提升基于token的时间序列大语言模型（TS-LLMs）的性能，实验结果表明该方法具有竞争力和强泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token的时间序列大语言模型（TS-LLMs）忽略了时间序列token固有的连续性和有序性，这严重限制了模型性能。本文旨在通过保留这些属性来提升TS-LLMs的有效性。

Method: 提出COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter）策略，在token嵌入的初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束，以保持时间序列token的连续性和有序性。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准上，COM一致性地提升了基于token的TS-LLMs的性能，取得了具有竞争力的结果和强泛化性。

Conclusion: 保留时间序列token的连续性和有序性对基于token的TS-LLMs的有效性至关重要，COM策略是一种有效且通用的改进方法。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [176] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismFlow提出了一种基于Koopman动力学专家的流匹配方法，通过置信感知的赢家通吃机制实现模态特定专家学习，有效解决了标准流匹配中全局向量场估计器在多模态多尺度时间序列数据上的谱收缩问题，在多个基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列信号通常呈现多模态模式和多尺度动力学（包括振荡和高频变化），而标准Flow Matching使用单一有限容量的全局向量场估计器，在多模态时间分布中，不同模态经过相近流状态时需要不相容的条件速度，导致估计器学习到过度平滑的局部传输场近似，造成谱失真和模态覆盖不足。

Method: 提出PrismFlow方法：(1) 引入Koopman启发的动力学专家，每个专家在潜空间中学习残差修正，将局部非线性时间演化近似为线性变换；(2) 提出置信感知的赢家通吃(WTA)目标，只更新与每个样本最对齐的专家，其余专家梯度被掩码，鼓励模态特定专业化；(3) 采样时，所选专家向全局传输场添加残差动力学修正，在保持FM稳定性的同时恢复细粒度高频时间结构。

Result: 在多个基准上，PrismFlow有效缓解了标准FM中的谱收缩问题，Context-FID提升15.6%，Discriminative Score提升38.6%，同时在低数据场景下保持鲁棒性，并有效适用于预测和插补任务。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过引入多专家架构和WTA机制，在不牺牲FM稳定性的前提下，显著提升了多模态多尺度时间序列生成的质量，特别是高频细节和模态覆盖能力。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [177] [TaxDistill: Improving Metagenomic Taxonomic Annotation via Distilled Genomic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28868)
*Rongye Ye,Lun Li,Zheng Luo,Yiran Zhan,Shuhui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TaxDistill知识蒸馏框架，利用500M参数的基因组基础模型GenomeOcean作为教师网络生成软标签，以减轻传统相似性搜索工具带来的标签噪声，从而提升宏基因组分类性能。在CAMI2数据集上，TaxDistill将MMseqs2的F1分数从0.763提升至0.941，优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宏基因组分类方法依赖序列相似性搜索，受限于微生物多样性和参考数据库不完整性；而基于学习的方法虽进行后验校正，但训练时依赖相似性搜索工具生成的标签，不可避免地引入标签噪声，损害表示学习和分类性能。

Method: 提出TaxDistill知识蒸馏框架：以500M参数的GenomeOcean基础模型作为教师网络，提取深层语义特征并基于置信度生成软标签；将软标签知识蒸馏至轻量级学生网络，有效减少初始检索工具引入的标签噪声。

Result: 在七个CAMI2数据集上，TaxDistill在多数场景下优于现有基线。例如在Gastrointestinal数据集上，将MMseqs2的F1分数从0.763提升至0.941，超越Taxometer基线。

Conclusion: TaxDistill为复杂宏基因组分析中标签校正提供了一种可靠方法，通过知识蒸馏有效缓解标签噪声问题。

Abstract: Metagenomic taxonomic annotation aims to identify the microbial origins of DNA fragments in environmental samples. Traditional methods that rely on sequence similarity are often constrained by the high microbial diversity and the incompleteness of reference databases, which has motivated the development of learning approaches such as Taxometer that perform post hoc correction to learn more informative metagenomic sequence representations. However, these methods typically rely on labels derived from similarity search tools during training, which inevitably introduces noise that can impair representation learning and degrade classification performance. To address this issue, we propose TaxDistill, a knowledge distillation framework for metagenomic classification. We introduce GenomeOcean, a 500M parameter genomic foundation model, as the teacher network to extract deep semantic features and generate soft labels based on confidence. By distilling this soft label information into a lightweight student network, TaxDistill effectively reduces the label noise introduced by initial retrieval tools. Comprehensive experiments on seven diverse CAMI2 datasets demonstrate that TaxDistill outperforms existing baselines in most scenarios. For instance, on the Gastrointestinal dataset, it improves the F1 score of MMseqs2 from 0.763 to 0.941, outperforming the Taxometer baseline. Overall, TaxDistill provides a reliable method for label correction in complex metagenomic analysis.

</details>


### [178] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态学习中模态不平衡问题，提出首个从标签侧设计的平衡多模态标签重塑方法（BMLR），通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态的映射难度，从而提升多模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过增强弱模态或调整优化梯度来缓解模态不平衡，但这些策略往往以牺牲强模态的优化能力为代价，且未从模态层面分析学习速度差异的根源。

Method: 提出Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR)，从标签侧设计，通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态在特征空间到共享标签空间之间的映射难度，促进模态交互并向每个模态注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 在多种架构上的大量实验表明，BMLR一致地提升了多模态性能，并与多种模型设计具有良好的兼容性。

Conclusion: 从标签侧设计多模态平衡策略是可行且有效的方向，BMLR通过均衡映射难度而非抑制强模态来实现模态平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [179] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏差和噪声的三元统计框架研究柏拉图表征假说（PRH），发现：(1) 信号层面，线性表征假说（LRH）通过对象-属性的线性编码促进跨模态对齐；(2) 偏差层面，不同模型架构的隐式偏差可通过中心化和归一化部分缓解；(3) 噪声层面，有限样本训练导致的表征噪声与数据稀缺相关（词频与对齐度正相关）。最终提出统计模型精炼LRH并解释跨架构表征对齐现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同AI模型（不同架构、模态、训练方式）之间表征为何趋向对齐的柏拉图表征假说（PRH）背后的统计机制。

Method: 将表征分解为信号（对象-属性的线性编码）、偏差（架构差异）和噪声（有限样本误差）三个分量，利用稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征，并通过中心化/归一化缓解偏差，分析词频与对齐度的相关性验证噪声机制。

Result: (1) 稀疏自编码器提取的线性特征比稠密表征具有更强的跨模态对齐；(2) 中心化和归一化一致提升跨模型对齐；(3) 词频与对齐度呈强且一致的正相关，表明数据稀缺驱动表征噪声。

Conclusion: 提出了一个精炼线性表征假说的统计模型，统一解释了现代AI架构中表征对齐的多项现象。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [180] [Conf-Gen: Conformal Uncertainty Quantification for Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28920)
*Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem,Kevin Zhang,Wei Cui,Marc T. Law,Kin Kwan Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Generation (Conf-Gen)框架，将共形预测扩展到生成式AI任务（如LLM、图像生成器、对话系统、AI智能体），在统一和泛化现有方法的同时，为生成模型提供形式化的不确定性量化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测(CP)和共形风险控制(CRC)在监督学习中已建立完善的不确定性量化框架，但近年AI突破主要由无监督生成模型（如LLM、图像生成器）驱动，这些模型与CP/CRC不直接兼容。现有尝试未能统一处理各类生成任务。

Method: 将CRC框架适配到生成任务，同时放宽其理论假设。Conf-Gen统一并泛化了之前将CP应用于LLM的尝试，并扩展至全新领域。

Result: 展示了Conf-Gen在三个新颖应用中的灵活性：图像生成器产生非记忆化图像的共形保证、对话AI系统提出足够澄清问题的保证、AI智能体输出正确性的保证。

Conclusion: Conf-Gen成功将共形方法论从监督学习扩展到生成式AI，为生成模型提供了可靠的不确定性量化框架，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) and its extension, conformal risk control (CRC), are established frameworks for quantifying uncertainty in supervised machine learning through formal guarantees. However, recent breakthroughs in artificial intelligence (AI) have been driven by unsupervised generative models, such as large language models (LLMs) and image generators, which are not directly compatible with CP or CRC. In this work we introduce conformal generation (Conf-Gen), a general framework adapting CRC to generative tasks while relaxing its theoretical assumptions. Conf-Gen unifies and generalizes previous attempts to apply CP to LLMs, and extends conformal methodology to entirely new domains. We demonstrate the flexibility of Conf-Gen through some novel applications, including obtaining conformal guarantees on: image generators producing non-memorized images, conversational AI systems having asked enough clarifying questions, and the output of AI agents being correct.

</details>


### [181] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将经典配对二项样本量计算方法（Miettinen, 1968）适配到量化基准测试的保守最小可检测效应（MDE）界δ* ≤ (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})√(ρ_d/m)，将“量化声明有多可靠？”转化为基准设计者在运行前可承诺的一行预算。通过四个模型、四个基准（k=5次分割，n=100）和一个并行的MMLU提示模板研究，发现假设ρ_d=0.10时所有NF4-FP16差异均低于MDE，且大部分跨分割标准差在二项参考的±1.5个百分点内，表明n=100子样本上的“基准不可靠性”多为二项采样噪声。提示模板范围（2-10个百分点）达到或超过最大量化差异（3.2个百分点），量化审计若未先固定提示模板则会吸收模板方差。论文补充了一个五行的预注册模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准测试中，如何评估量化声明的可靠性是一个关键问题。现有的基准测试在n=100等小样本上报告方差时，往往将二项采样噪声误判为基准不可靠性，且量化审计与提示模板的方差混叠在一起。作者希望提供一个保守的统计界值，帮助基准设计者明确量化声明的可靠性界限，并区分量化噪声与提示模板噪声。

Method: 将Miettinen (1968)的配对二项样本量计算适配到量化基准场景，推导出保守的MDE界δ* ≤ (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})√(ρ_d/m)，其中m为配对项目数，ρ_d为FP16-NF4不一致率。在四个模型和四个基准上进行了k=5次分割、n=100的试点审计，并做了并行MMLU提示模板研究对比量化噪声与提示噪声的规模。补充了一个五行预注册模板。

Result: 假设ρ_d=0.10（未测量的规划值）时，所有观察到的NF4-FP16差异均低于MDE；大部分跨分割标准差落在二项参考√(p(1-p)/n)的±1.5个百分点内，说明n=100子样本上的“基准不可靠性”主要是二项采样噪声。只有OPT-WinoGrande单元格（|Δ|=3.2 pp）在ρ_d=0.10时低于MDE但在ρ_d=0.05时高于MDE，展示了规划权衡。MMLU提示模板范围（2-10 pp）达到或超过最大量化差异（3.2 pp）。

Conclusion: 配对二项MDE界为量化声明的可靠性提供了一个简洁的统计预算工具，使基准设计者能在运行前预先承诺可靠性水平。量化审计必须首先固定提示模板，否则模板方差会淹没量化噪声。五行预注册模板有助于提高量化审计的透明度和可重复性。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [182] [Theoretical Foundations and Effective Algorithms for Policy-Aware Simulator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29032)
*Christoph Dann,Yishay Mansour,Mehryar Mohri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将世界模型学习目标从预测精度转向策略鲁棒性，通过零和极小化博弈框架，使模拟器学习关注策略重要区域，实现从仿真到真实环境的零差距迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MBRL代理通过最小化预测损失学习世界模型，但RL优化器会利用微小模型误差导致仿真器过度优化，造成策略在仿真中成功但在真实世界失败的现实差距问题。

Method: 将模拟器学习形式化为模型玩家与对抗策略玩家之间的零和极小化博弈；提供在线学习可学习性证明、基于评论家的局部损失界、误差-MDP对偶性三个理论结果；利用对偶性设计可证明收敛的主动数据选择算法。

Result: 在连续控制任务中，策略重要区域的预测误差降低1.5-2.2倍，纯仿真训练的策略达到接近最优的真实世界性能。

Conclusion: 模拟器学习应追求策略鲁棒性而非预测精度，所提博弈框架能有效弥合仿真与真实世界之间的现实差距。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) agents typically learn world models by minimizing predictive loss. However, powerful RL optimizers inevitably exploit minor model inaccuracies, leading to simulator exploitation and a reality gap where policies succeed in simulation but fail in the real world. We propose that the objective for learning simulators should be strategic robustness rather than predictive accuracy, and formulate this as a zero-sum minimax game between a model player and an adversarial policy player. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis: (1) an online learning guarantee showing the game is learnable with sublinear regret bounds; (2) a tractable critic-based simplification bounding the global policy-value gap by the local critic's loss; and (3) an Error-MDP duality, proving that finding the worst-case policy is formally dual to a standard RL problem where the reward is the one-step critic error. This duality yields a provably convergent active data selection algorithm. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate that our approach reduces prediction error in strategically important regions by $1.5$-$2.2\times$ and enables policies trained purely in simulation to match near-optimal real-world performance.

</details>


### [183] [Towards Continuous-time Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28880)
*Dennis Thumm,Ruben Wiedemann,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连续时间因果先验数据拟合网络的轨迹律不变性准则，构建三层分类法（离散/粗网格/细网格），通过随机DAG上的OU或小MLP非线性漂移实现顶层，实验证明细网格积分在8/8项指标上显著优于粗网格（符号一致性p<1/256），并发布先验及药代动力学/物理系统数据的零样本协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将离散时间因果先验数据拟合网络扩展到连续时间时，若SDE仅每观测间隔积分一次，轨迹律依赖于观测时刻，先验仍是离散时间马尔可夫模型。需要明确定义连续时间因果模型的连续性准则，使模型对观测时间表具有不变性。

Method: 提出轨迹律对观测时间表不变的精确连续性准则；构建三层分类法（离散、粗观测网格积分、细网格积分解耦观测）；在随机DAG上使用OU或小MLP非线性漂移实现顶层，支持不规则观测表和硬/软/时变干预；进行2×2编码器×积分器消融实验。

Result: 细网格积分在8/8项指标上优于粗网格（符号一致性p<1/256），且随评估网格细化差距扩大；编码器维度在细积分下无效，在粗积分下时间感知编码器占优。

Conclusion: 细网格积分是实现轨迹律对观测时间表不变的有效方法，在连续时间因果建模中优于粗网格方法，发布的先验和零样本协议为后续研究提供基准。

Abstract: Extending discrete-time causal Prior-data Fitted Networks for time series to continuous time invites writing the mechanism as a stochastic differential equation (SDE) -- but if the SDE is integrated \emph{once per observation gap}, the trajectory law depends on when it is observed, and the prior remains a discrete-time Markov model in SDE clothing.
  We propose a precise continuity criterion -- trajectory-law invariance to the observation schedule -- together with a three-tier taxonomy (discrete; naive observation-grid integration; fine-grid integration with decoupled observation) and a construction realising the top tier on a random DAG with OU or small-MLP nonlinear drifts, irregular observation schedules, and hard / soft / time-varying interventions.
  A $2 \times 2$ encoder $\times$ integrator ablation, run independently on a linear and a nonlinear prior, finds fine-grid integration beats naive on 8/8 cells (sign-consistency $p < 1/256$) with the gap growing as the eval grid refines; the encoder axis is null with fine integration but time-aware-leading with naive.
  We release the prior and a preliminary zero-shot protocol on pharmacokinetic and physical-system data.

</details>


### [184] [Optimal Gap-Dependent Regret for Private Stochastic Decision-Theoretic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29148)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对完整信息下的事件级纯差分隐私随机决策在线学习问题，提出了一个无时间视界依赖的算法，并证明了与已知下界匹配的最优遗憾界 $O(\log K/\Delta_{\min} + \log K/\varepsilon)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hu和Mehta在COLT提出的公开问题要求确定在纯事件级差分隐私下，随机决策在线学习的最优间隙相关遗憾率。

Method: 算法将时间划分为指数增长的数据块，每个块内执行单一动作；通过指数机制基于前一个块的数据无关随机前缀选择下一个动作；利用随机前缀将块遗憾转化为所有前缀长度上softmax选择误差的和；通过单个熵势函数控制所有隐私主导的大间隙动作。

Result: 对于任意时间视界T，算法实现了显式遗憾界 $\operatorname{Reg}_T \le 1000 \cdot (\frac{\log K}{\Delta_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon})$，与已知下界 $\Omega(\frac{\log K}{\min\{\Delta_{\min},\varepsilon\}})$ 匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了Hu和Mehta提出的COLT公开问题，确定了纯事件级差分隐私下随机决策在线学习的最优间隙相关遗憾率。

Abstract: We study stochastic decision-theoretic online learning with full information and event-level pure differential privacy. A COLT open problem of Hu and Mehta asks to determine the optimal gap-dependent regret rate for stochastic decision-theoretic online learning under pure event-level differential privacy. For $K$ actions, losses in $[0,1]$, and a unique best action separated from the second-best action by gap $Δ_{\min}$, the known lower bound is of order $
  \frac{\log K}{\min\{Δ_{\min},\varepsilon\}}, $
or equivalently, up to universal constants, of order \[
  \frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}. \] We give a horizon-free pure-DP algorithm and prove the explicit regret bound \[
  \operatorname{Reg}_T
  \le
  1000 \cdot \left(\frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}\right) \] for every horizon $T$. The numerical constant is not optimized. The algorithm partitions time into blocks of exponentially increasing size, plays a single action throughout each block, and chooses the next action by an exponential mechanism applied to a data-independent random prefix of the previous block. The random prefix converts block regret into a sum, over all prefix lengths, of softmax selection errors. A single entropy-potential argument controls all privacy-dominated large-gap actions at cost $\log K/\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [185] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文蒸馏形式化为潜在记忆管理问题，通过将每个上下文蒸馏为独立的LoRA适配器构建模块化记忆库，引入Self-Gating机制决定是否激活潜在记忆，并采用缓存共享提升推理效率。实验表明该方法在检索场景下显著优于基线，Self-Gating通过停用不必要记忆提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏方法忽略了多个蒸馏后的潜在记忆应如何存储、检索以及在非理想环境下安全激活的问题。

Method: 1) 将每个上下文蒸馏为独立的LoRA适配器，形成模块化记忆库；2) 查询时检索候选记忆并将查询路由到最合适的适配器；3) 使用Self-Gating机制决定是否激活潜在记忆；4) 引入缓存共享降低推理时的管理开销。

Result: 该方法在检索场景下显著优于基线方法；Self-Gating机制通过停用不必要的潜在记忆提升了模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将上下文蒸馏形式化为潜在记忆管理问题，通过模块化记忆库、显式记忆选择与Self-Gating机制，有效解决了多个蒸馏记忆的存储、检索和安全激活问题。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection.
