Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [186] [Do Deep Networks Forget Initialization? A Forgetting-Time View of Practical Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29152)
*Mohua Das,Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Shibshankar Dey,Gareth H. McKinkey,Tomaso Poggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机初始化对训练后网络预测的影响程度，提出了"初始化记忆"这一可量化指标，发现低学习率SGD能记住初始化（测试准确率相差26.5个百分点），而Adam族方法和大学习率SGD能擦除初始化记忆，表明训练后的归纳偏差是架构先验经训练动态过滤后的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机初始化会诱导函数空间中的先验，但实际使用的预测器是训练后产生的。研究者希望量化训练流程在多大程度上保留了初始先验，即初始化记忆问题。

Method: 定义初始化记忆为验证集选定的预测器对随机初始化尺度的依赖程度。在CIFAR-10上对ResNet进行控制实验，比较不同学习率、优化器（SGD vs Adam）、批大小、显式L2正则化等条件下的初始化记忆程度。

Result: 低学习率SGD（批大小128）的测试准确率在初始化尺度变化下相差26.5个百分点，训练5000轮后差距仍基本不变。Adam族方法基本消除这种依赖。大学习率SGD配合显式L2范数控制也能擦除初始化记忆。

Conclusion: 训练后网络的归纳偏差不是单纯的架构先验，而是架构先验经训练流水线遗忘动态过滤后的结果；并且那些能提升泛化性能的正则化方法恰恰是那些能擦除初始化记忆的方法。

Abstract: Randomly initialized neural networks induce a prior over functions, but the predictor used in practice is produced only after training. We ask how much of this initial bias survives the training pipeline. To make the question measurable, we introduce initialization memory: the dependence of the validation-selected predictor on the scale of the random initialization. We perform controlled CIFAR-10 experiments on ResNets where initialization memory already sharply separates training regimes. Low-learning-rate SGD can interpolate while still remembering its initialization: on ResNet-9 with batch size $b=128$, test accuracy varies by $26.5$ percentage points across initialization scales despite $\ge99.5\%$ training accuracy. This is not undertraining: extending the same low-learning-rate regime to $5{,}000$ epochs leaves the spread essentially unchanged. In contrast, Adam-family methods largely erase the dependence. SGD can also be made to forget when larger learning rates are paired with explicit $L_2$ norm control. We interpret these findings in terms of the time scale of forgetting: gradient-flow-like dynamics can preserve initialization memory, whereas stochastic finite-step effects, explicit norm decay, and adaptive preconditioning erase it on scales governed by the size of explicit or implicit regularization. The practical inductive bias of a trained network is therefore not the architectural prior alone, but the architectural prior after being filtered by the forgetting dynamics of the training pipeline; and the same regularizers that improve generalization are precisely those that erase memory of initialization.

</details>


### [187] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）研究LoRA微调对语言模型内部表征几何结构的影响，发现LoRA诱导的特征与预训练SAE字典之间存在较弱的几何对齐，表明LoRA更新在残差流中占据部分独特的表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调已被广泛使用，但其内部表征变化仍未被充分理解，本文旨在探究LoRA诱导的表征几何结构。

Method: 提出delta激活框架隔离适配器贡献，在Gemma-2-9B上训练不同秩（4/8/16/32）的适配器专用SAEs，使用余弦相似度、主角度分析和CKA评估表征对齐。

Result: LoRA特征字典与预训练SAE特征几何对齐较弱；适配器专用SAEs重建delta激活效果更好；特征密度随秩和深度增加，几何分歧保持稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调可诱导预训练可解释性字典无法完全捕捉的特征结构，对机械可解释性、微调分析和安全审计有重要意义。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [188] [Causal Label Recovery in Payment Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29272)
*Gaurav Dhama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对支付网络欺诈检测中标签的系统性偏差问题，提出序列三重稳健（STR）估计器，该估计器同时纠正授权、发行方报告、延迟和标签腐败四种缺陷，达到半参数效率界，并推导出最优训练延迟，实现使用几天而非几个月的数据进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈检测模型依赖的拒付标签存在系统性偏差：每个标签必须经历授权、发行方报告和延迟三道门控，且可能被第一方滥用或发行方误分类污染。这些缺陷对检测性能施加了极小极大下界，本文旨在探明该下界是否可达。

Method: 将观测管道形式化为具有三个倾向阶段和腐败层的序贯缺失数据问题；构建序列三重稳健（STR）估计器，在每个门控处仅需倾向模型或结果回归之一正确即可获得一致性；采用噪声率调整的伪标签进行腐败校正、经验贝叶斯收缩稳定小发行商的逆倾向权重、插件方差估计器提供有效置信区间、以及伯恩斯坦浓度不等式提供有限样本保证。

Result: STR估计器同时纠正四种缺陷并达到半参数效率界（渐近方差下界）；推导出最优训练延迟（成熟窗口），最小化标签质量损失与模型过时之和；STR允许使用数天而非数月前的数据进行训练，解耦模型新鲜度与拒付成熟周期；STR在任意样本量下均优于朴素拒付训练。

Conclusion: 序列三重稳健估计器理论上达到了欺诈检测性能的不可约下界，实践上大幅缩短了训练数据延迟，实现了模型时效性与标签质量的解耦。

Abstract: Fraud detection models in payment networks train on chargeback labels that are systematically biased. Every label must survive three sequential gates: authorization (declined transactions generate no labels), issuer reporting (unreported fraud is invisible), and delay (pending chargebacks are missing at training time). Labels that do arrive may be corrupted by first-party misuse or issuer misclassification. A companion paper [arXiv:2605.27557] proved that these four impairments impose a minimax lower bound on detection performance. This paper asks: can that bound be achieved?
  We formalize the observation pipeline as a sequential missing-data problem with three propensity stages and a corruption layer, and construct the Sequential Triply Robust (STR) estimator. The STR corrects for all four impairments simultaneously and achieves the semiparametric efficiency bound -- no estimator can have lower asymptotic variance. It is sequentially triply robust: at each gate, consistency requires only that either the propensity model or the outcome regression is correctly specified, not both. We provide corruption correction via noise-rate-adjusted pseudo-labels, empirical Bayes shrinkage to stabilize inverse-propensity weights for small issuers, a plug-in variance estimator yielding valid confidence intervals, and a Bernstein concentration inequality for finite-sample guarantees.
  On the operational side, we derive the optimal training delay -- the maturity window that minimizes the sum of label-quality loss and model staleness -- and prove that the STR permits training on data that is days old rather than months old, decoupling model freshness from the chargeback maturity cycle. The STR provably dominates naive chargeback-based training in mean squared error for any sample size.

</details>


### [189] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Guidance框架，利用生成过程的几何特征，通过条件期望算子的奇异函数学习控制信号的低维表示，实现无需重训练或反向传播的稳定、高保真扩散模型控制，在CIFAR-10上条件准确率提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍，并揭示了有效引导的最优时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在噪声逐渐破坏数据的过程中，仅有少数特征对控制信息保持有效，现有方法需要重训练或反向传播，效率低下。作者旨在利用生成过程的固有几何结构，学习一个低维控制基，实现高效、稳定的引导。

Method: 将控制信号的特征定义为条件期望算子的奇异函数，通过自监督目标学习该基函数；学习到的基可将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码等）直接投影到采样轨迹上，无需重训练或去噪器反向传播。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，条件准确率比最强无训练基线提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍；同一表示空间同时支持标签、CLIP和空间掩码引导，无需辅助模型。

Conclusion: 该方法揭示了生成过程中的相变现象，准确定位了有效引导的最优时间窗口，为扩散模型的高效可控生成提供了理论基础和实用框架。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [190] [Attention as In-Context Empirical Bayes: A Two-Stage View via Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29351)
*Matthew Smart,Soumya Ganguly,Nilava Metya,Alexandre V. Morozov,Anirvan M. Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了最小注意力仅变换器（attention-only transformer）在全标记破坏（all-token corruption）下可解释为两阶段经验贝叶斯推理：单个注意力层计算基于上下文经验分布的核加权后验均值；深度通过粒子动力学精炼分布（阶段1），长程跳跃连接携带噪声输入作为后验推理的查询（阶段2），揭示了深度与注意力残差的不同统计角色。研究显示，在无需显式噪声调度的情况下，固定核带宽与有限积分范围即可产生有效去噪，并建立了深度-噪声关系。对于良好先验类，经验估计器在渐近条件下收敛于贝叶斯最优预测器。该框架将注意力机制解释为基于样本的后验估计，而非显式密度建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从统计推理角度理解仅注意力变换器在标记破坏任务中的工作机制，揭示注意力层、深度与跳跃连接的不同统计角色，并为注意力机制提供贝叶斯解释框架。

Method: 采用两阶段经验贝叶斯框架分析最小注意力仅变换器：阶段1通过深度（多层注意力）实现粒子动力学对经验分布的精炼；阶段2利用长程跳跃连接携带噪声输入作为后验推理查询。理论分析结合核密度估计、后验均值恢复保证与逆向扩散极限的关联。

Result: 揭示了深度与注意力残差的统计角色分离；证明固定核带宽与有限积分范围即可实现有效去噪，无需显式噪声调度；建立了深度-噪声关系；给出了后验均值恢复的保证，证明经验估计器在渐近条件下收敛于贝叶斯最优预测器。

Conclusion: 注意力机制可被统计解释为基于样本的后验估计的上下文推理过程，无需显式密度建模；该框架为理解深度变换器的统计行为提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study minimal attention-only transformers under all-token corruption and show they admit a two-stage empirical Bayes interpretation. A single attention step computes a kernel-weighted posterior mean with respect to the empirical distribution defined by the context. Depth refines this distribution through particle dynamics (Stage 1), while a long-range skip-connection carries the noisy input as a query for posterior inference (Stage 2), revealing distinct statistical roles for depth and attention residuals. The framework isolates a minimal setting in which the context itself induces a depth-dependent energy landscape governing in-context inference. We show that effective denoising can emerge without an explicit noise schedule: a fixed kernel bandwidth and finite integration horizon suffice, yielding a principled depth-noise relationship. We further establish a posterior-mean recovery guarantee for a class of well-behaved priors, where the empirical estimator converges to the Bayes-optimal predictor under asymptotic conditions. Connecting these dynamics to reverse-diffusion limits, our results provide a statistical interpretation of attention as in-context inference via sample-based posterior estimation, without explicit density modeling.

</details>


### [191] [A Theoretical and Experimental Study of a Novel Adaptive Learning Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29273)
*Sakshi Kumari,Shyam Kumar M,Sushmitha P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Adam和AMSGrad等自适应优化方法，并基于视线方法提出了一种新的优化器C-Adam，提供了收敛性理论证明并通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应学习率优化器（如Adam）在实际应用中不保证收敛，AMSGrad虽解决了部分问题但仍存在局限性，因此需要设计一种既能减少计算开销和振荡、又能保证收敛的新型优化器。

Method: 基于视线方法（line of sight approach）提出C-Adam优化器，并给出收敛性的理论证明，通过多个真实数值实验进行验证。

Result: C-Adam在理论上证明了收敛性，并在基于真实场景的数值实验中表现优于Adam和AMSGrad等基线方法。

Conclusion: C-Adam作为一种新型自适应优化器，在保证收敛性的同时有效降低了计算开销和振荡，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: A crucial component of machine learning algorithms is minimizing loss functions with less computational cost and less oscillations. While adaptive learning rate-based optimizers have been widely used for real-world tasks, they do not guarantee convergence, which is why AMSGrad was later introduced to investigate the non-convergence behaviour of Adam. In this paper, popular adaptive optimization methods like Adam and AMSGrad are critically reviewed with an emphasis on their fundamental design concepts. To address limitations of the above mentioned optimizers, a new optimizer variant, C-Adam, is proposed based on the line of sight approach. A theoretical proof for convergence is also provided and the optimizer is validated through a number of real-life based numerical experiments.

</details>


### [192] [Sequential Physics-Constrained Neural Operator Forward Modeling for the $\textit{Norne}$ Reservoir System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28909)
*Clement Etienam,Juntao Yang,Oleg Ovcharenko,Nick Luiken,Tsubasa Onishi,Nefeli Moridis,Issam Said*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了面向三相黑油油藏动态序贯代理建模的神经算子数学与计算框架，重点研究傅里叶神经算子(FNO)及其物理信息变体(PINO)。针对Norne基准油藏（46×112×22网格，113,344个单元，3298天生产历史），围绕四个相互关联的理论问题展开：函数空间适定性、协变量偏移量化、物理约束谱稳定性及K步TBPTT梯度分析。实验验证表明，自回归PINO代理在3298天预测中保持R²>0.99（油）、R²>0.90（气）、R²≈0.80（压力）和水相单调递增R²，并在单GPU上实现约10^4倍于传统有限体积模拟器的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 油藏数值模拟中，高保真有限体积求解器计算成本极高，难以支撑大规模不确定性量化与历史匹配所需的数千次模拟运行。现有代理建模方法在长时间序列自回归预测中面临误差累积、外推能力不足及理论保证缺失等问题。因此，亟需建立兼具计算效率与长期预测稳定性的神经算子代理框架，并提供严格的理论收敛性保障。

Method: 本文构建了基于FNO/PINO的序贯代理建模框架，理论层面：(1)在乘积Sobolev空间中对隐式时间步映射进行泛函分析，建立适定性和局部Lipschitz常数估计；(2)利用Wasserstein-2距离量化协变量偏移，导出指数级人口风险差异上界；(3)通过物理约束谱稳定性分析，证明PINO训练可降低Jacobian谱半径，实现时间均匀有界rollout误差；(4)推导K步TBPTT梯度几何衰减偏置、最优截断窗口及Adam收敛率。数值层面：在Norne油藏真实数据上训练并验证理论预测。

Result: 自回归PINO代理在3298天全周期预测中：油相R²>0.99，气相R²>0.90，压力R²≈0.80，水相R²单调递增。训练在8块NVIDIA B200 GPU上耗时不到1小时完成。1000成员集成模拟在单块B200 GPU上1分钟内完成，相比OPM有限体积求解器获得约10^4倍墙钟加速比。

Conclusion: 本文从理论到实验系统验证了FNO/PINO框架用于三相黑油油藏动态代理建模的可行性与优越性，提供了完整的泛函分析基础、误差传播保证及梯度收敛性证明。该方法在保持高精度（尤其油相）的同时实现了数量级级别的计算加速，为大尺度油藏不确定性量化提供了高效可靠的代理工具。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive mathematical and computational framework for sequential surrogate modeling of three-phase black-oil reservoir dynamics using neural operators, with particular emphasis on Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) and their physics-informed variant (PINO). The application focus is the Norne benchmark reservoir, defined on a heterogeneous $46\times112\times22$ grid ($N=113,344$ cells), with a production history spanning $T=30$ timesteps covering 3298 days. Our theoretical contributions are organized around four interlocking problems: (1) functional-analytic formulation in a product-Sobolev-space setting, including well-posedness of the implicit timestep map and sharp local Lipschitz estimates; (2) covariate shift quantification, proving that the Wasserstein-2 distance grows as $W_2 \leq \varepsilon(L^n-1)/(L-1)$, with exponential population-risk discrepancy for $L>1$; (3) physics-constrained spectral stability, showing PINO training with $λ_R \geq λ^*_R$ reduces the learned Jacobian spectral radius to $ρ_F + Cλ_R^{-1/2}$, yielding uniform-in-time rollout error $|δ_n| \leq \varepsilon/(1-ρ)$; and (4) $K$-step TBPTT gradient analysis, deriving geometric bias decay $O(ρ^K)$, optimal window $K^ = O(\log(T/σ^2))$, and Adam convergence $O(1/\sqrt{t}) + O(ρ^{K^*})$. Empirical validation confirms all theoretical predictions: autoregressive PINO surrogates sustain $R^2>0.99$ (oil), $R^2>0.90$ (gas), $R^2\approx 0.80$ (pressure), and monotonically improving $R^2$ (water) across the full 3298-day horizon, trained on eight NVIDIA B200 GPUs in under one hour. A 1000-member ensemble runs in under one minute on a single B200 GPU, giving a ${\sim}10^4\times$ wall-clock speedup over the OPM finite-volume simulator.

</details>


### [193] [Singularity-aware Optimization via Randomized Geometric Probing: Towards Stable Non-smooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29547)
*Ruoran Xu,Borong She,Xiaobo Jin,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S-Adam是一种针对非光滑损失景观（如ReLU和量化算子导致）的优化器，通过局部几何不稳定性指标动态调节步长，在理论上证明了以最优速率收敛到Clarke驻点，并在量化感知训练和高噪声小批量学习中取得了优于AdamW和Prox-SGD的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构中广泛存在的非光滑组件（如ReLU激活函数、量化算子）导致损失景观不光滑，传统自适应优化器（如Adam）在此类非光滑区域出现梯度震颤（梯度信号冲突引起的剧烈振荡），导致收敛性能差和泛化能力下降。

Method: 提出Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam)，核心包括：(1) 局部几何不稳定性(LGI)指标，通过随机化方向导数的方差来估计Clarke次微分的直径，作为局部几何不稳定的计算高效估计量；(2) 自适应阻尼机制 exp(-λρ)，在高不稳定区域降低更新步长，在光滑区域保持快速收敛。

Result: 在量化感知训练(QAT)和高噪声小批量学习任务中，S-Adam一致优于AdamW和Prox-SGD：CIFAR-100上准确率提升最高达6%，TinyImageNet上提升达3%，同时有效缓解了梯度振荡问题。理论分析证明几乎必然收敛到(δ,ε)-Clarke驻点，且达到最优O(1/√T)收敛率。

Conclusion: S-Adam通过感知局部几何不稳定性来动态调节优化行为，为非光滑深度学习优化提供了一种理论严谨且实践有效的解决方案，在量化感知训练等实际场景中展现了显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Deep learning optimization relies heavily on the assumption of smooth loss landscapes, a condition systematically violated by modern architectures due to non-smooth components such as ReLU activations and quantization operators. In such non-smooth regimes, adaptive optimizers such as Adam suffer from gradient chattering, violent oscillations caused by conflicting signals within the Clarke subdifferential, leading to poor convergence and suboptimal generalization. To address this, we introduce Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam), a novel optimizer that stabilizes training by dynamically modulating step sizes based on local geometric instability. Our key contribution is the Local Geometric Instability (LGI) metric, a computationally efficient estimator of the Clarke subdifferential diameter derived from the variance of randomized directional derivatives. S-Adam incorporates an adaptive damping mechanism exp(-$λ$$ρ$) that decelerates updates in high-instability regions while preserving fast convergence in smooth basins. We provide a rigorous convergence analysis using differential inclusions, proving that S-Adam converges almost surely to ($δ$,$ε$)-Clarke stationary points at the optimal O(1/$\sqrt(T)$) rate. Empirical evaluations on Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) and high-noise small-batch learning demonstrate that S-Adam consistently outperforms AdamW and Prox-SGD, achieving accuracy gains of up to 6 percent on CIFAR-100 and 3 percent on TinyImageNet while effectively mitigating gradient oscillations.

</details>


### [194] [Cycle-Space Informed Detection of Autoencoded Blind False Data Injection Attacks on Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28912)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对AI驱动数据中心和储能系统对实时测量数据依赖增强的背景下，本文揭示了一种基于自编码器学习的盲虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA），该攻击通过雅可比零空间生成扰动，可规避残差检测和时间序列异常检测器。为抵御此类攻击，提出基于拓扑的循环空间检测器（CSD），利用网络循环空间强加结构约束，并通过最小循环基（MCB）证明其最优泛化误差。该方法无需精确线路参数，在IEEE 14至118节点系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于统计或数据驱动的检测方法在面对攻击者利用相同数据结构构造隐式扰动时失效，亟需一种不依赖精确参数且能抵御零空间利用型数据驱动FDIA的检测方法。

Method: 提出拓扑信息引导的循环空间检测器（CSD），利用电网拓扑的循环空间构造结构约束来增强零空间估计；采用最小循环基（MCB）实现最优泛化误差。

Result: 在IEEE 14、30、57、118节点系统上的仿真表明，所提方法在真实测量噪声下能有效检测数据驱动的FDIA。

Conclusion: 基于拓扑循环约束的方法比单纯依赖数值零空间估计更优，无需精确线路参数，能更好分离正常与攻击测量值，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI-driven data centers and large-scale energy storage systems is increasing the reliance of power system operation on real-time measurement data and automated decision-making. However, many existing detection methods rely on statistical or data-driven analysis of measurements and can fail when attackers exploit the same data structure to craft stealthy perturbations. To illustrate this limitation, we demonstrate a blind False Data Injection Attack (FDIA) in which an Autoencoder learns the measurement manifold and generates perturbations aligned with the Jacobian null space, thereby allowing the attack to evade both residual-based baddata detectors and time-series anomaly detectors. To mitigate data-driven FDIAs which exploit the null space, we propose a topology-informed Cycle-Space Detector (CSD) that leverages the Cycle-Space of the network to impose structural constraints that enhance null space estimation. In addition, we prove that by using the Minimum Cycle Basis (MCB), the proposed CSD achieves the optimal generalization error for attack detection. By exploiting topology-derived cycle constraints rather than relying solely on numerical null space estimation, the proposed method does not require precise line parameters and improves the separation between normal and attacked measurements. Simulation results on IEEE 14-, 30-, 57-, and 118-bus systems demonstrate that the proposed method effectively detects data-driven FDIAs under realistic measurement noise.

</details>


### [195] [On the Optimizer Dependence of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29387)
*Vansh Ramani,Shourya Vir Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现神经标度律中的标度指数α并非固定常数，而是依赖于优化器的选择。在随机特征回归实验中，预条件优化器（如自然梯度）相比梯度下降能产生更陡峭的标度（更大的α），在自然语言特征谱附近（s≈1.0）自然梯度达到α≈0.31而梯度下降仅α≈0.12，差异达2.6倍。该结论是否迁移至大规模LLM训练仍待验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战神经标度律中α为固定常数的传统假设，探究优化器选择对标度指数的影响。

Method: 在随机特征回归这一神经标度的经典理论框架中，控制实验设置，测量五种优化器变体在六种谱条件下的α值。

Result: 预条件优化器系统性地产生更大的α值，α偏移在s=1.5附近达到峰值，在s=2.0仍保持较大值。在s≈1.0时自然梯度的α是梯度下降的2.6倍。

Conclusion: 标度律预测应考虑优化器选择；提供了预测先进优化器何时有效的谱诊断方法；该结果在大规模LLM训练中的迁移性仍为开放问题。

Abstract: The scaling exponent $α$ in neural scaling laws $L(N) \propto N^{-α}$ is commonly treated as a fixed constant set by architecture and data. We present evidence that $α$ depends systematically on the optimizer. In controlled random-feature regression experiments -- the canonical theoretical framework for neural scaling -- we measure $α$ across five optimizer variants and six spectral conditions. Preconditioned optimizers consistently yield steeper scaling (larger $α$), with the $α$-shift increasing across most of the tested spectral range, peaking near $s = 1.5$, and remaining large at $s = 2.0$. At $s \approx 1.0$ (characteristic of natural language), the full natural gradient achieves $α\approx 0.31$ versus $α\approx 0.12$ for gradient descent -- a $2.6\times$ larger fitted exponent that, within the random-feature model, compounds with each model-size doubling. Whether and how this exponent shift transfers to large-scale LLM training -- where recent evidence suggests the advantage may attenuate with scale -- remains an important open question. Our results imply that scaling-law forecasts should account for optimizer choice, and we provide a spectral diagnostic predicting when advanced optimizers will pay off.

</details>


### [196] [Open Problem: Separating Geometric and Algorithmic Compression via Cayley-Table Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29885)
*Dongsung Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出深度学习缺乏对算法复杂度的隐式偏置，提出凯莱表补全作为离散代数版的矩阵补全测试基准，并展示了算子值张量分解结合平坦性先验可产生隐式的离散结合律偏置，最后呼吁建立精确恢复的理论保证并推广到更广泛的离散算法公理发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在连续域表现优异，但无法外推精确的算法规则或离散代数规则，缺乏对算法复杂度最小化的隐式偏置。作者希望建立凯莱表补全作为测试该缺失偏置的规范基准。

Method: 将凯莱表补全问题形式化为离散代数的矩阵补全对应物；采用算子值张量分解结合平坦性先验（flatness prior）来产生对离散结合律的隐式算法偏置。

Result: 证明了算子值张量分解与平坦性先验的组合可以隐式地偏向于精确的离散结合律，类似于矩阵分解加权重衰减隐式地偏向低线性秩。

Conclusion: 提出了凯莱表补全的精确恢复理论界作为开放问题，并呼吁学界将连续平坦性先验推广到无需组合搜索即可自主发现更广泛的离散算法公理。

Abstract: Modern statistical learning theory and deep learning characterize generalization primarily in terms of continuous capacity control (e.g., norm-based regularization, margin maximization, low-rank bias). While highly successful in continuous domains, deep learning consistently fails to extrapolate exact algorithmic or discrete algebraic rules, reflecting a missing inductive bias toward algorithmic complexity minimization. We propose the Cayley-table completion as the canonical testbed for this missing bias, serving as the discrete algebraic counterpart to matrix completion. Just as matrix factorization combined with weight decay yields an implicit geometric bias toward low linear rank, recent results demonstrate that operator-valued tensor factorizations paired with a flatness prior yield an implicit algorithmic bias toward exact discrete associativity. We pose the open problem of establishing formal exact recovery bounds for Cayley-table completion, and challenge the community to generalize continuous flatness priors to autonomously discover broader discrete algorithmic axioms without combinatorial search.

</details>


### [197] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM生成的奖励塑形视为调试而非一次性生成，通过诊断驱动的迭代优化，在MiniGrid的DoorKey-8x8任务上将成功率从2.3%提升至97.6%，在KeyCorridor任务上从31.2%提升至86.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏、结构化强化学习任务中，LLM生成的奖励塑形存在严重的一次性生成失败模式（奖励泛滥、语义/API误解），需要系统性的调试方法而非简单重试。

Method: 提出诊断驱动的迭代精炼框架：（1）识别两种主要故障模式（奖励泛滥、语义/API误解）及一种罕见弱塑形案例；（2）利用训练诊断和故障模式分类法指导奖励函数的有针对性修订；（3）通过消融实验、预算匹配、Best-of-3比较等控制实验验证调试机制的有效性。

Result: DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%。静态词汇表控制恢复大部分差距（87.6%; 70.7%），证明分类法提示是主要机制。连续控制任务（MuJoCo）中基于成功的诊断在密集奖励场景下可能失效。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于PPO下具有可靠接口的稀疏结构化任务；LLM生成奖励塑形应被理解为调试过程而非一次性生成；分类法驱动的调试是主要贡献机制，动态标签提供部分增量证据。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [198] [The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411)
*Shu Wan,Abhinav Gorantla,Huan Liu,K. Selçuk Candan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过3,450个任务的SCM3K基准测试，系统评估了马尔可夫边界在表格预测中的实用性。理论上，马尔可夫边界是最优预测特征集，但实际效果复杂：使用真实边界能显著提升预测性能，尤其在特征空间大且稀疏时；然而，通过因果发现恢复边界的标准流程效果不佳，原因包括发现算法优化结构而非预测、误分类的不对称代价、以及存在多个等效特征集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论上马尔可夫边界是预测最优特征集，但现代回归器仍使用全部特征。作者试图探究马尔可夫边界在表格预测任务中的实际有效性。

Method: 在SCM3K基准（3,450个任务，40-1000个特征，6个SCM家族）上，使用6种回归器，对比完整特征集、真实马尔可夫边界和因果发现恢复的边界进行预测实验。

Result: 真实边界显著提升预测性能（尤其大且稀疏的特征空间）；但因果发现恢复边界的流程效果不佳，无法超越完整特征集；问题源于发现算法优化结构而非预测、误分类的不对称代价、存在多个等效优特征集。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫边界的预测价值受限于当前因果发现方法的局限性，未来需发展预测对齐的特征选择方法和能利用因果结构的表格模型。

Abstract: Under standard graphical assumptions, the Markov boundary of a target variable is the smallest set of features that renders every other feature redundant. Once the boundary is observed, the target is conditionally independent of the rest of the table. This is a tempting object for tabular prediction, since it names exactly the columns a model should need. Yet modern regressors are still trained on the full feature set. We ask whether the Markov boundary is genuinely useful for prediction on SCM3K, a 3,450-task synthetic SCM benchmark with feature counts from 40 to 1000 and six SCM families, evaluated with six regressors. The answer is more nuanced than the theory suggests. Restricting a regressor to the oracle boundary often improves prediction substantially, and the improvement grows as the feature space becomes larger and sparser. But the natural pipeline of recovering the boundary with causal discovery and training on the recovered mask does not deliver. Existing estimators exhaust the compute budget before reaching the regime where the boundary helps most, and even where they run they rarely beat the full feature set. We trace this to three causes. Discovery optimizes structural recovery rather than prediction. False negatives and false positives carry sharply asymmetric predictive cost. The exact boundary is only one of many feature sets that beat all features. We then develop what these facts imply for prediction-aligned feature selection and for tabular models that learn to use causal structure.

</details>


### [199] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CosmicFish-HRM 是一个紧凑型语言模型，通过分层推理模块（HRM）实现自适应推理深度，根据输入复杂度动态分配计算资源，在小型模型上验证了非均匀推理行为的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽具备强推理能力，但通常依赖巨大参数量和昂贵推理成本。本文探索另一种方向：在紧凑模型中通过自适应推理深度来提升推理能力，避免单纯依赖参数规模扩展。

Method: 提出 CosmicFish-HRM 模型，核心是分层推理模块（HRM），在推理时动态执行高低层推理循环，根据输入复杂度自适应决定何时停止。同时结合 Grouped Query Attention、RoPE、SwiGLU 等现代 transformer 组件。

Result: 模型学习到了非均匀推理行为，能够根据不同任务和输入分配不同的推理步数。在小型规模下，额外推理基础设施带来一定开销，但推测随着模型规模增大，HRM 核心的相对成本会降低，这一权衡将变得有利。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度可能成为替代单纯依赖参数规模来提升推理能力的一种有前景的方案。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [200] [On the Construction and Implications of Low-Loss Valleys in LoRA-based Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29580)
*Daniel Dold,Emanuel Sommer,Julius Kobialka,Oliver Dürr,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-Curve，一种在LoRA参数空间中通过分段贝塞尔曲线连接多个独立微调最优解的方法，证明可形成连续低损谷，结合平坦极小扰动和JS散度正则化，在不牺牲性能的前提下提高了预测分布互信息，实现了参数空间连续遍历与功能多样性的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）已成为大语言模型的标准范式，但在此框架下对认知不确定性进行原则性估计仍具挑战。近期研究表明LoRA机制中离散多模态方法（如深度集成）相较于单模态方法并无显著优势，这与深度学习中集成独立最优解通常能提升泛化性能的普遍观察相矛盾。连接这些模式通过连续低损谷可进一步增强贝叶斯模型平均的效果，然而LoRA空间中是否存在这种连续结构、能否产生被局部或离散方法忽略的功能多样性，尚未被研究。

Method: 提出LoRA-Curve方法，在LoRA参数空间中使用分段贝塞尔曲线参数化，包含两种变体：自由配置（联合优化所有控制点）和锚定配置（连接独立微调的LoRA最优解）。从理论上证明了曲线沿路径的连续性和损失的Lipschitz正则性。结合平坦极小扰动和Jensen-Shannon散度正则化器，实现连续参数空间遍历。

Result: 在Qwen2.5 7B模型上对推理和分类基准进行实验，结果表明线性插值会遇到损失屏障，而锚定的多段曲线能够通过连续低损谷连接独立最优解。结合平坦极小扰动和JS散度正则化后，LoRA-Curve在未牺牲性能的前提下，使预测分布的互信息显著提高，并将参数空间的连续遍历与功能多样性关联起来。

Conclusion: LoRA-Curve成功在LoRA参数空间中发现并利用了连续低损谷结构，弥补了离散或局部方法在功能多样性上的不足，为LoRA框架下的不确定性估计和贝叶斯模型平均提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are standard for large language models, principled estimation of epistemic uncertainty remains challenging. Recent results in the LoRA regime suggest that discrete multi-mode approaches such as deep ensembles offer little benefit over single-mode methods. This contradicts broader observations in deep learning, where ensembling independent optima typically improves generalization, and linking these modes through continuous low-loss valleys further enhances Bayesian model averaging (BMA). Whether such structure exists in the LoRA space and whether it yields functional diversity missed by local or discrete methods has not been studied. We introduce LoRA-Curve, a segmented Bézier curve parameterization in the LoRA space, with two variants: a free configuration that jointly optimizes all control points, and an anchored configuration that connects independently fine-tuned LoRA optima. We prove pathwise continuity and Lipschitz regularity of the loss along the curve and empirically show, across reasoning and classification benchmarks with Qwen2.5 7B, that linear interpolation encounters loss barriers, while our anchored multi-segment curves connect independent optima through continuous low-loss valleys. Combined with flat-minima perturbations and a Jensen-Shannon divergence regularizer, LoRA-Curve yields measurably higher mutual information of the predictive distribution without sacrificing performance, and links continuous parameter-space traversal to functional diversity.

</details>


### [201] [The Sample Complexity of Multiclass and Sparse Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29645)
*Liad Erez,Fan Chen,Alon Cohen,Tomer Koren,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机i.i.d.设置下的上下文赌博机问题，聚焦于$s$-稀疏奖励场景（每个上下文的奖励向量$L_1$范数不超过$s \ll |A|$）。主要贡献是设计了两种算法，能以高概率输出一个相对于策略类$\Pi$的$\epsilon$-最优策略，样本复杂度为$\tilde{O} ((s/\epsilon^2 + |A|/\epsilon)\log |\Pi|/\delta)$，并将该界推广至Natarajan类，同时给出了匹配的下界（对数因子内），填补了先前工作中额外的$\Theta(|A|^9)$依赖缺口。两种方法分别为：基于决策-估计系数（DEC）的探索-优化算法，以及基于低方差探索技术的专用算法，后者还可推广至上下文组合半赌博机问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文赌博机工作（Erez et al., 2024, 2025）在处理$s$-稀疏奖励设定时存在过大的$\Theta(|A|^9)$依赖项，本文旨在消除这一额外依赖，获得最优样本复杂度界。

Method: 1) 基于决策-估计系数（DEC）的分析框架，设计探索-优化算法；2) 基于低方差探索技术的专用算法，可推广至上下文组合半赌博机。

Result: 获得$\tilde{O} ((s/\epsilon^2 + |A|/\epsilon)\log |\Pi|/\delta)$的样本复杂度上界，并给出匹配下界，消除了$\Theta(|A|^9)$依赖。

Conclusion: 本文通过两种互补方法解决了$s$-稀疏奖励上下文赌博机中的最优样本复杂度问题，并推广至更广的设定，为相关领域提供了理论最优算法。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits in the stochastic i.i.d.\ setting, where a learner observes contexts drawn from an unknown distribution, selects actions from a finite set $A$, and aims to identify an approximately optimal policy from a given class based on bandit feedback. Motivated by bandit multiclass classification with zero-one rewards, we focus on the \emph{$s$-sparse} setting in which, for every context, the reward vector has $L_1$-norm at most $s \ll |A|$. Our main result is the design of algorithms that, with high probability, output an $ε$-optimal policy compared to policy class $Π$ using $\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$ samples. We extend this bound to general Natarajan classes and complement it with a matching lower bound (up to logarithmic factors), thereby closing a substantial gap left by prior work (Erez et al., 2024, 2025), which incurred an additional $Θ(|A|^9)$ dependence. We obtain these results via two complementary approaches. First, we analyze contextual bandits through the lens of contextual decision making with structured observations, designing an exploration-by-optimization algorithm whose sample complexity is governed by the \emph{decision-estimation coefficient} (DEC; Foster et al., 2021, 2022). We show that, with $s$-sparse rewards, the induced model class admits a sharp DEC bound that scales with $s$ and directly yields the optimal rate. Since this approach is largely information-theoretic and involves solving complex min-max optimization problems, we also develop a second, more specialized algorithmic method based on a low-variance exploration technique. This approach leads to concrete, tractable algorithms and naturally extends to contextual combinatorial semi-bandits, leading to improved sample complexity guarantees for bandit multiclass list classification.

</details>


### [202] [A Training-Time Diagnostic for Generalization via the Log-Alignment Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28975)
*Ali Shehper,Ashish Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对数对齐比(LAR)作为衡量参数-激活对齐的指标，将其重新表述为权重谱与激活谱的重叠度，并证明LAR能追踪记忆与泛化之间的转变、预测有效维度以及跟踪泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以有效区分模型是在记忆数据还是学习泛化特征，亟需一种能从前向传播中直接计算、无需验证数据且计算开销可忽略的指标来追踪这一转变过程。

Method: 将LAR重新定义为权重谱（矩阵归一化平方奇异值的分布）与激活谱（输入沿奇异方向投影的归一化平方的分布）之间的重叠度，并在grokking和3B参数语言模型预训练两种设置下分析其行为。

Result: 在grokking中，LAR可预测学习函数的有效维度$k \approx n^{2(1-\text{LAR})}$；在3B参数语言模型预训练中，LAR与泛化差距的变化一致，其下降速度在过拟合临近时增加。

Conclusion: LAR是一个计算高效、无需验证数据的指标，能够有效追踪模型从记忆到泛化的转变，并预警过拟合的发生。

Abstract: We study the log-alignment ratio (LAR), a measure of parameter-activation alignment, introduced in parameterization theory. We reformulate it as the overlap between a weight spectrum $p$ of the normalized squared singular values of a matrix and an activation spectrum $q$ of the normalized squared projections of inputs onto its singular directions. We show that unembedding LAR tracks the transition between memorization and generalization in two different settings by capturing the spread of $p$ and $q$ during training. In grokking, LAR predicts the effective dimension of the learned function: $k \approx n^{2(1-\text{LAR})}$, where $n$ is the input dimension of the matrix. In 3B-parameter language model pre-training, its deviation from a non-overfitting baseline tracks the generalization gap, and its rate of decline increases as overfitting approaches. LAR is computable from quantities available during the forward pass with negligible computational overhead, and requires no held-out validation data.

</details>


### [203] [Kernel Renormalization in Bayesian Deep Neural Networks: the Equivalent Wishart Ansatz in the Proportional Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29684)
*Paolo Baglioni,Christian Keup,Vincenzo Zimbardo,Rosalba Pacelli,Alessandro Vezzani,Raffaella Burioni,Pietro Rotondo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种等效Wishart假设来近似预测深度贝叶斯MLP在比例宽度极限下的泛化性能，并扩展到CNN架构，通过重正化NNGP核与最多L个标量序参数描述表示学习，在L~O(10)和P~O(10^3)的基准数据集上验证了理论的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层单隐藏层网络在比例宽度极限（训练集大小P与宽度N同步增长）下已得到深入研究，但将这些非微扰结果扩展到深度非线性网络极具挑战性，需要有效的近似方法。

Method: 提出等效Wishart假设捕获深度MLP层次化经验核的主导随机涨落，在比例极限下对配分函数进行大偏差分析，用重正化NNGP核和最多L个自洽确定的标量序参数描述表示学习；对CNN架构，识别层次化局部核重正化机制以量化有限宽度效应。

Result: 在L~O(10)和P~O(10^3)的经典基准数据集上，有效理论与贝叶斯后验采样实验整体吻合良好，同时发现两种类型的系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功将比例极限分析从浅层网络推广到深度网络，为理解深度贝叶斯神经网络的泛化行为提供了有效近似框架。

Abstract: The scaling limit where both the size of the training set $P$ and the width $N$ of a deep neural network grow at the same rate, the so-called proportional-width regime, has been intensely studied for shallow, single-hidden-layer networks. However, extending these non-perturbative results from shallow architectures to deep non-linear networks has proven very challenging. Here we present an effective approximate approach to predict the generalization performance of Bayesian multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) of fixed depth $L$ on arbitrary high-dimensional data. We propose an equivalent Wishart Ansatz to capture the dominant stochastic fluctuations of the hierarchical empirical kernels of MLPs. This allows us to perform a large deviation analysis for the partition function of MLPs in the proportional limit, expressed in terms of a renormalized NNGP kernel. In this description, even strong representation learning in the proportional limit is encoded in at most $L$ scalar order parameters, determined self-consistently. Extending the approach to convolutional architectures (CNNs), we identify a hierarchical local kernel renormalization mechanism, which allows to quantify more complex data-dependent transformations of the large-width kernel in CNNs due to finite-width effects. We test our effective theory against sampling experiments from the Bayesian posterior of finite deep neural networks with depths $L \sim O(10)$ and $P\sim O(10^3)$ on classic benchmark datasets, finding overall very good agreement together with two distinct types of systematic deviations.

</details>


### [204] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）在基于InceptionTime架构的EEG抑郁症检测模型中的应用，发现不同方法在额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其是右半球）存在部分收敛的归因模式，但方法间的差异性也凸显了假设对解释结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在EEG抑郁症分类中表现优异，但其高容量模型的决策过程难以解释，缺乏对模型可解释性的系统研究。

Method: 在InceptionTime架构上应用五种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance），在受试者层级分层5折交叉验证框架下，通过全局归因聚合进行EEG片段和受试者级别的分析。

Result: 不同方法揭示了部分收敛的归因模式，强调了额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其是右半球）的重要性。梯度法与扰动法之间存在实质性一致，而DeepSHAP产生截然不同的归因分布。方法间的变异性凸显了方法学假设对解释结果的影响。

Conclusion: 不同事后可解释性方法捕捉到EEG深度学习模型中部分重叠的相关结构，观察到的归因模式与既往EEG抑郁症研究基本一致，但应视为探索性分析而非确切的神经生理生物标志物或临床适用性证据。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [205] [CB-SLICE: Concept-Based Interpretable Error Slice Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29836)
*Yael Konforti,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Elaf Almahmoud,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CB-SLICE，一种基于概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的误差切片发现方法，利用CBM中概念预测失败来识别模型系统性错误群体，提供更忠实、更丰富的错误解释，并在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有误差切片发现方法（SDMs）生成的解释与模型推理过程脱节，只能近似逼近而非准确反映错误根源，需要更忠实于模型内部机制的误差切片识别方法。

Method: 利用概念瓶颈模型（CBM）中下游任务失败通常源于概念误预测这一特性，提出CB-SLICE方法，将共享概念预测失败的样本分组，并识别每个切片失效模式中最关键的关键词概念。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，CB-SLICE在发现已知偏差方面优于现有最先进方法，同时提供更丰富、更忠实的模型错误解释。

Conclusion: 概念表示是误差切片识别的强有力候选，基于CBM概念的SDM能够提供精细且直接关联错误源的解释，有效改进模型调试和偏差缓解。

Abstract: Despite strong average-case performance, deep learning models often exhibit systematic errors on specific population groups, known as error slices. Identifying these groups and the root causes of their failures is critical for model debugging and bias mitigation. However, existing error Slice Discovery Methods (SDMs) typically generate explanations disconnected from the model's inference process, thus only approximating the underlying error source and may be inaccurate.
  We address this limitation by leveraging Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), whose predictions are directly dependent on human-understandable semantic concepts. Since downstream task failures in CBMs commonly arise from concept mispredictions, concept representations provide a strong candidate for error slice identification, offering fine-grained explanations directly linked to the error source. Building on this insight, we introduce CB-SLICE, a concept-based SDM that groups samples with shared concept prediction failures and identifies the keyword concepts most responsible for each slice's failure mode. Across multiple benchmarks, we show that CB-SLICE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in uncovering well-known biases while providing richer and more faithful explanations of model errors.

</details>


### [206] [The Hamilton-Jacobi Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28983)
*Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza,Erika Fille T. Legara,Christopher P. Monterola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明神经网络训练等价于求解哈密顿-雅可比初值问题，其中梯度步选择最匹配观测数据的粘性哈密顿-雅可比方程初值；推理时输入为解的空间点，初始条件编码在权重中。该对应关系对log-sum-exp层精确成立，对残差网络、Transformer、循环架构（RNN、LSTM、SSM）结构成立，它们离散化同一类哈密顿-雅可比方程。单一变形参数ε统一了网络、热带代数、粘性PDE、凸优化四个视角。定量结果包括：固定t时的极小极大最优泛化率O(n^{-1/(d+2)})、ε控制的对抗鲁棒性、残差网络的反向传播对应哈密顿系统的协态方程（庞特里亚金最大值原理）、通过PDE求积得到与数据本征维数一致的标度指数、闭式O(N)影响函数（softmax归因权重π_j）及其熵景观在ε增大时经历折叠分岔合并归因盆地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将神经网络训练与推理过程映射到粘性哈密顿-雅可比方程的初值问题求解框架，建立网络、热带代数、粘性PDE、凸优化四者之间的统一理论联系，从而揭示其内在数学结构并获得定量分析结果。

Method: 建立log-sum-exp层与Hopf-Cole传播子的精确对应，进而将残差网络、Transformer、RNN/LSTM/SSM等架构统一解释为同一类哈密顿-雅可比方程的离散化形式；引入单一变形参数ε连接四个视角，利用庞特里亚金最大值原理分析反向传播，运用PDE理论导出泛化率、对抗鲁棒性、标度指数和影响函数等定量结果。

Result: 得到固定时间下的极小极大最优泛化率O(n^{-1/(d+2)})；对抗鲁棒性由ε控制；残差网络反向传播等价于哈密顿系统的协态方程；标度指数与数据本征维数一致；闭式O(N)影响函数（softmax归因权重）的熵景观在ε增大时发生折叠分岔，合并归因盆地。

Conclusion: 神经网络可被严格解释为粘性哈密顿-雅可比方程的数值求解过程，该统一视角提供了泛化、鲁棒性、反向传播、标度律和归因机制等多方面的定量理论预测。

Abstract: In this paper, training a neural network is identified, exactly, as a search through Hamilton--Jacobi initial-value problems: each gradient step selects the initial data of a viscous Hamilton--Jacobi equation whose Hopf--Cole propagator best fits the observations; at inference, the input is the spatial point at which that solution is evaluated and the initial condition is already encoded in the weights. The correspondence is exact for log-sum-exp layers and structural for broader architectures: residual networks, transformers, and recurrent architectures (RNNs, LSTMs, SSMs) each discretize the same class of Hamilton--Jacobi equations, with architecture-dependent Hamiltonian and viscosity. A single deformation parameter $\varepsilon$ unifies all four perspectives (network, tropical algebra, viscous PDE, convex optimization) in a commutative diagram closed under Lipschitz conditions. Quantitative consequences include: the minimax optimal generalization rate $O(n^{-1/(d+2)})$ for fixed $t$; adversarial robustness controlled by $\varepsilon$; backpropagation as the co-state equation of the Hamiltonian system for residual networks (Pontryagin Maximum Principle); scaling exponents consistent with data intrinsic dimension via PDE quadrature; and a closed-form $O(N)$ influence function (softmax attribution weights $π_j$) whose entropy landscape undergoes fold bifurcations as $\varepsilon$ increases, each merging attribution basins.

</details>


### [207] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BrainSimSiam，一种轻量级自监督表示学习框架，通过仅使用正样本对从fMRI数据中学习鲁棒且通用的特征，在样本量有限且标签质量不稳定的情况下，在下游分类和回归任务中超越全监督基线，接近大规模模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据获取成本高、精神科评分量表主观性强，导致数据集样本量小、标签质量参差不齐，加之fMRI数据的高维特性，模型过拟合风险显著增加。现有fMRI基础模型需要大量计算资源进行预训练和微调，难以广泛应用。

Method: 提出BrainSimSiam，一种轻量级自监督表示学习框架，利用仅含正样本的数据对进行对比学习，不依赖负样本或大batch size，通过孪生网络结构学习鲁棒且可泛化的fMRI特征表示。

Result: 在多个下游分类和回归任务中，BrainSimSiam学到的表示优于全监督基线，性能接近大规模模型，同时计算资源需求显著降低。

Conclusion: 轻量级自监督框架能够从有限fMRI数据中学习到通用且鲁棒的表示，为数据受限的神经影像学应用提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive. We show that a lightweight self-supervised framework yields representations that generalize across diverse downstream tasks, outperforming fully supervised baselines and approaching the performance of large-scale models. We introduce BrainSimSiam, a data-efficient self-supervised representation learning framework that leverages positive-only data pairs to learn robust and generalizable features. We demonstrate that the learned representations achieve strong performance across multiple downstream classification and regression tasks, highlighting the potential of BrainSimSiam for data-limited neuroimaging applications.

</details>


### [208] [Ridge Regression from Poisson Resetting: A Renewal Perspective on Spectral Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30059)
*Petar Jolakoski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了随机重置（非平衡统计物理）与岭回归（统计学习）之间的理论联系。对于线性梯度流，以速率r重置到原点产生的稳态均值恰好等于惩罚参数λ=r的岭估计量。进一步将这一恒等式推广到一般更新重置律，证明指数重置时间分布是唯一能在每个特征方向精确再现标量岭滤波器的更新律，而非指数更新律则生成替代谱滤波器。在涨落层面（加性Ornstein-Uhlenbeck噪声/SGD近似），等式仅在均值层面成立，重置过程具有非零稳态协方差，而确定性岭估计则是一个固定估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接非平衡统计物理中的随机重置与统计学习中的岭正则化，揭示两者之间尚未被认识到的深层理论关系。

Method: 利用拉普拉斯变换关系将岭回归与梯度流的指数时间平均联系起来，将指数时间解释为泊松重置的稳态年龄；建立一般更新重置律与谱滤波器的对应关系；通过加性OU过程扩展分析涨落层面的协方差与风险。

Result: 指数重置时间是唯一能精确再现标量岭滤波器的更新律；非指数重置律生成替代谱滤波器；在加性噪声下均值等式成立但协方差结构不同。

Conclusion: 随机重置与岭正则化之间存在深刻的理论对应关系，为理解正则化机制提供了新的非平衡统计物理视角。

Abstract: We connect stochastic resetting from non-equilibrium statistical physics with ridge regularization in statistical learning. For linear gradient flow, resetting to the origin at rate $r$ produces stationary mean $(X^\top X+rI)^{-1}X^\top y$, exactly the ridge estimator with penalty $λ=r$. This uses the known Laplace-transform relationship between ridge regression and exponential-time averaging of gradient flow, with the exponential time now interpreted as the stationary age associated with Poisson resetting. We then extend this identity to general renewal reset laws: the exponential reset time distribution is the unique renewal law whose stationary mean reproduces scalar ridge in every eigendirection as an exact filter identity for every positive curvature, while non-exponential renewal laws generate alternative spectral filters. At the fluctuation level, we study a separate additive Ornstein-Uhlenbeck extension with constant diffusion, interpreted as a stylized SGD approximation. In this setting, the equality holds only at the level of the mean, since the reset process has a nonzero stationary covariance from accumulated OU noise and reset-timing variance, whereas deterministic ridge is a fixed estimator with the same center. Stylized experiments compare the deterministic renewal-induced filters directly and illustrate when filters induced by non-exponential reset-time laws can differ predictively from ridge. The results for the stationary mean and the induced spectral filters are established for continuous-time gradient flow with isotropic resetting on quadratic objectives; the covariance and risk formulas additionally assume additive noise with state-independent covariance.

</details>


### [209] [FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001)
*Nishal Thomas,Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MathCheck (ICLR 2025) 的释义质量审计发现3.1%的语义错误，移除后GPT-4o排名从第2降至第4；跨模型一致性协议可自动检测这些错误。进一步发现Claude Haiku 4.5准确率86%但SCR仅50%，而9个模型的准确率差距仅10个百分点但SCR差距达32个百分点，并证明了"无免费基准"推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试忽视语义等价性对模型评测的影响，单模型评估无法检测释义错误导致的排名偏差，且存在深层测量缺口：标准准确率无法反映模型在语义等价重述下的不一致性。

Method: 对MathCheck进行释义质量审计，使用跨模型一致性协议（MathCheck上>=3/4模型，主评估上>=6/9）自动检测语义错误；提出FormInv审计协议、SCR和每定理Cochran's Q作为不变性度量，以及FormInvSelector用于情境感知模型选择。

Result: MathCheck中3.1%释义语义错误导致排名变化；自建数据集中47%的自动生成连接变体释义有语义错误；准确率差距仅10个百分点的模型，SCR差距达32个百分点；证明了无免费基准推论——不存在帕累托支配所有释义家族的模型。

Conclusion: 基准测试设计者选择的释义家族隐式决定了哪个模型获胜；FormInv提供了可复现的审计协议和不变性度量，揭示了标准基准无法捕获的模型不一致性问题。

Abstract: A paraphrase-quality audit of MathCheck (ICLR 2025) detected 4 semantically incorrect paraphrases in 129 groups (3.1%); removing them drops GPT-4o from rank 2 to rank 4 and elevates Claude Haiku and DeepSeek V3 above it; these ranking changes are invisible to any single-model evaluation. Cross-model unanimity found these errors automatically (>= 3/4 models for MathCheck; >= 6/9 for our primary evaluation) for under $10; in our own dataset the same protocol found that 47% of auto-generated connective-variation paraphrases were semantically incorrect. That flaw compounds a deeper measurement gap: Claude Haiku 4.5 achieves 86% accuracy yet SCR=50%, meaning half its theorems are answered differently under semantically equivalent restatements, while aggregate accuracy across 9 models spans only 86-96% yet Semantic Consistency Rates (SCR) span 50-82% -- a 32-point gap invisible to standard benchmarks. Formally, for any target ranking over 9 frontier models there exists a weighting over paraphrase families that realizes it (No-Free-Benchmark corollary), because no model Pareto-dominates all families -- so benchmark designers who select families are implicitly choosing which model wins. FormInv supplies the audit protocol (replicated on external benchmarks at 100% recall), SCR and per-theorem Cochran's Q as primary invariance measures evaluated on 9 models across 366-811 items (on Lean4-verified theorems), and FormInvSelector for regime-aware model selection.

</details>


### [210] [FedQHD: Closed-Form Function-Space Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29002)
*Yuchen Hou,Yongshan Chen,Zhuowen Zou,Calvin Yeung,Mohsen Imani,Tian Lan,Mahdi Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedQHD，一种使用超维随机特征编码器的联邦Q学习方法，通过线性读出结构实现函数空间中一致的参数聚合，并形式化了联邦差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FedAvg风格的参数平均在函数空间下不一致：当客户端使用异构编码器或相同的非线性网络时，平均参数并不对应任何公共函数空间中客户端值函数的加权平均。

Method: 使用超维（随机特征）状态编码器搭配线性读出层，使Q函数在状态上非线性但在可训练参数上线性。同构编码器下直接加权平均，异构编码器下通过共享锚点集构建全局教师并通过岭回归投影到各客户端表示。

Result: 在四个连续状态、离散动作控制基准上，FedQHD匹配或优于FedAvg风格基线及蒸馏方法，同时计算量大幅减少。联邦差距对编码器维度的经验依赖性与理论分析一致。

Conclusion: 线性结构使得函数空间共识更新等价于本地读出矩阵的加权平均；联邦差距可分解为子空间错位、锚点集条件数和正则化偏置三个部分，锚点与维度比m≥Di时差距可降至编码器异构性底限的倍数。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning enables decentralized agents to collaboratively improve policies or value estimates without exchanging raw trajectories. However, FedAvg-style parameter averaging is not function-space consistent: when clients use heterogeneous encoders or even identical nonlinear networks, averaged parameters need not correspond to the weighted average of client value functions in any common function space. We propose FedQHD, a federated Q-learning method using hyperdimensional (random-feature) state encoders with a linear readout, so that Q-functions are nonlinear in state yet linear in trainable parameters. This linear structure enables closed-form aggregation. With a shared encoder, the function-space consensus update coincides exactly with weighted averaging of local readout matrices. With heterogeneous encoders, the server constructs a global teacher by averaging client Q-values on a shared anchor-state set, and each client compiles this teacher into its local representation via a single ridge projection. We formalize the federation gap -- the error incurred when compiling a federated teacher into a heterogeneous client representation -- relative to a client-specific oracle projection. We show that this gap decomposes into subspace misalignment, anchor-set conditioning, and regularization bias. We further identify the anchor-to-dimension ratio $m \geq D_i$ as the well-conditioned regime in which the gap reduces to a multiple of the encoder heterogeneity floor. On four continuous-state, discrete-action control benchmarks, FedQHD matches or outperforms FedAvg-style baselines and distillation-based alternatives while requiring substantially less computation, and the empirical dependence of the federation gap on encoder dimension matches our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [211] [Learning to Extrapolate to New Tasks: A Relational Approach to Task Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30132)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出关系型任务外推器（RTE），通过将目标任务分解为已知锚任务和变换关系，学习关系算子实现系统性的任务外推，在函数预测和序列预测的多项外推任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习系统在插值任务上表现出色，但在面对训练分布支持范围之外的全新任务时泛化能力严重不足，即使基础模型取得了巨大进步，这一问题在简单的参数外推场景中依然存在。

Method: RTE将每个目标任务分解为一个已知的锚任务和连接锚与目标的变换关系，然后学习一个关系算子，将锚-变换对映射到目标任务的预测结果上。核心思想是外推本质上是关系性的——如果在训练中学到了任务A到B的变换，测试时可将相同变换应用于已知任务来关联未见任务。

Result: 在函数预测的参数外推、长度外推和组合外推等多个任务外推场景中，以及将RTE集成到基础模型的微调算法中进行序列预测时，RTE均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 外推本质上是关系性的，通过学习任务之间的变换关系而非直接学习任务本身，RTE能够实现更有效的系统性外推，为提升学习系统在分布外任务上的泛化能力提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern learning systems excel at interpolation but struggle to generalize to unseen tasks outside the training distribution's support. This failure occurs even in simple settings, such as handling task parameters beyond the training range, and persists despite advances in foundation models. To this end, we develop the Relational Task Extrapolator (RTE), an algorithm designed to enable systematic extrapolation to novel tasks. The key observation is that extrapolation is inherently relational: extrapolating to unseen tasks requires learning how tasks transform into one another. If a model learns the transformation between tasks A and B during training, it can apply that same transformation to relate known tasks to unseen ones at test time. RTE operationalizes this idea by decomposing each target task into a known anchor task and a transformation linking the anchor and target. It then learns a relational operator, mapping an anchor-transformation pair to predictions for the target task. We instantiate RTE across multiple task extrapolation regimes in function prediction, e.g. where target tasks use out-of-range parameters (parameter extrapolation), have greater compositional depth (length extrapolation), and/or recombine function primitives in unseen ways (compositional extrapolation). We further extend RTE to sequence prediction, integrating it into fine-tuning algorithms for foundation models. Across empirical studies, we find that RTE substantially outperforms existing approaches on extrapolation to novel, unseen tasks.

</details>


### [212] [LoRe: Adaptive Interaction-Evaluation Routing with Per-Step Interaction Budgets for Iterative Graph Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29005)
*Jintao Li,Yong-Yi Wang,Zheng-An Wang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRe是一种无需训练、推理时即插即用的轻量级方法，通过动态路由计算到高冲突或高不确定性的交互上，在每次迭代中仅评估固定比例的边/因子交互，从而显著提升扩散神经组合优化求解器的可扩展性，在MIS、TSP等任务上实现数倍加速和内存缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的神经组合优化求解器在每次迭代中反复评估密集的边/因子交互，导致推理时间开销大且受内存瓶颈限制，难以扩展到大规模问题。

Method: 受多体物理计算方法启发，提出LoRe：一种无需训练、推理时即插即用的包装器。它在扩散迭代的每一步中，通过动态路由计算到高冲突或高不确定性的交互上，仅评估固定比例的交互，而非使用静态kNN图或静态掩码等固定稀疏化策略。

Result: 在MIS问题上：可扩展性提升超过3倍（超越基线OOM限制），~8倍加速，~12倍峰值内存降低，且解质量保持不变。在TSP上（n=1000）：~15倍加速，44倍内存降低，路由质量竞争力强。同时展现出零样本拓扑鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LoRe通过动态交互预算分配有效解决了扩散神经求解器在大规模组合优化问题上的计算和内存瓶颈，是一种跨任务泛化性好、无需重新训练的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based neural solvers for combinatorial optimization repeatedly re-evaluate dense edge/factor interactions, making inference expensive in wall-clock time and often memory-bound at scale. Inspired by the computational methodologies of many-body physics, we introduce LoRe, a training-free, inference-time drop-in wrapper that enforces per-step interaction-evaluation budgeting: at each iteration, it evaluates only a fixed fraction of interactions by dynamically routing computation to high-conflict or high-uncertainty interactions, instead of using a fixed sparsification (e.g., static kNN graphs or static masks). Under fully inclusive end-to-end wall-clock accounting, LoRe substantially improves scalability on the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem, extending feasible inference more than $3\times$ beyond the baseline's out-of-memory limit, delivering a $\sim 8\times$ speedup and a $\sim 12\times$ peak-memory reduction, with solution quality preserved in this regime. Demonstrating cross-task generality on the large-scale Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and zero-shot robustness to topology shifts, LoRe achieves a $\sim 15\times$ speedup at $n=1000$ with a $44\times$ memory reduction and competitive tour quality.

</details>


### [213] [CalArena: A Large-Scale Post-Hoc Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30188)
*Eugène Berta,David Holzmüller,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了迄今为止最大规模的校准后处理方法标准化基准（近2000项实验），涵盖表格数据和计算机视觉任务，提出了基于适当评分规则的PHI指标作为校准评估的替代方案，并通过全面实验发现：平滑校准函数优于分箱方法、高维场景需专用多类方法、通用机器学习模型缺乏校准特定设计则不具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分类器普遍存在校准不良问题，后处理校准方法虽被广泛使用但数量众多，且现有评估规模小、不一致，导致难以确定哪些方法在实践中真正有效，亟需大规模标准化基准进行系统比较。

Method: 构建覆盖近2000次实验的大规模标准化基准，包含表格数据和计算机视觉任务（二分类、多分类、大规模分类），汇聚经典模型、现代深度学习架构和基础模型的预测结果；提出基于适当评分规则的PHI指标用于比较后处理方法；在统一评估框架内实现数十种校准方法的可复现实现。

Result: 平滑校准函数优于分箱方法；高维场景必须使用专用多类方法；通用机器学习模型若无校准特定设计则不具备竞争力。

Conclusion: 该基准是目前最全面的后处理校准实证研究，揭示了跨领域的统一模式，并提供了完整的数据、代码和评估工具以促进未来研究。

Abstract: Reliable probability estimates are critical in many machine learning applications, yet modern classifiers are often poorly calibrated. Post-hoc calibration provides a simple and widely used solution, but the large number of proposed methods, combined with small-scale and inconsistent evaluations, makes it difficult to determine which approaches are truly effective in practice. We introduce a large-scale, standardized benchmark for post-hoc calibration, covering nearly 2000 experiments across tabular and computer vision tasks, including binary, multiclass, and large-scale classification settings. Our benchmark aggregates predictions from a diverse set of classical models, modern deep learning architectures, and foundation models, and provides unified, reproducible implementations of dozens of calibration methods within a common evaluation framework. We argue that Post-Hoc Improvement (PHI) in proper scoring rules offers a principled alternative to traditional calibration error estimators for comparing post-hoc methods, capturing both calibration quality and potential degradation to the model's predictive performance. Using this framework, we conduct the most comprehensive empirical study of post-hoc calibration to date. Our results reveal consistent patterns across domains: smooth calibration functions outperform binning-based approaches, dedicated multiclass methods are essential in high-dimensional settings, and generic machine learning models are not competitive without calibration-specific design. To facilitate future research, we release all data, code, and evaluation tools, providing a plug-and-play benchmark for developing and comparing calibration methods.

</details>


### [214] [Causal Intelligence for Constraint-Aware Intervention Design to Induce State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29008)
*Zixuan Song,Uwe Mueller,Dimitris V. Manatakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST是一种因果智能方法，通过学习上下文特定的因果图和结构因果模型，结合约束感知的多目标优化，实现将系统从源状态驱动到目标状态的干预策略设计，并在合成基准和真实生物数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型大多缺乏机制性洞察，且没有用于决策制定的原则性框架，难以通过有针对性的干预将系统从一个状态驱动到另一个状态。

Method: COAST方法：学习上下文特定的因果图和结构因果模型，将观测到的分布变化归因于机制层面的因果驱动因素，并提出一种新颖的约束感知多目标优化公式，平衡干预效果、干预复杂度和目标状态稳定性。该方法模块化且领域无关，集成了特征选择、因果发现、因果建模以及干预识别与评估。

Result: 在合成基准和真实生物数据集上，COAST恢复了关键的因果驱动因素，并识别出稳健的单目标和多目标干预策略，实现了期望的状态转换，同时提供了透明的机制性理由以指导实验验证。

Conclusion: COAST为系统中状态转换的干预设计提供了一个原则性的因果智能框架，具备模块化、领域无关和可解释性强的特点，能够有效指导实验验证。

Abstract: Driving a system from one state to another through targeted interventions is a fundamental challenge in science, yet most predictive models offer limited mechanistic insight and no principled framework for decision-making. Here we present COAST (Causally Optimal Actions for State Transitions), a causal-intelligence approach for the in-silico design of constrained interventions that induce user-defined state transitions. Given data characterizing source and target states, COAST learns context-specific causal graphs and structural causal models, attributes observed distributional shifts to mechanism-level causal drivers, and introduces a novel constraint-aware multi-objective optimization formulation that balances transition efficacy, intervention complexity, and target-state stability. The approach is modular and domain-agnostic, integrating feature selection, causal discovery, causal modeling, and intervention identification and evaluation through interchangeable components. Across synthetic benchmarks and real biological datasets, COAST recovers key causal drivers and identifies robust single- and multi-target intervention strategies that achieve desired state transitions, accompanied by transparent mechanistic rationales to guide experimental validation.

</details>


### [215] [Statistical Embeddings for Similarity, Retrieval, and Interpretable Alignment of Numeric Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30289)
*M. Ross Kunz,John Merickel,Keith Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化探索性数据分析描述符和典型相关分析的方法，用于在异构特征空间中对数值表格数据集进行有意义的跨数据集对齐和相似性度量，并支持差分隐私保护。在15个数据集上取得了0.9的P@1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型缺乏在不同特征空间中对数值数据集进行有意义的原生表示机制，现有方法要么需要共享变量定义的预测建模，要么缺乏可解释的跨数据集对齐能力。

Method: 1) 通过结构化探索性数据分析描述符对数值表格数据集进行特征化；2) 使用预训练句子变换器将这些描述符嵌入到共享向量空间；3) 通过典型相关分析(CCA)量化跨数据集相似性；4) 采用惩罚化CCA恢复稀疏、可解释的变量级对应关系；5) 可选地在嵌入前对描述符集施加差分隐私保护。

Result: 在15个数据集（涵盖通用基准、材料信息学和核级石墨表征）上，总P@1得分为0.9，最近邻检索和聚类结构在嵌入消融和差分隐私预算下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该框架为将异构数值数据集成到检索增强生成流水线中提供了原则性路径，同时保留了统计上下文，可直接应用于数据驱动的算法选择和未知数据集的仿真模型初始化。

Abstract: Numeric tabular datasets are the dominant data format in scientific practice, yet large language models lack native mechanisms for representing numeric datasets in a meaningful way across heterogeneous feature spaces. Existing approaches either target predictive modeling over individual datasets, which requires a shared set of variable definitions, or lack mechanisms for interpretable cross-dataset alignment. The proposed methodology characterizes numeric tabular datasets through structured exploratory data analysis descriptors, embeds those descriptors into a shared vector space using a pretrained sentence transformer, and quantifies cross-dataset similarity via Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA). Furthermore, a penalized formulation of CCA is applied to recover sparse, interpretable variable-level correspondences between datasets, identifying which statistical descriptors or variable-level quantities drive cross-dataset alignment without requiring shared variable names or feature conventions. Differential privacy is optionally applied to the descriptor set prior to embedding, supporting deployment in sensitive data contexts without requiring access to raw observations at time of comparison. The methodology is evaluated across 15 datasets spanning general-purpose benchmarks, materials informatics, and nuclear-grade graphite characterization. Results demonstrate a total P@1 score of 0.9, with known nearest-neighbor retrieval and cluster structure remaining robust across embedding ablations and differential privacy budgets. The proposed framework provides a principled pathway for integrating heterogeneous numeric data into retrieval-augmented generation pipelines while preserving statistical context, with direct applications to data-driven algorithm selection and simulation model initialization for unknown datasets.

</details>


### [216] [Label-Free Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Model Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29009)
*Matt Gorbett,Hossein Shirazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨模型熵(CME)作为无标签强化学习奖励信号，用独立验证器模型对生成器响应的平均对数似然作为奖励，避免自我强化错误，在开放式指令跟随任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法受限于奖励信号：依赖真实可验证奖励（仅适用于数学、代码等自动正确性检查领域）或人类偏好标签（成本高、易被奖励黑客攻击）。无标签方法使用自指信号（多数投票、token熵）却会强化模型自身错误。

Method: 提出Cross-Model Entropy (CME)，即生成器响应在独立验证器模型下的平均对数似然，作为无标签奖励信号。CME连续、无需训练，基于原理：验证器感到"不惊讶"的响应很可能是正确或高质量的。因验证器独立于生成器，信号无法通过自一致性游戏。将CME集成到GRPO中，无需修改其他训练环节。

Result: 在开放式指令跟随任务（UltraFeedback提示，AlpacaEval 2.0评估）上，CME奖励在四个模型家族（Qwen、Llama、Gemma、OLMo）和三种训练范式（预训练、SFT、指令微调）的头对头LLM-as-Judge比较中均击败未训练基线，调整后的胜率范围为52.5%至71.4%。

Conclusion: CME将无标签RL扩展到开放式指令跟随领域——这是自指信号不适用或不擅长的场景，为更通用的RL后训练提供了有效替代方案。

Abstract: Post-training large language models with reinforcement learning is bottlenecked by the reward signal. Existing approaches require either ground-truth verifiable rewards, restricting training to domains with automatic correctness checks (e.g., mathematics, code execution), or human preference labels, which are expensive to collect and prone to reward hacking. Recent label-free methods replace ground-truth verifiers with self-referential signals like majority voting or token entropy over a model's own outputs, but risk reinforcing a model's own errors. In this work we propose Cross-Model Entropy (CME), the mean log-likelihood of a generator's response under a separate verifier model, as a label-free reward signal for RL post-training. CME is continuous, training-free, and grounded in the principle that responses a verifier finds unsurprising are likely correct or high quality. Because the verifier is independent of the generator, the signal cannot be gamed through self-consistency. We integrate CME into GRPO with no other changes to the training loop, extending label-free RL to open-ended instruction following -- a regime where self-referential signals are inapplicable or poorly suited. On open-ended instruction following (UltraFeedback prompts, evaluated on AlpacaEval 2.0), CME rewards beat the untrained base in head-to-head LLM-as-Judge comparisons across four model families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma, OLMo) and three training regimes (pretrained, SFT, and instruction-tuned), with tie-adjusted win rates ranging from 52.5% to 71.4%. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [217] [Reasoning with Sampling: Cutting at Decision Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30327)
*Felix Zhou,Anay Mehrotra,Quanquan C. Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出熵切割Metropolis-Hastings算法，利用基础模型的下一词熵作为代理来识别推理轨迹中的关键决策点并从中重采样，相较于均匀随机切割更高效地从幂分布中采样，在多个基准测试上持续超越基线及RL训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明从基础模型的幂分布中采样可媲美强化学习后训练的效果，但高效采样需要"混合"到幂分布，即需要在目标分布的模态间移动（如尝试不同推理策略）。先前工作提出的均匀随机切割方法倾向于重写局部细节而非重新审视关键决策点，效率低下。

Method: 提出Entropy-Cut Metropolis-Hastings算法，利用基础模型的下一词熵作为代理识别推理轨迹中的关键决策点（如证明策略或算法的选择），从这些位置进行重采样而非均匀随机切割。

Result: 在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA Diamond和AIME26四个基准测试上，该方法一致优于基线方法和RL训练模型。在简化推理模型中证明，其混合时间与轨迹中的决策数量成比例，而非与更长的词元数量成比例。

Conclusion: 熵跳跃是识别推理决策点的有效代理，基于此的采样方法在效率和性能上均优于均匀切割方法和强化学习训练的模型。

Abstract: Frontier reasoning models are produced by posttraining base language models with reinforcement learning. Recent work has challenged this by showing that sampling from a sharpened version of the base model's distribution, a so-called power distribution, elicits comparable reasoning without additional training, curated datasets, or verifiers. However, making this method practical requires efficiently sampling from the power distribution. A sampler needs to "mix" to the power distribution, which necessitates moving between modes of the target distribution; intuitively, e.g., trying different reasoning strategies. The samplers proposed in prior works repeatedly select a "cut" position in the current reasoning trace uniformly at random and resample the suffix from that position onward. However, reasoning traces typically contain a few consequential decisions (e.g., the choice of proof strategy or algorithm), and we observe that a uniformly chosen cut tends to rewrite local details rather than revisit decision points. We introduce an algorithm (Entropy-Cut Metropolis-Hastings) that uses the base model's next-token entropy as a proxy to identify key decision points and resample from those positions. We empirically verify that entropy jumps are a useful proxy for decision points and, in a stylized model of reasoning, prove that our method's mixing time scales with the number of decisions in a trace rather than with the number of tokens, which can be much larger. Across MATH500, HumanEval, GPQA Diamond, and AIME26, our method consistently improves over baselines and RL-trained models.

</details>


### [218] [Designing Active Tether-Net Systems for Space Debris Capture with Graph-Learning-Aided Mixed-Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29021)
*Feng Liu,Achira Boonrath,Gishnu Madhu,Eleonora M. Botta,Souma Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的优化方法，用于解决主动绳索-网系统在捕获空间碎片时涉及的多变量混合组合非线性规划（MCNLP）问题，通过将组合优化简化为连续优化，显著加快了收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动绳索-网系统是捕获大型非合作目标（如空间碎片）的有效方案，但系统的设计和控制选择涉及连续、整数和分类变量的混合组合非线性规划问题，传统二进制编码方法难以有效求解高非线性和多模态的MCNLP问题，而整数编码方法可能引入组合间的伪关系。

Method: 采用并扩展了一种图学习辅助优化方法：训练图神经网络（GNN）对候选组合（表示为图中的节点）进行评分和推荐，以候选设计的连续变量向量作为输入，从而将MCNLP优化问题简化为标准NLP问题，并使用粒子群优化（PSO）算法结合基于梯度的微调进行求解。

Result: 在同时设计网的形态、机动单元（MUs）的质量和推进器选择以及控制器瞄准点的任务中，基于GNN的推荐器相比直接求解MCNLP问题，能够显著更快地收敛到类似的优化解。

Conclusion: 图学习辅助优化方法能有效处理绳索-网系统中复杂的混合组合非线性规划问题，为空间碎片捕获系统的设计优化提供了高效可行的新途径。

Abstract: Active tether-net systems are a promising solution for capturing large non-cooperative targets, such as space debris, by deploying a flexible net manipulated by maneuverable units (MUs). However, concurrent systematic explorations of design and control choices of the tether-net system to understand its full potential remain limited, partly due to the complex, constrained, nonlinear optimization problem that it presents -- one that involves a mixture of continuous, integer and categorical variables, with the latter two arising from net connectivity and component choices, respectively. Classical binary encoding methods are often ineffective for solving highly nonlinear and multimodal Mixed Combinatorial Nonlinear Programmings (MCNLPs) in engineering design, while integer coding approaches can introduce spurious relations among combinations. Given the graph-structured characteristics of the combinatorial space, this paper adopts and extends a new graph-learning-aided optimization approach to solve this MCNLP problem. Here, a Graph Neural Network (GNN) is trained to score (as output) and thereof recommend candidate combinations represented as nodes in a graph, with the continuous variable vector portion of a candidate design given as input. As a result, the MCNLP optimization reduces to an NLP, which can be solved using standard solvers. While this reduction approach is agnostic to the choice of the NLP solver, here a state-of-the-art Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) algorithm with gradient-based fine-tuning is used as the solver. Demonstrated on the problem of concurrently designing the morphology of the net, choice of mass and thrusters in the MUs and aiming points used by the controller of the tether-net system, the GNN-based recommender is shown to provide significantly faster convergence to similar optimal solutions, compared to direct solution of the MCNLP problem.

</details>


### [219] [Return-to-Go Is More Than a Number: Q-Guided Alignment for Return-Conditioned Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29028)
*Yuxiao Yang,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-ALIGN DT 通过引入 Q 函数对齐输入 RTG 与策略性能，结合 RTG 扰动微调技术，实现了对条件序列模型更好的可控性和性能，在 D4RL 基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件序列模型（CSMs）将回报-待实现（RTG）作为简单数值输入，而未将其与策略的实际性能对齐，导致控制信号与策略输出之间存在不一致。

Method: 提出 Q-ALIGN DT 框架，利用 Q 函数为 CSMs 提供密集引导，并通过 RTG 扰动技术对 CSM 进行微调，确保高 RTG 映射到高期望回报的轨迹。

Result: 在 D4RL 基准上实现优越的可控性和性能；有效学习了结构化的策略族，保持精确对齐；在速度跟踪等任务上泛化良好，优于先前方法。

Conclusion: Q-ALIGN DT 能够高效学习期望策略，当 RTG 足够高时输出接近最优的策略，实现了输入 RTG 与策略 Q 值的一致性对齐。

Abstract: Conditioned Sequence Models (CSMs) learn policies by treating return-to-go (RTG) as a control signal. However, existing CSMs often treat the RTGs as simple numerical inputs rather than aligning them with the performance of their policies. In this paper, we propose Q-ALIGN DT, a framework that enforces this alignment by ensuring the $Q$-value of the output policy is consistent with the input RTG. By leveraging a $Q$ function to provide dense guidance to CSMs and further fine-tuning it using an RTG-perturbation technique with the CSM, our method ensures that higher RTGs are consistently mapped to trajectories with higher expected returns. Theoretically, we show that Q-ALIGN DT can efficiently learn the desired policy and output a near-optimal one when the RTG is sufficiently high. Empirically, we demonstrate through extensive experiments that Q-ALIGN DT achieves superior controllability and performance across the D4RL benchmark. Remarkably, our model effectively learns a structured family of policies that maintains precise alignment and generalizes to tasks like velocity-tracking where prior methods fail.

</details>


### [220] [Moment Matching Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29033)
*Yiyan,Liang,Sifei Liu,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoMa QL框架，利用最大均值差异（MMD）匹配原始分布与目标分布的所有阶统计量，加速基于分数的生成模型和流模型的推理过程，在D4RL任务中实现计算效率与性能的兼顾，并在离线到在线RL任务中展现出更快的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数和流的生成模型在强化学习中存在推理延迟长的瓶颈，限制了其在迭代采样场景下的应用效率。

Method: 提出Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL)框架，引入统计假设检验中的最大均值差异（MMD）技术，强制匹配原始分布与目标分布的所有阶矩统计量，确保条件得分函数的分布级收敛。

Result: 在D4RL任务中，MoMa QL在保持竞争力性能的同时显著提升计算效率；在离线到在线RL任务中，通过加速流策略的动作采样过程，展现出更优的在线微调适应能力。

Conclusion: MoMa QL通过矩匹配机制有效缓解了生成模型在RL中的推理延迟问题，兼具稳定性和效率，特别适用于需要快速在线适应的场景。
