Abstract: Score-based and flow-based generative models exhibit remarkable expressive capacity in capturing complex distributions, and have been extensively deployed in tasks ranging from image generation to reinforcement learning. Nevertheless, these models suffer from prolonged inference latency, which imposes a significant computational bottleneck in RL with iterative sampling. To overcome this limitation, we propose a new framework named Moment Matching Q-Learning (MoMa QL), which utilizes a technique from statistical hypothesis testing known as maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) that intend to match all orders of statistics between the original and target distribution. By enforcing strong regularization on all moment statistics, this algorithm guarantees distribution-level convergence for conditional score function and remains stable under various hyperparameters. Empirically, we show that our method MoMa QL is more computationally efficient with a comparable if not competitive performance in various D4RL tasks. Remarkably, by accelerating the action sampling process for flow-based policies, MoMa QL demonstrates superior performance in offline-to-online RL tasks because of faster and stronger adaptability for online interactive finetuning.

</details>


### [221] [Parallel Adaptive Multi-Objective Evolutionary Learning of Discretized Bayesian Network Classifiers for Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29058)
*Damy M. F. Ha,Tanja Alderliesten,Peter A. N. Bosman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并行化策略和自适应过拟合抑制机制来提升Baymex算法（多目标进化贝叶斯网络学习算法）的可扩展性，并在临床分类任务中验证其性能，在16核CPU上获得最高54倍加速，且生成紧凑、临床可检查的贝叶斯网络分类器，性能与临床基线模型相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Baymex算法虽在贝叶斯网络学习中表现出色，但存在计算耗时长且仅在合成数据上评估的局限，亟需提升其可扩展性并验证其在真实临床数据上的表现。

Method: 引入并行化策略加速计算；设计自适应过拟合抑制机制引导优化向低过拟合网络方向搜索；将Baymex重新配置为通过多目标优化交叉熵损失和BIC复杂度项来训练贝叶斯网络分类器。

Result: 在16核CPU上实现最高54倍加速；在RADCURE、SUPPORT和一个内部数据集上，Baymex获得了与决策树、逻辑回归、朴素贝叶斯和随机森林等临床基线模型统计上相似或更优的预测性能，同时生成紧凑且临床可检查的贝叶斯网络，其包含的预测因子与已知临床因素一致。

Conclusion: Baymex通过并行化和自适应过拟合抑制显著提升了可扩展性，并在真实临床分类任务中证明了其作为可解释AI工具的有效性，能在不牺牲预测性能的前提下生成可解释的紧凑贝叶斯网络分类器。

Abstract: Bayesian Networks (BNs) are of interest from an explainable AI viewpoint, offering transparent probabilistic models for decision support. Baymex is a recently introduced multi-objective evolutionary algorithm for learning discretized BNs, enabling experts to trade-off different objectives of interest, such as likelihood, model complexity, and prior beliefs. While Baymex has been shown to outperform state-of-the-art BN learning approaches, Baymex still 1) requires a lot of computation time and 2) has only been evaluated on synthetic data. To improve scalability, we introduce a parallelization strategy as well as a mechanism that enables adaptively steering optimization toward networks that overfit less. We furthermore reconfigure Baymex to train a BN classifier through multi-objective optimization of cross-entropy loss and the BIC complexity term so as to evaluate its performance on real-world clinical classification tasks. Besides observing speedups up to over 54 times on a 16-core CPU, comparisons against clinically familiar baselines (decision trees, logistic regression, naive Bayes, and random forests) on two open-source (RADCURE and SUPPORT) and one in-house dataset, show that Baymex obtains statistically similar or better predictive performance while producing compact, clinically inspectable BNs. Importantly, Baymex finds multiple plausible BN classifiers that contain predictors consistent with established clinical factors.

</details>


### [222] [Ensemble Score Filtering for Real-Data Energy Consumption Forecast Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29072)
*Ruoyu Hu,Dahai Yu,Feng Bao,Guang Wang,Guannan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用集成评分滤波器（EnSF）对预训练的黑盒时空能耗预测模型进行数据同化校正，解决高维部分观测下的能耗状态估计问题，实验表明EnSF优于开环传播和集成卡尔曼滤波（EnKF）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际能耗数据常存在不完整、含噪声或延迟的问题，需要结合学习型预测模型与数据同化方法进行序贯校正，以提高状态估计的准确性。

Method: 将预训练的黑盒时空预测模型视为状态传播器，采用EnSF同化部分含噪观测；EnSF基于分数扩散模型近似滤波分布，利用封闭形式分数表示和蒙特卡洛近似避免重训练。

Result: 开环传播长期不可靠，EnSF显著改善状态估计；非线性观测下EnSF比EnKF提供更强的校正效果。

Conclusion: EnSF能有效融合学习型预测模型与数据同化，在高维部分观测的能耗预测中提升估计精度，尤其适用于非线性观测场景。

Abstract: Accurate estimation and forecasting of energy consumption are important for power-system operation, planning, and demand-side management. In practice, however, complete and timely measurements may not always be available, and the observed data can be partial, noisy, or delayed. This motivates the use of learned forecasting models for predicting the evolving consumption state, together with data assimilation methods for sequential forecast correction. In this work, we study a high-dimensional data assimilation problem for real energy-consumption data. \modeltext{The forward prediction is supplied by a pretrained black-box spatio-temporal forecasting model, which is treated as the state propagator in the filtering procedure.} We employ the Ensemble Score Filter (EnSF) to assimilate partial and noisy observations and to correct the forecast trajectory over time. The EnSF uses score-based diffusion models to approximate filtering distributions and avoids retraining neural-network score models during assimilation by using a closed-form score representation and Monte Carlo approximation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that open-loop propagation of the learned forecasting model can become unreliable over long horizons, while EnSF-based correction substantially improves state estimation. Comparisons with the Ensemble Kalman Filter (EnKF) further show that EnSF provides stronger correction under the nonlinear observation setting considered in this work.

</details>


### [223] [Knowledge Offloading: Decomposing LLMs into Sparse Backbones and Memory Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29075)
*Karim Galliamov,Rochelle Choenni,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出知识卸载(KOFF)框架，将预训练LLM分解为稀疏共享骨干网络和领域特定外部记忆模块，在约12%全局稀疏度下保持模型性能，表明知识可在共享核心与可交换外部记忆间重新分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM将通用能力和领域知识编码在同一组参数中，缺乏模块化分离。探索是否可以将专门化知识从共享骨干网络中移出至外部记忆模块，实现计算架构重组。

Method: 基于冻结基模型，联合学习结构化剪枝掩码和轻量恢复模块(LoRA适配器+可学习KV缓存)，将LLM分解为稀疏共享骨干和领域特定记忆。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen系列(3B-8B)模型上，约12%全局稀疏度下KOFF保留大部分未剪枝模型性能，而相同稀疏度下无记忆剪枝性能严重下降；语言特异性神经元优先被移除，语言通用神经元保留在骨干中。

Conclusion: 知识可以在共享核心与可交换外部记忆之间重新分配，LoRA和可学习KV记忆具有互补性，分解具有语义意义。

Abstract: LLMs encode both general capabilities and domain-specific knowledge in a single set of parameters. We ask whether this capacity can be reorganized: keeping broadly useful computation in a shared backbone, while moving specialized knowledge into external memory modules. We propose \emph{knowledge offloading} (KOFF), a framework for decomposing a pretrained LLM into a sparse shared backbone and domain-specific memories. Starting from a frozen base model, we jointly learn a structured pruning mask and lightweight recovery modules, implemented as LoRA adapters and learned key-value caches. Across Llama and Qwen models from 3B to 8B, we find that non-trivial capacity can be moved out of the shared backbone without a large loss in model ability. At around 12\% global sparsity, KOFF preserves much of the unpruned model's performance, while pruning the same frozen model without memories degrades sharply. Ablations show that LoRA and learned KV memories are complementary, and specialization analyses suggest that the learned decomposition is meaningful: language-specific neurons are preferentially removed while language-general neurons largely remain in the backbone. These results suggest that knowledge can be reallocated between a shared core and swappable external memories.

</details>


### [224] [OISD: On-Policy Internal Self-Distillation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29089)
*Xinyu Liu,Darryl Cherian Jacob,Yang Zhou,Jindong Wang,Pan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OISD框架，通过在策略内部自蒸馏（从最终层向中间层传递logit对齐和注意力对齐信号），提升强化学习后训练阶段中间表征的预测能力，在数学推理任务上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL后训练方法主要利用稀疏的最终结果奖励优化最终输出策略，忽略了中间表征中包含的预测信号，未能充分挖掘模型的推理潜力。

Method: 提出OISD框架，包含两个核心对齐机制：(1) Logit对齐，传递高层推理行为（如何思考）；(2) 注意力对齐，传递一致的注意力模式（关注哪里）。最终层同时作为策略网络和固定的内部教师，指导选定的中间层。结合GRPO，使用带符号优势加权的Jensen-Shannon对齐进行蒸馏。

Result: 在四个数学推理任务上，OISD相较于强推理RL基线取得了显著且一致的改进。

Conclusion: 通过将最终层的预测信号蒸馏到中间表征，可以有效提升推理能力，无需外部特权信息，为RL后训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning (RL) post-training approaches primarily optimize the final output policy using sparse outcome-level rewards, while largely overlooking predictive signals encoded in intermediate representations. In this paper, we introduce a new paradigm called on-policy internal self-distillation and propose the OISD framework, which improves reasoning by transferring on-policy predictive signals from the final layer to intermediate representations. During rollout and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) optimization, the final layer acts as both the policy and a detached internal teacher for selected intermediate layers, which are guided to align with it through two complementary mechanisms: logit alignment, which transfers high-level reasoning behaviors (how to think), and attention alignment, which enforces consistent attention patterns (where to look) from the final layer to the selected intermediate layer, both without requiring external privileged information. Our OISD, together with GRPO, employs signed advantage-weighted Jensen--Shannon alignment to distill informative intermediate representations while preserving policy consistency under a unified acting policy. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of OISD, with substantial and consistent improvements over strong reasoning RL baselines across four mathematical reasoning tasks. The code will be released at https://github.com/THE-MALT-LAB/OISD

</details>


### [225] [Model Merging by Output-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29101)
*Bethan Evans,Benjamin Etheridge,Stephen Roberts,Jared Tanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将模型合并形式化为一个凸二次规划问题，在残差更新上求解，通过校准输入和微调模型输出最小化平方输出校准目标，统一了现有方法（任务算术、模型汤、TIES、DARE）作为特例，并提供了预测合并质量的闭式诊断指标。单层设置匹配或超越现有方法，多层顺序合并方法在语言和视觉基准上持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法（如任务算术、模型汤、TIES、DARE）虽然计算高效且经验成功，但依赖于启发式设计选择，缺乏正式的最优性保证。作者旨在为模型合并提供理论基础和最优性保障。

Method: 将模型合并形式化为一个凸二次规划问题，在残差更新上最小化平方输出校准目标；推导出闭式诊断指标（残差能量分数）预测合并质量；扩展到多层场景的顺序逐层合并算法。

Result: 在单层设置中，QP方法匹配或超越现有方法；最优基在显著增益时超过廉价的对角QP；多层顺序合并方法在语言和视觉基准上持续优于基线。

Conclusion: 提出的凸二次规划框架为模型合并提供了理论基础和最优性保证，统一了现有启发式方法，并提供了可预测合并质量的诊断工具，在多任务合并场景中具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Model merging combines fine-tuned checkpoints into a single multi-task model without retraining. Existing methods - such as task arithmetic, model soups, TIES, and DARE - are computationally efficient and empirically successful, but rely on heuristic design choices and lack formal optimality guarantees. We show that merging can be formulated as a convex quadratic programme over residual updates, yielding weights that minimise a squared-output calibration objective using calibration inputs and fine-tuned model outputs, and subsuming existing methods as special cases. Our framework yields a closed-form diagnostic - the fraction of residual energy captured by a chosen basis - that predicts downstream merge quality using only the calibration set. Empirically, the QP matches or outperforms existing methods in the single-layer setting, and we characterise when the optimal basis provides significant gains over the cheaper diagonal QP. We extend to multi-layer merging via a sequential layer-wise algorithm and demonstrate consistent gains across language and vision benchmarks.

</details>


### [226] [Bridging Chemists and AI: An Expert-Augmented Framework for Interpretable Route Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29108)
*Yujia Guo,Mikhail Kabeshov,Tat Hong Duong Le,Samuel Genheden,Marco V. Mijangos,Varvara Voinarvoska,Giulia Bergonzini,Ola Engkvist,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合机器学习与化学专家知识的合成路线评估框架，使用DeepSets模型基于树编辑距离训练，再通过专家评估微调，能输出定量分数和可解释的定性类别（Good/Plausible/Bad），在评分和分类任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的合成路线评估系统过于简化多目标优化问题，且依赖专利路线等代理数据集而缺乏普适标准。迫切需要一种既能定量评分又具备可解释性的评估框架。

Method: 采用DeepSets模型，先用参考路线与机器生成路线之间的树编辑距离进行训练，再用化学专家评估进行微调。模型同时输出定量分数和三个定性类别（Good, Plausible, Bad）。

Result: 类别评估预测的Spearman相关系数0.78，Pearson相关系数0.77；评分预测的top-1准确率60.2%，远超基线方法的17.5%。

Conclusion: 专家增强的数据驱动评分框架能有效结合机器学习与化学领域知识，在合成路线评估中实现了显著优于以往方法的性能，兼具准确性与可解释性。

Abstract: Selecting efficient multi-step synthetic routes is a central challenge in organic synthesis, particularly in medicinal and process chemistry, where route choice directly impacts feasibility, cost, and development efficiency. Data-driven assessment systems often oversimplify the multi-objective nature of synthesis design and rely on proxy datasets, such as patent routes, rather than universally grounded criteria. To address this, we introduce an expert-augmented, data-driven scoring framework that integrates machine learning with chemists' domain knowledge for both numerical and explainable route assessment. A DeepSets-based model is trained using tree edit distance between reference and machine-generated routes, and then fine-tuned with expert evaluations to produce both quantitative scores and interpretable qualitative categories: Good, Plausible, and Bad. The resulting system achieves a Spearman correlation coefficient of 0.78 and a Pearson correlation of 0.77 for category assessment prediction, and 60.2% top-1 ranking accuracy for score prediction, substantially outperforming the previous baseline of 17.5%.

</details>


### [227] [When and How Long? The Readout-Mediator Angle in Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29126)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现线性探针可以完美解码模型表征，但与模型实际计算方向正交，导致探针完全无关模型行为；通过日历日期推理任务证明探针与因果子空间存在正交性，并在多尺度模型和多个任务中复现，质疑了基于探针的可解释性和安全监控方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证线性探针是否真正捕捉到了模型用于推理的因果表征，质疑探针基于可解释性的安全性监控部署的有效性。

Method: 使用sin/cos探针解码日间特征，与DAS识别的因果子空间对比，计算读出头-中介子空间夹角并与Haar均匀零分布比较；通过电路逆向工程分析注意力头和MLP的贡献；使用稀疏自动编码器分解验证语义分离。

Result: 探针方向与模型实际使用的因果子空间正交，与随机子空间夹角无差异；消融探针方向不影响模型性能，而消融DAS四维子空间完全破坏性能；该正交性在1.5B-9B四个规模、两个模型家族及空间位移、符号算术任务中均复现。

Conclusion: 读出头-中介子空间正交性是探针可解释性的通用失败模式，直接质疑了基于探针的运行时安全监控方案的有效性。

Abstract: A linear probe can decode a representation almost perfectly and yet be completely irrelevant to how the model uses it. On calendar-date duration reasoning in language models, a $\sin$/$\cos$ probe recovers day-of-year from a layer's activations, yet ablating its direction has no effect on the model's answers -- while ablating a four-dimensional subspace found by Distributed Alignment Search (DAS) at the same layer collapses performance entirely. We measure the angle between these two subspaces -- the \emph{readout-mediator angle} -- and find it indistinguishable from the angle between two random subspaces (the Haar-uniform null), meaning the probe has learned a direction orthogonal to the model's actual computation. Reverse-engineering the circuit reveals why: attention heads route month-grained context through learned QK offsets at ${\pm}30$ and ${\pm}61$ days, and MLPs then convert \emph{when} (absolute date) into \emph{how long} (duration) -- all downstream of the causal subspace the probe never touches. Sparse-autoencoder decomposition confirms the split: probe-aligned and DAS-aligned features encode semantically disjoint concepts with negligible causal overlap. The dissociation replicates across four scales ($1.5$-$9\,$B) and two model families, with preliminary evidence on two further domains (spatial displacement, symbolic arithmetic), suggesting that readout-mediator orthogonality is a general failure mode of probe-based interpretability. This directly undermines proposals to deploy probes as runtime safety monitors: the probe can report high confidence on a direction the model has silently abandoned.

</details>


### [228] [Apertus LLM Family Expansion via Distillation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29128)
*Andrei Panferov,Davit Melikidze,Martin Jaggi,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了蒸馏和量化是扩展现有模型家族至新规模和硬件格式的性价比方案，基于Apertus 8B模型生成了参数最高达4B的Apertus-v1.1蒸馏模型族，在1.7T许可令牌上训练，展示了在覆盖广泛硬件和系统需求方面的成本效益和强大精度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs被广泛应用于各种场景（如聊天助手、数据标注），需要满足预算和硬件约束。模型通常以不同大小的批次发布以适应不同约束，因此需要一种经济有效的方式来扩展现有模型家族至新规模和硬件格式。

Method: 采用蒸馏（distillation）和量化（quantization）作为扩展模型家族规模和硬件格式的方法。基于开源配方Apertus 8B LLM，在1.7T许可令牌上训练，生成了参数最高达4B的Apertus-v1.1蒸馏模型族。

Result: 成功验证了蒸馏和量化是成本效益高的方式，能够有效覆盖大范围的硬件和系统需求，同时保持强大的精度性能。

Conclusion: 蒸馏和量化是扩展现有LLM模型家族至新规模和硬件格式的经济有效途径，有助于满足多样化的预算和硬件约束需求。

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs has led to their use in great variety of applications and scenarios, such as chatbot assistants and data annotation, creating the need for the models to satisfy certain budget and hardware constraints. This has led to the trend of LLMs being released in batches consisting of similar models of various sizes for the family of models to adhere to as wide of a range of constraints as possible. In this paper, we validate distillation and quantization as a cost-effective way to expand model families to new sizes and hardware formats. Based on the open-recipe Apertus 8B LLM, we produce Apertus-v1.1 - a distilled family of models with up to 4B parameters trained on 1.7T permissive license tokens. We demonstrate cost-efficiency and strong accuracy performance of our approach for covering large ranges of hardware and systems requirements.

</details>


### [229] [Unveiling Multi-regime Patterns in SciML: Distinct Failure Modes and Regime-specific Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29153)
*Yuxin Wang,Yuanzhe Hu,Xiaokun Zhong,Xiaopeng Wang,Haiquan Lu,Tianyu Pang,Michael W. Mahoney,Yujun Yan,Pu Ren,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了科学机器学习（SciML）模型在不同超参数设置下表现出的多机制（multi-regime）行为，提出了一种机制感知的诊断框架，通过联合分析性能、训练动态和损失景观几何，发现三个关键结果：SciML模型存在一致的三机制结构；优化有效性具有机制特异性；SciML模型可能出现精细故障模式，挑战传统损失景观指标的解释。实验涵盖物理信息神经网络、神经算子、神经常微分方程等多种模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的训练行为会随超参数设置不同而落入不同的"训练机制"，在科学机器学习（SciML）模型中这种多机制行为尚未被充分研究。本文旨在建立一个统一的、任务无关的视角来理解SciML模型中的故障模式，并提供机制感知的鲁棒性改进指导。

Method: 提出一种机制感知的诊断框架，联合分析性能、训练动态和损失景观几何。通过在多种SciML模型（PINNs、神经算子、神经常微分方程）上进行实验，覆盖代表性ODE和PDE基准问题，研究不同约束施加方式和优化器设计下的多机制行为。

Result: 三个关键发现：(i) 多种标准SciML模型、不同约束施加方式和优化器设计下均存在一致的三机制结构；(ii) 优化有效性具有机制特异性，没有单一方法在所有机制下表现良好；(iii) SciML模型可能出现精细故障模式，挑战传统损失景观指标的标准解释。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的、任务无关的视角来理解SciML中的故障模式，并为提高模型鲁棒性提供了机制感知的指导方向。

Abstract: Neural networks trained under different hyperparameter settings can fall into distinct training "regimes," with consistent behavior within regimes and qualitative differences across regimes. In this paper, we study such multi-regime behavior in scientific machine learning (SciML) models through a regime-aware diagnostic framework that jointly analyzes performance, training dynamics, and loss-landscape geometry. We identify three key findings: (i) a consistent three-regime structure emerges across many standard SciML models, different constraint enforcements, and various optimizer designs; (ii) optimization effectiveness is regime-specific, with no single method performing well across all regimes; and (iii) SciML models can exhibit fine-grained failure modes that can challenge conventional interpretations of standard loss-landscape metrics. Our results provide an approach to establish a unified, task-oblivious perspective on failure modes in SciML and to inform regime-aware guidance for improving robustness. We validate these findings across widely-used SciML models, including physics-informed neural networks, neural operators, and neural ordinary differential equations, on benchmarks spanning representative ordinary and partial differential equations.

</details>


### [230] [RUBRIC-ARROW: Alternating Pointwise Rubric Reward Modeling for LLM Post-training in Non-verifiable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29156)
*Haoxiang Jiang,Zihan Dong,Tianci Liu,Wanying Wang,Ran Xu,Tony Yu,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RUBRIC-ARROW框架，通过交替训练评分规则生成器和条件裁判，利用成对偏好数据联合训练逐点评估器，在主观非可验证场景中实现具有竞争力的奖励建模精度并提升下游策略训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点式奖励建模在主观、非可验证场景中难以进行绝对评分，现有基于评分规则的方法依赖前沿大语言模型且存在硬布尔聚合导致的平局问题。

Method: 提出RUBRIC-ARROW交替训练框架：(1) 联合训练评分规则生成器和条件裁判；(2) 强化学习阶段仅使用成对偏好数据；(3) 采用基于概率的评分规则减少平局，结合阶段特定的基于偏好的奖励；(4) 交替GRPO方案协同训练逐点评估器。

Result: 在奖励建模精度上达到竞争性水平，并在下游策略后训练中取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RUBRIC-ARROW通过交替框架有效解决了主观场景中点式奖励建模的评分难题，证明了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Pointwise reward modeling offers critical signals for LLM post-training, yet struggles with absolute scoring in subjective, non-verifiable settings. Rubric-based methods address this by decomposing evaluation into explicit criteria, but existing approaches typically depend on frontier LLMs and suffer from ties caused by hard Boolean aggregation. We present RUBRIC-ARROW, an alternating framework that jointly trains a rubric generator and a rubric-conditioned judge, with its RL stage using only pairwise preference data. Our method couples a probability-based scoring rule that reduces ties with phase-specific preference-based rewards and an alternating GRPO scheme that together train the pointwise evaluator. Extensive experiments show that RUBRIC-ARROW achieves competitive reward-modeling accuracy and yields consistent gains for downstream policy post-training.

</details>


### [231] [Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29157)
*Yifei Zuo,Dhruv Pai,Zhichen Zeng,Alec Dewulf,Shuming Hu,Zhaoran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Parallax，一种可扩展的参数化局部线性注意力机制，通过消除数值求解器、引入查询样投影器来学习KV协方差，在硬件感知算法下超越FlashAttention，并在0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中实现一致的困惑度改进和下游任务性能提升，首次展示了注意力机制中架构-优化器协同设计的实证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM已成为AI核心范式，但注意力机制的核心计算原语长期未变。局部线性注意力（LLA）虽在理论上优于softmax注意力，但由于计算和数值稳定性问题，未能在大规模LLM预训练中扩展。

Method: 提出Parallax：参数化局部线性注意力。1）消除LLA中的数值求解器；2）学习额外的查询样投影器来探测KV协方差；3）提出硬件感知算法，增加算术强度使注意力进入更计算密集的区间；4）在架构-优化器协同设计中引入Muon优化器以释放Parallax能力。

Result: 解码内核在多种批大小和上下文长度下匹配或超越FlashAttention 2/3；在0.6B和1.7B规模预训练中实现一致的困惑度改进，增益可迁移至下游基准；在参数匹配和计算匹配控制下均表现出帕累托改进。

Conclusion: Parallax首次实现了注意力机制中架构-优化器协同设计的实证验证，表明通过精心设计的参数化局部线性注意力可以在不增加计算负担的情况下持续提升LLM性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the central paradigm in artificial intelligence, yet the core computational primitive of attention has remained structurally unchanged. Local Linear Attention (LLA) is an attention mechanism derived from nonparametric statistics in the test-time regression framework. In contrast to prior research on efficient attention variants, LLA upgrades the local constant estimate in softmax attention to a local linear estimate, yielding provably superior bias-variance tradeoffs for associative memory. However, LLA has not been scaled in LLM pretraining due to computational and numerical stability concerns. We introduce Parallax, a parameterized Local Linear Attention that is scalable for LLMs. Parallax eliminates the numerical solver in LLA and learns an extra query-like projector that probes the KV covariance. We place Parallax within a family of attention mechanisms connected by the bandwidth, the probe construction and the affine structure. We propose a hardware-aware algorithm that increases the arithmetic intensity over FlashAttention, shifting attention into a more compute bound regime. Our prototype decode kernel matches or outperforms FlashAttention 2/3 across diverse batch sizes and context lengths. We pretrain Parallax at 0.6B and 1.7B scales and find consistent perplexity improvements throughout pretraining with gains that transfer to downstream benchmarks. The advantage persists under both parameter-matched and compute-matched controls, demonstrating a Pareto improvement. We perform careful pretraining ablations and identify a novel phenomenon whereby Muon unlocks the capacity of Parallax. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration of strong architecture-optimizer codesign for attention mechanisms in the architecture research literature.

</details>


### [232] [PROTOCOL: Late Interaction Retrieval for Protein Homolog Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29158)
*Gabrielle Cohn,Rohan Gumaste,Minh Hoang,Vihan Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProtoCol利用ColBERT风格的延迟交互机制，通过残基级别的嵌入比较来提升蛋白质远程同源检索的灵敏度，在SCOPe和Pfam基准测试中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在序列相似性弱的"twilight zone"区域，传统比对方法和基于池化的蛋白质语言模型嵌入方法会丢失局部模体、结构域和保守残基信息，导致远程同源检索灵敏度不足。

Method: ProtoCol将蛋白质表示为残基嵌入的集合，采用ColBERT风格的延迟交互机制（MaxSim），独立编码蛋白质并预计算候选表示，通过残基级比较来评分候选序列。

Result: 在SCOPe超家族和Pfam clan基准测试中，ProtoCol优于序列组成方法、基于比对的方法、池化PLM方法和训练的单向量基线方法。

Conclusion: 延迟交互是远程同源检索的有效检索层，残基级别的比较能够捕捉到全局池化所丢失的局部模式，从而提升远程同源检测的灵敏度。

Abstract: Protein homology search underlies function annotation, structure prediction, and evolutionary analysis, but remains challenging in the "twilight zone," where global sequence similarity is weak and classical alignment methods lose sensitivity. Protein language models provide context-aware representations that could improve alignment sensitivity in this regime. However, prior protein embedding-based retrieval pipelines often pool these representations into a single vector, potentially obscuring local motifs, domains, or conserved residues that reveal remote homology. We introduce ProtoCol, a model which represents proteins as sets of residue embeddings and uses ColBERT-style late interaction to test whether residue-level comparison improves homolog retrieval. ProtoCol encodes proteins independently, keeps candidate representations pre-computable, and scores candidates with MaxSim over residue embeddings. On SCOPe superfamily and Pfam clan benchmarks, ProtoCol outperforms sequence-composition, alignment-based, pooled PLM, and trained single-vector baselines, supporting late interaction as an effective retrieval layer for remote homology search.

</details>


### [233] [Evolutionary Refinement of Generative Graph Topologies: A Hybrid WGAN-GA Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29161)
*James Sargant,Seyedeh Ava Razi Razavi,Renata Dividino,Sheridan Houghten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在GAN生成图结构的基础上，利用遗传算法（GA）对边进行精细化调整，以降低生成图与真实图在度分布、谱分布等结构属性上的偏差，实验表明GA refinement持续降低了综合MMD指标，使生成图更接近真实结构模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GAN的图生成方法虽然在边建模和类别密度匹配上有所改进，但在度分布、谱分布等关键结构属性上与真实图仍存在明显偏差，这些偏差表明重要结构特征未被完整保留。本文旨在通过后处理精炼来减小这些偏差。

Method: 在GAN框架（生成器产生节点特征和连接模式，GNN-based判别器评估图真实性和类别一致性）基础上，引入遗传算法对生成图的边进行精细化调整，引导合成图更接近真实数据分布，同时保持多样性和新颖性。

Result: 实验结果表明，GA精炼持续降低了基模型的综合最大均值差异（MMD），生成图在结构模式上更接近真实图。

Conclusion: 进化精炼是一种有效且灵活的方法，能够修正GAN-based图生成器中的剩余结构偏差，提升其在真实图合成和数据增强中的适用性。

Abstract: Generating realistic graph-structured data is challenging due to discrete connectivity, varying graph sizes, and class-specific structural patterns. Recent Generative Adversarial Networks (GAN)-based graph generation methods improve edge modelling by learning connectivity and matching class-specific density distributions. However these models still exhibit noticeable deviations such as in degree and spectral distribution when compared to real graphs, indicating that important structural properties are not fully preserved. This work aims to reduce these deviations by refining the graphs produced by an existing GAN-based graph generator framework with a Genetic Algorithm (GA). In the GAN framework, the generator produces both node features and connectivity patterns, while a GNN-based critic evaluates graph realism and class consistency to ensure global structural and class alignment. Building on this foundation, we apply a GA to refine the edges of generated graphs. The refinement process guides synthetic graphs toward closer agreement with real data, while preserving diversity and novelty. Experimental results show that the GA refinement consistently lowers combined Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) compared to the base model, leading to graphs that more closely match real structural patterns. This demonstrates that evolutionary refinement is an effective and flexible way to correct residual structural deviations in GAN-based graph generators, improving their suitability for realistic graph synthesis and data augmentation.

</details>


### [234] [Probabilistic bias adjustment of seasonal forecasts using generative machine learning: A case study of Arctic sea ice predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29172)
*Parsa Gooya,Reinel Sospedra-Alfonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于条件变分自编码器（cVAE）的概率后处理框架，通过生成器替代高斯参数化解码器、使用连续排名概率评分（CRPS）替代均方误差作为目标函数，并采用更高分辨率的目标数据集，有效改善了季节性海冰预测的校准性、分辨率、锐度及谱能量，克服了标准cVAE的模糊性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性气候预测中，大集合和高分辨率预报虽能提升不确定性采样和尺度过程捕捉，但计算成本高昂，且预报集合存在漂移、系统偏差和时空误差，需要精细的后处理与校准。现有标准cVAE方法存在预测中细尺度能量丢失和特征模糊的局限性。

Method: 1) 在cVAE框架中使用生成器替代高斯参数化解码器；2) 目标函数从均方误差（MSE）改为连续排名概率评分（CRPS）；3) 使用比原始预报更高分辨率的目标数据集进行训练。

Result: 调整后的预报在校准性、与观测分布的一致性、误差大小方面均优于基准预测，同时在分辨率、锐度和谱功率方面相较于标准cVAE有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该扩展框架有效解决了标准cVAE的模糊性问题，能够生成任意大、校准良好、偏差校正且具有更高分辨率的季节性海冰预测集合，为季节性气候预测后处理提供了有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Seasonal climate predictions support planning and risk management by offering early information of the most likely-to-occur climate conditions in the coming months, and associated uncertainties. Ensemble forecasts enable this by simulating many plausible outcomes, allowing predictions to be expressed as usable probabilities. Large ensembles and high-resolution forecasts strengthen this guidance by better sampling uncertainty and capturing finer-scale processes but come with significant computational cost. Moreover, forecast ensembles drift and exhibit systematic biases and spatio-temporal errors that grow with lead time, requiring careful post-processing and calibration. A probabilistic post-processing framework based on conditional Variational Autoencoders (cVAEs) was developed at the Canadian Center for Climate Modeling and Analysis to generate large ensembles of bias adjusted seasonal predictions of Arctic sea ice. The generative model was designed to learn the observational distribution conditioned on the biased model prediction. This enables generation of arbitrarily large ensembles of well-calibrated, bias corrected forecasts with improved skill. Here, we extend this framework to address the loss of fine-scale energy and the characteristic blurriness in predictions, a known limitation of standard cVAEs. Specifically, we employ a generator in place of the Gaussian parametrized decoder in the cVAE and use Continuous Ranked Probability Score in the objective function instead of the Mean Square Error. We further use a higher resolution target dataset compared to the raw forecast. We show that the adjusted forecasts are better calibrated, more consistent with the observational distribution, and exhibit smaller errors than benchmark predictions, while also enhancing the resolution of the raw forecasts and improving sharpness and spectral power relative to the standard cVAE.

</details>


### [235] [TIMEGATE: Sustainable Time-Boxed Promotion Gates for Continual ML Adaptation Under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29183)
*Abhijit Chakrabroty,Suddhasvatta Das,Kevin A. Gary,Yash Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIMEGATE是一个策略管理层，通过预算时间、标注、训练和评估来管理持续适应中的ML系统，实现了显著的计算节省和性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ML系统向持续适应演进，每次重训练循环消耗大量计算、标注和能源资源，需要有效的资源管理策略。

Method: 提出TIMEGATE策略管理层，通过预算时间、标注、训练和评估来管理适应过程，并发出度量可用性信号M用于部分与完全评估决策。

Result: (i) 标注比训练效率高2.3倍；(ii) 在LLaMA-3.1-8B+QLoRA上准确率从0.80提升至0.96；(iii) M信号在28个敏感度测试中有效；(iv) 100周期模拟节省66%评估计算且无静默错误提升；(v) 10%切片评估在H200上节省89%的墙钟时间和能源。

Conclusion: TIMEGATE能够有效管理持续学习中的资源分配，在不影响性能的前提下大幅降低计算和能源成本。

Abstract: As machine learning(ML) systems evolve to continual adaptation, each re-training cycle uses compute, annotation, and energy. We introduce TIMEGATE, a policy layer managing adaptation by budgeting time, labeling, training, and evaluation. TIMEGATE emits a metric-availability signal M for partial vs. full-evaluation decisions. We validate: (i) labeling outperforms training by 2.3x on Adult tabular; (ii) it transfers to LLaMA-3.1-8B + QLoRA on SST-2 (accuracy 0.80 to 0.96; M =1 in 35/36 runs); (iii) M is informative, 28-cell sensitivity shows M drops to 0.81 at tight thresholds; (iv) 100-cycle simulation achieves 66% evaluation-compute savings with no silent mis-promotions; (v) 10%-slice evaluation on LLaMA uses 89% less wall-clock and energy on a single H200 (ratios agree to 0.2%).

</details>


### [236] [Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression: Scientific Discovery via LLM-Driven Equation Search with Granular Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29184)
*Evgeny S. Saveliev,Samuel Holt,Nabeel Seedat,David L. Bentley,Jim Weatherall,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IGSR（Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression）的方法，将LLM与影响引导的剪枝策略和蒙特卡洛树搜索相结合，通过粒度的项贡献评估来提升符号回归的效率与可解释性，并在生物数据集上通过湿实验验证了其科学发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的符号回归方法使用全局标量指标（如全局MSE）作为反馈信号，无法识别方程中各组分的具体贡献或错误来源，导致搜索效率低下且缺乏可解释性指导。

Method: IGSR将方程发现建模为两阶段迭代过程：1) LLM生成候选基函数ψ_j(x)构建线性模型；2) 使用粒度影响分数Δ_j量化每个项对泛化精度的边际贡献，通过影响引导的剪枝逐步优化模型结构。将此机制集成到MCTS中，平衡对新函数形式的探索与高影响组件的利用。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench、药理学PKPD模型、流行病学模拟和真实基因组数据等多个基准测试上验证了有效性。在高维生物数据集案例研究中，IGSR发现了DNA甲基化与RNA聚合酶II暂停之间的新关系，并通过湿实验得到了验证。

Conclusion: IGSR通过引入粒度影响分数作为结构化反馈信号，显著提升了LLM在符号回归中的效率与可解释性，具备了从真实数据中发现新科学假设并得到实验验证的能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for scientific discovery, yet their application to symbolic regression is often constrained by inefficient search strategies and coarse feedback signals. Current methods typically guide LLMs using scalar metrics (e.g., global Mean Squared Error), which fail to identify which components of a proposed equation are driving performance or causing error. We introduce \textit{Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression} (IGSR), a method that frames equation discovery as an iterative two-step process combining diverse term generation with rigorous selection: an LLM generates candidate basis functions $ψ_j(\mathbf{x})$ for a linear model, which are then evaluated using granular influence scores $Δ_j$. These scores quantify each term's marginal contribution to generalization accuracy, enabling an influence-guided pruning process that systematically refines the model structure. Integrating this mechanism into a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) enables navigating the combinatorial search space while balancing exploration of novel functional forms with exploitation of high-influence components. We demonstrate IGSR's effectiveness on a diverse suite of benchmarks, including LLM-SRBench, pharmacological PKPD models, an epidemiological simulation, and real-world genomic data. Notably, we validate the framework's capacity for genuine discovery in a case study using a high-dimensional biological dataset, in which IGSR identified a novel relationship between DNA methylation and RNA Polymerase II pausing; a hypothesis that was subsequently supported via wet-lab experimentation.

</details>


### [237] [When RL Suppresses Its Own Vocabulary: Recovering Reasoning Diversity in Puzzle-to-Math Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29190)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Arjun Karuvally,Terrence Sejnowski,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究仅使用约束满足谜题（不含数学题）进行SFT和RL训练后，大模型推理能力如何跨领域迁移至数学问题。通过引入推理基元级分析框架，发现谜题SFT建立推理基元词汇表（+7pp），GSPO RL组合基元为更长推理链（+6pp），但抑制了探索性基元；引入基于困惑度的新颖性奖励后恢复探索基元（+7pp），最终将OLMo3-7B的硬数学能力从16%提升至36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在不同领域间跨域迁移的条件与机制，尤其是在后训练阶段完全不使用数学数据的情况下，推理能力如何从谜题领域迁移到数学领域。

Method: 提出推理基元级分析框架（9类跨度分类器+动机提取），将思维链片段分类为基元并追踪其演变。采用谜题SFT + 普通GSPO RL + 新颖性奖励（基于参考模型困惑度奖励多样化正确轨迹）的三阶段训练。

Result: 谜题SFT建立基元词汇表（+7pp pass@32）；GSPO RL组合基元为更长验证链（+6pp）但抑制探索基元；新颖性奖励恢复探索基元（+7pp）；最终将OLMo3-7B在OlymMATH-Hard上的性能从16%提升至36%。

Conclusion: 推理基元分析框架有效揭示了跨域迁移的机制；仅用谜题数据通过合理设计的RL训练可实现显著的数学推理能力迁移；单纯RL可能抑制探索行为，引入多样化奖励可缓解此问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves LLM reasoning, but the conditions under which it transfers across domains -- and why it does so -- remain under-explored. We study cross-domain transfer in a 7B model whose SFT and RL post-training stages use only constraint-satisfaction puzzles, with no mathematics problems in the post-training data. To analyze how transfer emerges, we introduce a reasoning primitive-level framework that combines a 9-class span classifier with motif extraction, allowing us to segment chain-of-thought traces into primitive motifs and track their evolution across training stages and domains. We find that puzzle SFT induces a reasoning-primitive vocabulary, yielding a $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} gain on OlymMATH-Hard. Vanilla GSPO then composes these primitives into longer compute-verify chains, adding a further $+6$pp. However, this RL stage also suppresses exploratory primitives such as \textit{hypothesize} and \textit{backtrack}. To address this, we introduce a novelty bonus that rewards diverse correct rollouts, using perplexity under the reference model as a signal. This restores recovery primitives during RL and adds a further $+7$pp \texttt{pass@32} relative to vanilla GSPO. Finally, the end-to-end recipe raises the hard-math capability ceiling from $16.0\%$ at the OLMo3-7B-Instruct-SFT base to $36.0\%$, without adding any mathematics problems during the SFT or RL stages.

</details>


### [238] [Stochastic Lifting for Generating Trajectories of Stochastic Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29194)
*Jules Berman,Tobias Blickhan,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机提升（Stochastic Lifting）方法，通过为每个状态转移附加独立高维随机标签，将随机动力系统的学习转化为确定性映射回归，实现单网络评估下的多样轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决随机物理系统中有限样本条件下，模型从相似当前状态预测多个可能下一状态时容易坍缩到均值预测的问题。

Method: 为训练数据中每个状态转移附加独立高维随机标签，将随机转移过程分解为当前状态与标签到下一状态的确定性映射，使用标准回归损失拟合；推理时每步采样新标签并自回归前向传播。

Result: 单网络评估每时间步即可生成多样轨迹，避免多模态预测的均值坍缩，在有限样本下有效学习随机动力系统的转移分布。

Conclusion: 利用随机标签作为辅助坐标，将随机系统建模转化为回归问题，在保持多样性的同时实现高效推理。

Abstract: Many stochastic physical systems evolve smoothly over time in the sense that the distribution of states changes regularly across time steps. The transition from current state to the next state can often be modeled as the combination of a smooth map and an explicit source of randomness. Stochastic Lifting exploits this structure by attaching an independent, high-dimensional random label to each state transition in the training data and fitting a transition map from the current state and label to the next state using a standard regression loss. The labels act as auxiliary coordinates that let the model represent multiple plausible next states from similar current states, avoiding collapse to a mean prediction in the finite-sample size regime. At inference, fresh labels are sampled at each time step and the learned map is rolled forward autoregressively, generating diverse trajectories with a single network evaluation per time step.

</details>


### [239] [Auditing Training Data in Generative Music Models via Black-Box Membership Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29202)
*Yi Chen Liu,Jiawei Yu,Kexin Cao,Syed Irfan Ali Meerza,Trishika Movva,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对生成式音乐模型的黑盒成员推断方法，通过候选音频与模型基于文本描述生成结果之间的语义和结构对齐程度来判断该音频是否属于训练集。该方法使用影子模型构建配对样本训练音乐审计器，在完全黑盒设置下实现高达98.6%的准确率，且假阳性率和假阴性率分别低至1.9%和1.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到音乐生成模型通常在大规模未公开语料上训练，缺乏验证特定音频样本是否被用于训练的机制，引发数据来源、知情同意和训练透明度方面的担忧。

Method: 核心思路是训练成员关系会导致候选样本与其文本描述条件下的生成结果之间产生更强的语义和结构对齐。具体方法包括：(1) 用候选音频的文本描述查询目标模型获得生成结果；(2) 在学习的特征空间中测量候选音频与生成输出之间的关系；(3) 通过影子模型构建每个音轨及其文本描述条件生成结果的配对样本，训练音乐审计器来分类成员关系。

Result: 在多个最先进的音乐生成器上，该方法实现了最高98.6%的准确率，假阳性率低至1.9%，假阴性率低至1.0%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在完全黑盒（无模型参数或训练元数据）的真实部署场景下，对训练数据进行可靠审计是可行的。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-music generation enable high-fidelity synthesis of structured musical audio, raising growing concerns about data provenance, consent, and training transparency. These models are typically trained on large-scale corpora with little disclosure, leaving no practical mechanism to verify whether a particular audio sample was included in training. In this paper, we investigate black-box membership inference for generative music models, aiming to determine whether a candidate music sample was used during training, given only query access to the deployed system. Our key insight is that training membership induces systematically stronger semantic and structural alignment between a candidate sample and the model's generation conditioned on its caption. We query the target model with the associated caption and measure the relationship between the candidate audio and the generated output in a learned feature space. To capture features that separate members from non-members, we construct paired examples consisting of each track and its caption-conditioned generation from shadow models, and train a music auditor to classify membership. The auditor captures alignment patterns characteristic of training membership and generalizes to unseen target models in a fully black-box setting without access to model parameters or training metadata. Across multiple state-of-the-art music generators, our method achieves up to 98.6% accuracy, with false-positive and false-negative rates as low as 1.9% and 1.0%, demonstrating that reliable training-data auditing is feasible in realistic deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [240] [Inferring the Size of Large Language Models From Popular Text Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29223)
*Ivica Nikolic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种黑盒方法，仅从模型生成的文本输出中推断大型语言模型（LLM）参数规模的下界，利用模型对流行文本的预测准确度与参数量的内在关联，通过统计检验和缩放律估计实现参数计数推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流LLM的开发者常不公开模型参数量，导致模型规模这一关键参考指标被隐藏，妨碍了对模型能力和成本的合理评估。

Method: 利用公开文本（经典文学、宗教文本、基础文献等）在预训练语料中的普遍存在性，构建不同长度文本片段的下一词预测准确率作为记忆信号，聚合形成准确率特征向量；基于此设计两种互补方法：成对统计检验（判断两个模型孰大）和PCA缩放律估计（将特征向量映射为参数计数）。

Result: 在开放权重模型上验证，两种方法均产生准确可靠的下界估计；应用于封闭权重模型时，能恢复内部产品层级关系，揭示不同开发商的缩放策略差异——部分采用大规模代际参数增长，部分受到严格参数上限约束。

Conclusion: 即使在严格的API限制下，也可以通过系统化的黑盒探测揭露隐藏的模型设计选择。

Abstract: The parameter counts of the most widely used large language models (LLMs) are often withheld by their developers, leaving model size -- a primary reference point for interpreting capabilities and costs -- largely undisclosed. We propose a black-box method to infer conservative lower bounds on LLM size from generated text outputs alone, requiring nothing beyond the ability to submit text fragments and observe next-token predictions. Our approach is grounded in a key observation: popular, widely-circulated texts -- such as classical literature, religious texts, and foundational documents -- are present in virtually every large-scale pretraining corpus, and how accurately a model predicts the next word across text fragments of varying length is a reliable signal of how much it has memorized them, which in turn is fundamentally limited by its total parameter count. We aggregate this memorization signal across a diverse corpus of texts and fragment lengths into a single accuracy profile vector per model, and build two complementary inference methods on top of it: a pairwise statistical test that determines which of two models is larger, and a scaling-law estimator that extracts a one-dimensional latent index from these vectors via Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to map the aggregated signal to a parameter count. Validated on a broad set of open-weight models, both methods produce accurate and reliable lower bounds. When applied to popular closed-weight models, our framework recovers internal product hierarchies and reveals a clear divergence in industry scaling strategies: while some developers yield significantly higher bounds indicative of large generational parameter growth, others operate under strict parameter ceilings, demonstrating that hidden design choices can be systematically probed even under strict API limitations.

</details>


### [241] [Traditional machine learning vs. deep learning from dynamic graph representations of proteins' 3D folds in the task of protein structure classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29228)
*Aydin Wells,Francis A. Gatsi,Aaron Striegel,Tijana Milenković*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了动态蛋白质结构网络（PSN）中传统机器学习（ML）与深度学习（DL）在蛋白质结构分类任务中的表现，发现在约44,000个数据集上两者准确率几乎持平，但DL平均慢10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证在动态PSN特征基础上，深度学习相比传统机器学习是否能带来性能提升。

Method: 在72个数据集（约44,000个CATH/SCOPe标记的动态PSN）上，对比传统ML（手工特征+现成分类器）与自动DL方法的分类准确率和速度。

Result: 传统ML与DL在绝大多数数据集上准确率接近持平，但DL平均慢10倍以上。

Conclusion: 在动态PSN-based PSC任务中，传统ML在保持同等准确率的同时效率远优于DL。

Abstract: Protein structure classification (PSC) uses supervised learning to predict a protein's CATH/SCOP(e) class from the protein's sequence or 3D structural feature(s). We already modeled 3D structures as (static) protein structure networks (PSNs), demonstrating the competitiveness of PSN-based features to sequence or direct (i.e. non-network) 3D structural features in the PSC task. More recently, we demonstrated the power of features extracted from dynamic PSNs over features extracted from static PSNs (and thus by transitivity over sequence and direct 3D structural features) in the same task. That dynamic PSN approach used traditional machine learning (ML), combining manual (pre-engineered) features with an off-the-shelf classifier. Here, we evaluate whether automatic deep learning (DL) from the dynamic PSNs yields improvements. Our evaluation on 72 datasets spanning ~44,000 CATH- or SCOPe-labeled dynamic PSNs reveals that in terms of PSC accuracy, traditional ML and DL are (close to) tied for a large majority of the datasets, while DL is on average 10+ times slower. We are the first to evaluate traditional ML vs. DL in the dynamic PSN-based PSC task.

</details>


### [242] [BlockBatch: Multi-Scale Consensus Decoding for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29233)
*Xiaoyou Wu,Cheng-Jhih Shih,Binfei Ji,Yong Liu,Yingyan,Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BlockBatch，一种训练无关的在线推理框架，通过并行执行多个不同块大小的分支来加速扩散语言模型推理，利用块大小多样性实现KV缓存轨迹的互补，平均减少26.6%的去噪NFE并实现1.33倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型(dLLM)推理存在块大小权衡难题：小块保留局部条件但需要更多去噪步，大块并行度高但容易过早承诺且累积缓存误差。现有方法为每个请求选择单一固定块大小，未能利用不同块大小之间的互补性。

Method: BlockBatch框架包含三个核心机制：1) 置信度门控的token合并(confidence-gated token merging)，在不同分支间选择高质量token；2) 基于领导者的同步(leader-based synchronization)，协调各分支的生成方向；3) 周期性全序列刷新(periodic full-sequence refreshes)，将局部块更新重新锚定到全局一致的KV状态。所有分支在同一批次前向传播中并行执行。

Result: 在3个代表性dLLM和4个数据集上，BlockBatch平均减少26.6%的去噪NFE，相比Fast-dLLM实现1.33倍平均端到端加速，同时保持生成质量。

Conclusion: 块大小多样性是一个实用且此前被低估的分支并行推理维度，为加速dLLM推理提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising multiple token positions in parallel, offering an attractive alternative to strictly autoregressive decoding. In practice, however, block-wise dLLM inference exposes a difficult granularity trade-off: small blocks preserve local conditioning but require many denoising steps, whereas large blocks expose more parallelism but can make premature commitments and accumulate cache error. Existing acceleration methods typically choose a single block size per request, leaving the complementarity among block sizes unused. We show that block size itself is a useful branching dimension. Different block sizes induce related but non-identical KV-cache trajectories: branches often share an initial prefix, bifurcate at semantically decisive positions, and later agree on syntactically lightweight tokens. Motivated by this structure, we propose BlockBatch, a training-free online inference framework that executes multiple block-size branches for the same request inside a batched forward pass. BlockBatch coordinates these branches through confidence-gated token merging, leader-based synchronization, and periodic full-sequence refreshes that re-anchor local block updates to a globally consistent KV state. Across 3 representative dLLMs and 4 datasets, BlockBatch reduces denoising NFEs by 26.6\% on average and achieves a 1.33$\times$ average end-to-end speedup over Fast-dLLM while preserving accuracy. These results identify block-size diversity as a practical and previously underexplored axis for branch-parallel dLLM inference.

</details>


### [243] [SigmaMedStat: Temporal Signal Modeling for ICU False Alarm Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29236)
*Arunkumar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SigmaMedStat机器学习系统，通过将ICU警报信号分割为10秒片段、提取CWT时频图、使用EfficientNet-B0编码并接入LSTM序列建模，在PhysioNet 2015数据集上达到AUC 0.822，优于静态基线模型（AUC 0.641），有效评估生理警报信号的可信度以减少临床警报疲劳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU中临床监视器每天产生大量警报（每患者350+次/天），其中72-99%临床无关，导致医护人员对警报脱敏，增加漏诊真实紧急情况的风险，构成患者安全危机。现有方法未能有效区分可信与不可信警报。

Method: 将每段60秒ICU警报记录分割为6个连续10秒片段；对每个片段生成连续小波变换（CWT）时频图；使用共享EfficientNet-B0编码器提取特征；特征序列输入两层LSTM网络进行时序建模；在PhysioNet/Computing in Cardiology Challenge 2015数据集（498条4通道ICU警报记录）上采用五折分层交叉验证评估。

Result: 五折分层交叉验证平均AUC为0.822±0.016（95% CI: [0.790,0.853]），显著优于基于完整60秒窗口的静态EfficientNet基线（AUC 0.641）。消融实验证实时间分块和多通道信号融合对性能均有独立贡献。心室扑动（AUC 0.820）识别最准确，心脏停搏（AUC 0.722）最难。主要失败模式为65个假阴性和85个高置信度误分类。

Conclusion: 提出的时序分块建模框架能有效提升ICU警报可信度评估性能，时间分块策略和多通道融合是关键贡献因素。代码与结果已公开。

Abstract: Alarm fatigue in intensive care units (ICUs) is a well documented patient safety crisis. Clinical monitors generate 350 or more alarms per patient per day, out of which 72-99% are clinically irrelevant. Staff desensitization to non-actionable alarms increases the risk of missed true emergencies. This paper presents SigmaMedStat, a machine learning system that evaluates the trustworthiness of physiological alarm signals before clinical action is taken. Four approaches were evaluated on the PhysioNet/Computing in Cardiology Challenge 2015 dataset of 498 four-channel ICU alarm recordings. Primary contribution is a temporal modeling framework that splits each 60 second recording into six consecutive 10-second chunks, and this in turn generates Continuous Wavelet Transform (CWT) scalograms per chunk, encodes each chunk with a shared EfficientNet-B0 encoder, and passes the resulting feature sequence to a two-layer Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network. Five-fold stratified cross-validation yields a mean AUC of 0.822 +/- 0.016 (95% CI: [0.790,0.853]), compared to 0.641 for a static EfficientNet baseline trained on the full 60-second window. Ablation studies confirm that temporal chunking and multi-channel signal fusion both contribute independently to classification performance. Per-alarm type analysis reveals that Ventricular Flutter is the most accurately classified alarm type (AUC 0.820) while Asystole remains the hardest (AUC 0.722). Error analysis identifies 65 false negatives and 85 high-confidence misclassifications as the primary failure modes. All code and results are publicly available at https://github.com/Arun-K-Ram/sigmamedstat.

</details>


### [244] [KLAS: Using Similarity to Stitch Neural Networks for Improved Accuracy-Efficiency Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29259)
*Debopam Sanyal,Anantharaman Iyer,Alind Khare,Trisha Jain,Akshay Jajoo,Myungjin Lee,Clayton Kerce,Alexey Tumanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KLAS框架，利用KL散度量化预训练模型间中间表示的相似性，从而自动选择最优拼接配置，构建更优的精度-效率权衡曲线，在ImageNet-1K上实现最高1.21%精度提升或1.33倍FLOPs减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拼接方法依赖启发式规则选择拼接配置，导致次优的精度-效率权衡且泛化性差。核心动机是：要构建更好的权衡曲线，需要显式捕获并利用被拼接预训练模型之间的相似性。

Method: 提出KLAS框架，通过计算中间表示的KL散度来量化模型间相似性，自动从O(k²n²)种可能的二元拼接中筛选最优配置，实现跨模型族的自动化泛化拼接选择。

Result: 在相同微调成本下，KLAS相比基线提升了拼接模型的精度-效率曲线：在ImageNet-1K上，相同计算成本下top-1精度最高提升1.21%，或在保持精度的情况下FLOPs减少1.33倍。

Conclusion: KLAS通过KL散度驱动的自动拼接选择，有效克服了现有方法的次优性和泛化性不足，为灵活模型部署提供了更优的精度-效率权衡方案。

Abstract: Given the wide range of deployment targets, flexible model selection is essential for optimizing performance within a given compute budget. Recent work demonstrates that stitching pretrained models within a model family enables cost-effective interpolation of the accuracy-efficiency tradeoff space. Stitching transforms intermediate activations from one pretrained model into another, producing a new interpolated stitched network. Such networks provide a pool of deployment options along the accuracy-efficiency spectrum. However, existing stitching approaches often yield suboptimal tradeoffs and lack generalizability, as they primarily rely on heuristics to select stitch configurations. We argue that constructing improved accuracy-efficiency tradeoffs requires explicitly capturing and leveraging the similarity between pretrained models being stitched. To this end, we introduce KLAS, a novel stitch selection framework that automates and generalizes stitch selection across model families by leveraging KL divergence between intermediate representations. KLAS identifies the most promising binary stitches from the $O(k^2n^2)$ possibilities for $k$ pretrained models of depth $n$. Through comprehensive experiments, we demonstrate that KLAS improves the accuracy-efficiency curve of stitched models at the same finetuning cost as baselines. KLAS achieves up to $1.21\%$ higher ImageNet-1K top-1 accuracy at the same computational cost, or maintains accuracy with a $1.33\times$ reduction in FLOPs.

</details>


### [245] [Robust Frequency-Calibrated Virtual EEG Channel Generation from Four Frontal Electrodes for Wearable EEG Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29263)
*Minghao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAVC-Net是一种紧凑的频率校准虚拟通道网络，能够从4个前额EEG电极（Fp1, Fp2, F7, F8）生成13个未测量的EEG通道，在PRED+CT数据集上实现了波形-频谱联合最优，相比最强非FAVC基线，对数谱距离和PSD KL散度分别降低30.09%和37.98%，并能在可穿戴式源扰动下保持频谱保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低通道可穿戴EEG仅使用四个前额电极，提供了稀疏且空间偏斜的头皮活动视图，因此需要从稀疏前额测量中生成未测量的EEG通道，以改善长期监测中的空间覆盖能力。

Method: FAVC-Net结合了共享多尺度源编码、源状态嵌入、目标条件有符号源块混合、GATv2注意力细化、注意力一致跳跃融合以及弱Welch功率谱密度校准。将稀疏到密集EEG生成视为联合强调幅度保真度、频谱分配、通道-频率纹理和对抗可穿戴输入鲁棒性的双域（时域+频域）增强问题。

Result: 在PRED+CT数据集上，FAVC-Net实现了最佳联合波形-频谱操作点。时域增益适中，但对数谱距离降低30.09%，PSD KL散度降低37.98%。在可穿戴式源扰动下，模型保持了频谱保真度并抵抗了频谱坍缩。

Conclusion: 虚拟EEG通道生成应被视为双域增强问题；生成的后部和顶叶通道应解释为源自稀疏前额测量的频率校准表示，而非独立的物理记录。

Abstract: Low-channel wearable electroencephalography (EEG) is attractive for long-term monitoring, but four frontal electrodes provide only a sparse and spatially biased view of distributed scalp activity. We present FAVC-Net, a compact frequency-calibrated virtual-channel network that generates 13 unmeasured EEG channels from Fp1, Fp2, F7, and F8. The model combines shared multi-scale source encoding, source-state embeddings, target-conditioned signed source-block mixing, GATv2-based attention refinement, attention-consistent skip fusion, and weak Welch power spectral density calibration. Rather than treating sparse-to-dense EEG generation as a purely waveform-matching task, the framework jointly emphasizes amplitude fidelity, spectral allocation, channel-frequency texture, and robustness to corrupted wearable inputs. On the PRED+CT dataset, FAVC-Net achieved the best joint waveform-spectral operating point among neural and interpolation baselines. Its time-domain gains were modest, whereas log-spectral distance and PSD KL divergence were reduced by 30.09% and 37.98% relative to the strongest non-FAVC comparator. Under wearable-like source perturbations, the model preserved spectral fidelity and resisted spectral collapse. These results support virtual EEG channel generation as a dual-domain augmentation problem, while emphasizing that generated posterior and parietal channels should be interpreted as frequency-calibrated representations derived from sparse frontal measurements rather than as independent physical recordings.

</details>


### [246] [LoopFM: Learning frOm HistOrical RePresentations of Foundation Model for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29280)
*Shali Jiang,Hua Zheng,Boyang Liu,Laming Chen,Kenny Lov,Chuanqi Xu,Lisang Ding,Qinghai Zhou,Can Cui,Xiaolong Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yasmine Badr,Xin Xu,Jiyan Yang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Gerard Jonathan Mugisha Akkerhuis,Chenxiao Guan,Rong Jin,Ruichao Qiu,Xian Chen,Shifu Xu,Zhehui Zhou,Ping Chen,Rui Yang,Haicheng Chen,Xiangge Meng,Song Zhou,Dharak Kharod,Shuyu Xu,Qiang Jin,Qiao Yang,Wankun Zhu,Qin Huang,Yuzhen Huang,Darren Liu,Parish Aggarwal,Hui Zhou,Erzhuo Wang,Shuo Chang,Xiaorui Gan,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Huayu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopFM通过将大模型（FM）的中间层嵌入结构化为下游垂直模型（VM）的输入特征（如用户历史序列），构建高带宽知识传输通道，无需实时FM推理或架构耦合，显著提升知识迁移比率（如KD基础上翻倍），在工业级系统（万亿参数FM、数十亿样本）带来+0.5%~+1.22%的转化率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏（KD）仅从大模型（FM）传递单一标量预测给垂直模型（VM），随着FM规模增大，单标量无法承载FM学习的丰富中间知识，导致迁移比率（VM捕获的FM提升比例）不断下降，成为KD的瓶颈。

Method: LoopFM将FM的中间层嵌入（如用户历史序列）结构化后作为VM的输入特征，构建高带宽传输通道；无需在服务阶段实时调用FM推理，也无需FM与VM的架构耦合；同时提供增益分解与迁移比率分析的理论框架。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，LoopFM实现显著AUC提升（如TaobaoAd上+6%+）；在工业级系统（数十亿样本、万亿参数FM）中，LoopFM在KD基础上将知识迁移比率大约翻倍，分别带来Y1H1中+0.5%转化率提升、Y1H2中两个独立部署分别+1.03%和+1.22%的转化率提升。

Conclusion: LoopFM通过利用FM的中间层表示作为特征输入，有效突破了KD的单标量瓶颈，实现了更高的知识迁移比率，且与KD互补，在大规模工业系统中验证了显著的业务效果提升。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers a single scalar prediction from a large foundation model (FM) to compact vertical models (VMs), suffering from diminishing transfer ratio -- the fraction of FM improvement captured by the VM -- as a single scalar cannot convey the rich intermediate knowledge that larger FMs learn. To address this bottleneck, we propose LoopFM (Learning frOm HistOrical ReP*resentations of FM), a framework that opens a high-bandwidth transfer channel by structuring FM intermediate embeddings as input features (e.g., user history sequence) for downstream VMs, without requiring real-time FM inference at serving and architectural coupling between FM and VM. We provide a theoretical framework for LoopFM with a gain decomposition and transfer-ratio analysis. On three public benchmarks, LoopFM demonstrates strong AUC improvements (e.g., 6\%+ on TaobaoAd) and complementary knowledge transfer capability with KD. On industrial-scale systems (billions of examples, trillion-parameter FMs), LoopFM approximately doubles the knowledge transfer ratio on top of KD, delivering a +0.5\% conversion improvement in Y1H1, and a +1.03\% and +1.22\% conversion improvement from two individual launches respectively in Y1H2.

</details>


### [247] [Do Physics Foundation Models Learn Generalizable Physics? A Bias-Aware Benchmark Across Physical Regimes and Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29283)
*Mengdi Chu,Yang Liu,Ayan Biswas,Han-Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过构建包含8种物理动力学、3种训练数据混合和25种测试场景的基准，对5种物理基础模型架构及其变体进行了60,000次测量，发现当前物理基础模型表现为条件性通用而非普适性通用，其泛化能力受物理机制、时间尺度、初始条件、预训练、模型大小和架构等多种因素制约，仅改进训练数据分布、预训练或缩放模型都无法可靠消除其能力偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理基础模型声称具有通用时空预测能力，但其评估往往在固定训练分布下仅报告单一平均分数，导致无法判断模型是否真正学习了可泛化的物理动力学，还是仅在特定设置下表现良好。

Method: 构建了一个包含8种物理动力学、3种训练数据混合和25种测试场景（涵盖分布内、分布偏移和分布外设置）的基准，评估了5种物理基础模型架构及每种架构的4种变体（scratch和三种不同规模的预训练版本），共获得60,000次测量。

Result: 当前物理基础模型表现为条件性通用而非普适性通用；其泛化能力依赖于物理机制、时间尺度、初始条件、预训练、模型大小和架构；改进训练数据分布只能部分缓解这一限制；预训练和缩放模型也无法可靠消除能力偏差。

Conclusion: 改进物理基础模型需要超越简单的模型缩放或数据扩展，转向学习能够更好捕获跨机制、时间尺度和分布偏移的可迁移物理知识的机制。

Abstract: Recent physics foundation models claim general spatiotemporal forecasting ability, yet their evaluations often collapse performance into a single average score under a fixed training distribution. This makes it difficult to determine whether a model has learned generalizable physical dynamics or only performs well under particular settings. We construct a benchmark with 8 physical dynamics, 3 training-data mixtures, and 25 test regimes induced by dynamic-scale and initial-condition complexity shifts, covering in-distribution, distribution-shift, and out-of-distribution settings. We evaluate five physics foundation model architectures and four model variants per architecture (scratch and three pretrained sizes), resulting in 60,000 measurements. Our results show that current physics foundation models behave as conditional rather than universal generalists: their generality depends on the physical regime, temporal scale, initial-condition setting, pretraining, model size, and architecture. Improving the training data distribution only partially mitigates this limitation. Pretraining and scaling are also unable to reliably remove their ability biases. We argue that improving physics foundation models requires moving beyond scaling models or expanding data, toward learning mechanisms that better capture transferable physical knowledge across regimes, temporal scales, and distribution shifts.

</details>


### [248] [NeuroEdge: Real-Time Hand Gesture Recognition with High-Density EMG Using Deep Learning at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29326)
*Peter Chudinov,Zhenyu Lin,Jay Motamarry,Srihita Panati,Xiaorong Zhang,Zhuwei Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroEdge系统，在资源受限的微控制器上实现了基于192通道高密度肌电信号（HD-EMG）的实时手势识别，通过ESP32与Sony Spresense双微控制器架构、轻量级1D-CNN模型以及DMA+SPI流水线同步设计，在7种手势分类中达到90%实时准确率和83ms平均延迟，证明了在边缘设备上部署复杂HD-EMG深度学习推理的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HD-EMG在神经-机器接口中潜力巨大，但深度学习模型受限于计算和内存约束，难以部署到嵌入式硬件上，现有方法无法在资源受限的微控制器上实现实时高分辨率肌电信号处理与推理。

Method: 设计NeuroEdge系统，包含HD-EMG StreamBridge（通过无线通信将Quattrocento放大器数据流式传输至ESP32微控制器）和EdgeDL Inference Engine（在Sony Spresense微控制器上执行轻量级深度学习推理）；采用1D-CNN模型进行实时滑动窗口处理；利用DMA数据传输和SPI突发通信实现ESP32与Spresense之间的流水线同步。

Result: 在192通道前臂HD-EMG数据上，7种手势的实时分类准确率达90%，平均延迟为83ms。

Conclusion: NeuroEdge证明了在基于微控制器的边缘设备上部署复杂HD-EMG手势识别的可行性，弥合了高分辨率生物信号采集与深度学习嵌入式推理之间的差距，为下一代神经-机器接口提供了可行方案。

Abstract: High-density electromyography (HD-EMG) has emerged as a powerful modality for decoding fine-grained neuromuscular activity, enabling real-time neural-machine interfaces (NMIs) for applications such as prosthetic control, rehabilitation, and augmented interaction. While deep learning approaches such as convolutional neural networks (CNNs)have demonstrated high classification accuracy for EMG-based gesture recognition, their deployment on embedded hardware remains a major challenge due to computational and memory constraints. This paper presents NeuroEdge, a real-time HD EMG-based NMI system that performs gesture recognition entirely on resource-constrained microcontrollers. The system features two custom-designed modules: the HD-EMG StreamBridge, a wireless communication interface that streams raw HD-EMG data from a Quattrocento amplifier to an ESP32 microcontroller; and the EdgeDL Inference Engine, a lightweight deep learning framework executing on a Sony Spresense microcontroller. A compact 1-dimensional CNN optimized for embedded inference processes, sliding windows of EMG data in real time. Data streaming and inference are pipelined and synchronized through an architecture that utilizes Direct Memory Access (DMA) for data transfer and Serial Peripheral Interface (SPI) burst communication between the ESP32 and Spresense, ensuring low-latency performance. Experimental results show that NeuroEdge achieves a real-time classification accuracy of 90% across seven hand gestures, with a total average latency of 83 ms using 192 channels of HD-EMG recorded from the forearm. Our system demonstrates the feasibility of deploying complex HD-EMG-based gesture recognition on microcontroller-based edge devices, bridging the gap between high-resolution biosignal acquisition and deep learning-based embedded inference for next-generation NMIs.

</details>


### [249] [Neural-Behavioral Representation of Natural Whole-body Movement in Monkeys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29355)
*Jieshi He,Puzhe Li,Yanan Sui,Mu-ming Poo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自由活动猴子的神经-行为记录与建模框架，结合大规模硬膜外皮层信号和多视角动作捕捉，利用自回归编码器-解码器模型成功解码了自然全身运动，无需显式物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动解码研究受限于约束任务和有限肢体运动，难以理解皮层活动如何表征灵长类动物的自然全身行为，主要挑战在于运动多样性以及大规模神经表征的不可及性。

Method: 构建定制化数据采集平台，同步采集自由活动猴子多个感觉运动相关脑区的大规模硬膜外皮层信号和多视角动作捕捉数据；使用自回归编码器-解码器模型学习紧凑的行为先验，并以神经信号为条件进行全身运动解码。

Result: 模型能够解码准确且逼真的全身运动，无需显式物理约束即实现了对自然行为的有效重建。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用大规模颅内神经活动解码灵长类自然全身运动提供了新颖的概念验证方法。

Abstract: Understanding how cortical activity represents natural whole-body behaviors in primates remains challenging. Limited by the diversity of movements and inaccessibility of large-scale neural representation of whole-body kinematics, previous motor decoding studies focused on constrained tasks and limited limb movements. Here, we present a neural-behavioral recording and modeling framework for freely moving monkeys, combining large-scale epidural cortical signals from distributed sensory- and motor-related areas with synchronized multi-view motion capture through a custom-made data collection platform. We reconstructed whole-body monkey kinematics and learned a compact behavior prior using an autoregressive encoder-decoder model. Conditioned on neural signals, the model decoded accurate and realistic whole-body movement without explicit physical constraints. Our results provide a novel proof-of-concept approach for decoding natural whole-body movements in primates using large-scale intracranial neural activity.

</details>


### [250] [Solving Integer Linear Programming with Parallel Tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29366)
*Kyuil Sim,Sanghyeok Choi,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需求解器、无需训练的基于采样的整数线性规划优化框架，采用局部平衡提议和并行回火（包括惩罚回火）技术，在多个基准测试中取得了优于或媲美经典求解器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ILP求解方法存在两类局限：经典精确求解器/启发式方法复杂，而基于学习的方法泛化能力差且依赖外部求解器。本文旨在提出一种无需求解器、无需训练、可直接探索离散可行区域的采样优化框架。

Method: 提出无求解器的采样优化框架：1）利用线性结构设计局部平衡提议构建转移核，避免梯度近似；2）集成并行回火克服ILP能量景观的多模态性；3）引入惩罚回火机制，在可行解上调节约束屏障的同时保留目标景观。

Result: 该方法在所有四个基准测试中一致优于SCIP，在200秒预算内与Gurobi在两项任务上相当或更优，对分布偏移的鲁棒性显著优于学习型方法，且在MIPLIB 2017实例上无需问题特定调优即可与经典求解器竞争。

Conclusion: 提出的采样框架有效解决了ILP优化问题，无需外部求解器或训练过程，在性能、泛化能力和鲁棒性方面均表现出色，为ILP提供了一种新的求解范式。

Abstract: Integer Linear Programming (ILP) serves as a versatile framework for modeling a wide range of combinatorial optimization problems, typically addressed by sophisticated exact solvers or heuristics. While learning-based approaches have recently shown their effectiveness, they suffer from poor generalization to out-of-distribution instances and inherent dependence on external solvers. In this work, we propose a solver-free, sampling-based optimization framework for ILP that directly explores discrete feasible regions without training or external solvers. Exploiting the linear structure of ILP, we employ a Locally-Balanced Proposal to construct a transition kernel, thereby avoiding the gradient approximation. To overcome the highly multimodal nature of ILP energy landscapes, we integrate Parallel Tempering. In addition to standard temperature tempering, we introduce penalty tempering, which modulates constraint barriers while preserving the objective landscape over feasible solutions. Empirically, our method consistently outperforms SCIP across all four benchmarks, matches or exceeds Gurobi on two of four tasks within a 200-second budget, and is substantially more robust to distribution shift than learning-based methods. Furthermore, on MIPLIB 2017 instances, our framework remains competitive with classical solvers without any problem-specific tuning.

</details>


### [251] [Deep Adaptive Dimension Reduction for Bayesian Inference in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29373)
*Yueyang Wang,Xili Wang,Kejun Tang,Xiaoliang Wan,Tao Zhou,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于变分流（VF）模型的深度自适应降维贝叶斯推断框架，用于高维PDE控制反问题，通过集成VAE非线性降维、双归一化流和迭代先验更新策略，结合自适应微调FNO代理模型，在100维Rosenbrock问题和三个标准PDE反问题中取得了优于MCMC、UKI和SVGD的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维PDE控制反问题面临后验分布非高斯、正演模型计算昂贵以及先验信息误设三大挑战，现有方法难以同时有效应对这些问题。

Method: 提出VF模型，将VAE非线性降维与潜在先验和编码器的双归一化流相结合，突破标准归一化流双射映射的降维限制；引入迭代先验更新策略，逐步将先验均值移向高概率后验区域；结合自适应微调FNO代理模型，形成闭环自适应循环。

Result: 在100维Rosenbrock问题和三个标准PDE反问题中，该方法在所有测试配置下均达到竞争性/优越精度，在高噪声观测和高维参数空间等挑战场景优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过降维、自适应先验和代理模型协同优化，有效解决了高维PDE反问题中的多重挑战，为复杂反问题提供了高效的贝叶斯推断方案。

Abstract: Solving high-dimensional PDE-governed inverse problems is often challenging due to complex non-Gaussian posterior distributions, expensive forward model evaluations, and misspecified prior information. To address these issues, we propose a deep adaptive dimension-reduction Bayesian inference framework based on the Variational Flow (VF) model. Since standard normalizing flows are restricted by bijective mappings and cannot directly reduce dimensions, VF overcomes this limitation by integrating VAE-based nonlinear dimension reduction with dual normalizing flows for the latent prior and encoder. This design provides a strictly higher evidence lower bound than VAE and allows more flexible approximation of complex posterior distributions. We further introduce an iterative prior updating strategy that gradually moves the prior mean toward high-probability posterior regions, avoiding manual prior tuning. These components form a closed adaptive loop together with an adaptively fine-tuned Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) surrogate: VF generates posterior-concentrated samples to refine the surrogate, while the updated surrogate further improves posterior inference. Numerical experiments on a 100-dimensional Rosenbrock problem and three standard PDE-governed inverse problems show that our method delivers competitive or superior accuracy compared with MCMC, UKI, and SVGD baselines across all tested configurations, with the most pronounced advantages emerging in challenging scenarios such as high-noise observations and high-dimensional parameter spaces.

</details>


### [252] [TRACER: Persistent Regularization for Robust Multimodal Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29380)
*Hesam Asadollahzadeh,Feng Liu,Christopher Leckie,Sarah M. Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACER框架，通过理论分析揭示多模态对比微调中灾难性遗忘的机制，证明WMA教师比EMA更优，并设计了一种结合对比学习与WMA引导多视角蒸馏的正则化方法，有效提升CLIP微调中的分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流多模态模型微调策略常导致灾难性遗忘，即分布外鲁棒性显著下降。现有正则化方法（如自蒸馏）和EMA教师策略存在未被充分认识的理论缺陷，亟需理论框架来指导更鲁棒的微调方法设计。

Method: 1. 构建多模态对比微调的理论框架，推导闭式解和几何分解；2. 分析证明自蒸馏优于其他正则化方法；3. 发现EMA教师存在坍缩问题；4. 证明WMA教师可在有限时间窗口内保持持续正则化力，实现任务子空间的无偏收敛同时保留正交知识；5. 提出TRACER：对比学习结合WMA引导的多视角蒸馏。

Result: 在CLIP微调上的大量实验表明，TRACER在三种骨干架构上均实现了分布外准确率和校准度的一致提升；全面的消融实验验证了方法的原理正确性和对超参数的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: WMA教师能有效避免EMA的坍缩问题，TRACER提供了一种有理论依据且实践鲁棒的多模态模型鲁棒微调方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Mainstream strategies for finetuning pretrained multimodal models often degrade out-of-distribution (OOD) robustness, a phenomenon known as catastrophic forgetting. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework for multimodal contrastive finetuning, yielding closed-form solutions and a geometric decomposition for each strategy. This framework shows that self-distillation is more effective than other regularization approaches to retain the knowledge of the pretrained model. Our analysis reveals a largely overlooked limitation: standard Exponential Moving Average (EMA) teachers, widely used in robust finetuning, suffer from collapse. To solve this, we prove that a Weighted Moving Average (WMA) teacher maintains a persistent regularizing force over finite horizons and yields bias-free convergence in the task subspace while preserving orthogonal knowledge. These insights motivate **TRACER** (**T**rajectory-**R**obust **A**nchoring for **C**ontrastive **E**ncoder **R**egularization), which combines contrastive learning with WMA-guided multi-perspective distillation. Extensive experiments on CLIP finetuning demonstrate consistent OOD accuracy and calibration gains across three backbone architectures, and comprehensive ablations confirm that TRACER is both principled and robust to hyperparameter choices. Code is available at [https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER](https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER).

</details>


### [253] [GDSD: Reinforcement Learning as Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29398)
*Xiaohang Tang,Keyue Jiang,Che Liu,Qifang Zhao,Xiaoxiao Xu,Sangwoong Yoon,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出引导式去噪器自蒸馏（GDSD），通过免似然的直接蒸馏避免了ELBO代理似然带来的训练-推理不匹配偏差，在规划、数学和代码基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ELBO的RL方法虽然高效，但由于使用ELBO作为似然代理引入了训练-推理不匹配偏差（TIM bias），导致性能退化。直接使用标准RL又面临扩散LLM策略似然不可解的问题。

Method: GDSD：从逆向KL正则化RL的闭式最优解中推导出优势引导的自教师模型，通过免归一化的目标函数将去噪器logits与教师对齐，将RL转化为免似然的自蒸馏过程，从而绕过TIM偏差。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B上的规划、数学和代码基准测试中，GDSD一致超越现有最先进的ELBO方法，训练奖励动态更稳定，测试准确率最高提升19.6%。

Conclusion: 直接的去噪器自蒸馏无需依赖ELBO似然代理，能够为扩散LLM提供更稳定、更有效的RL训练过程。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can be used to improve the policy (denoiser) of diffusion large language models (dLLMs), while being hindered by the intractability of the policy likelihood. A dominant and efficient family of methods replaces the likelihood in standard RL with its evidence lower bound (ELBO), estimated from randomly masked sequences. Despite being well aligned with pre-training, these approaches introduce bias through training--inference mismatch by using the ELBO as a likelihood surrogate, which can degrade performance. In this work, we propose Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation (GDSD) to directly distill the denoiser of dLLMs from an advantage-guided self-teacher, derived from the closed-form optimum of reverse-KL regularized RL. GDSD matches the dLLM's denoiser logits to the teacher's via a normalization-free objective, which reduces RL to likelihood-free self-distillation and thus bypasses the TIM biases. Recent ELBO-based methods emerge as instances of applying different distillation divergences, but with diagnosable pathologies that GDSD avoids. On planning, math, and coding benchmarks with LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B, GDSD consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art ELBO-based methods with a more stable training reward dynamics, achieving test-accuracy improvements of up to $+19.6\%$. These results suggest that direct denoiser self-distillation, without relying on an ELBO likelihood surrogate, can provide a more stable and effective RL procedure for dLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/GaryBall/GDSD.

</details>


### [254] [Rethinking Post-Training Recipes for Multimodal Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29401)
*Haoxin Liu,Yichen Zhou,Rajat Sen,B. Aditya Prakash,Abhimanyu Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PostTime通过后训练LLM结合强化学习，使其能利用多模态上下文信息修正数值TSFM的预测结果，在TimesX基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在零样本单模态预测中表现优异，但无法像LLM那样利用多模态、非数值的上下文信息（如文本、图像等），而这些信息往往对真实世界的轨迹有重要影响。作者旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出PostTime后训练方案：结合监督微调（SFT）和带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），并设计自动化推理轨迹生成方法。该方案训练LLM根据多模态上下文做出三种干预决策——修正、保留或忽略TSFM先验预测。

Result: 在TimesX多模态预测基准上，使用Gemma-3-4B LLM和TimesFM-2.5 TSFM进行评估，PostTime显著优于独立的TSFM、仅用LLM的基线以及现有的多模态预测方法。

Conclusion: 多模态上下文能够有效引导LLM修正数值时间序列预测，PostTime的后训练范式为融合TSFM的数值优势与LLM的上下文理解能力提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) excel at zero-shot unimodal forecasting using numerical data, but unlike LLMs they cannot consume multimodal, non-numerical context that often shape real-world trajectories. In this work, we bridge this gap and argue for a multimodal time-series forecasting approach that post-trains LLMs to act as context-guided revisors over strong numerical TSFM priors. We introduce PostTime, a post-training recipe combining Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), along with a methodology to generate automated reasoning traces for forecast revisions. PostTime teaches an LLM to generate context-conditioned forecast interventions -- decisions to revise, preserve, or ignore the TSFM prior based on the multimodal context. We evaluate this approach on the TimesX multimodal forecasting benchmark using a Gemma-3-4B LLM and TimesFM-2.5 TSFM, and show that it significantly outperforms standalone TSFMs, LLM-only baselines, and existing multimodal forecasting approaches.

</details>


### [255] [Information-Directed Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29405)
*Keru Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息导向采样(IDS)框架用于离线到在线决策，通过条件互信息形式化残差不确定性，证明IDS在贝叶斯遗憾界上继承汤普森采样性质，并在线性奖励模型和D4RL实验中验证其在离线数据存在偏差或低概率残差不确定性的场景下优于汤普森采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据虽能减少不确定性，但无法消除探索需求，反而改变了需要探索的残差不确定性。现有方法缺乏对这种残差不确定性的形式化刻画和相应的探索策略设计。

Method: (1) 用条件互信息 I(χ;τ_{1:T}|D_N) 形式化残差不确定性；(2) 提出参数化族 IDS(η≥0)，通过即时遗憾与信息增益的权衡选择动作；(3) 通过比率证书证明IDS继承任意汤普森采样策略的信息比界；(4) 在已知动力学贝叶斯线性奖励模型中推导对数行列式形式的条件互信息和遗憾界。

Result: (1) 在贝叶斯线性奖励模型中，vanilla IDS (η=0) 达到 Õ(Hd min{√T, T√C†/N}) 遗憾界；(2) 存在warm-start场景，IDS会选择有信息量的探测动作而汤普森采样不会，实现常数因子的贝叶斯遗憾分离；(3) D4RL实验验证IDS在离线数据信息丰富但存在偏差或低概率残差不确定性的场景中效果最佳。

Conclusion: IDS在离线数据信息充分但存在有偏或低概率残差不确定性时最有效，这一机制适用于离线强化学习、离线黑箱优化和贝叶斯优化等多个领域。

Abstract: Decision-making from offline datasets typically warm-starts a policy or score model from fixed offline data and then refines it with limited online interaction. Offline data reduces uncertainty, but it does not remove the need for exploration; it changes what remains to be explored. We formalise this residual uncertainty by the conditional mutual information $I(χ;τ_{1:T}\mid\mathcal{D}_N)$ between a learning target $χ$ and the online trajectories after conditioning on the offline dataset. This view leads naturally to information-directed sampling (IDS), a family parameterised by $η\ge 0$ that selects actions by trading off instantaneous regret against information gain. We prove a generic offline-to-online Bayesian regret bound for IDS through a ratio certificate: any information-ratio bound satisfied by a reference Thompson-sampling policy over the same randomised policy class is inherited by IDS. In a known-dynamics Bayesian linear-reward model, the conditional mutual information has a log-determinant form, and vanilla IDS ($η=0$) satisfies $\widetilde O\!\left(Hd\min\left\{\sqrt T,\,T\sqrt{C^\dagger_{β,\mathrm{IDS}_0}(N,T)/N}\right\}\right),$ where the coverage coefficient is tied to the visitation distribution induced by vanilla IDS itself. We also identify a warm-start regime with a dominated but informative probe in which vanilla IDS selects the probe while Thompson sampling never does, giving a constant-factor Bayesian regret separation. Controlled bandit experiments and D4RL offline-to-online RL experiments validate this mechanism: IDS is most beneficial when offline data is informative but leaves biased or low-probability residual uncertainty that targeted online actions can resolve, a regime shared by offline RL, offline black-box optimization, and Bayesian optimization.

</details>


### [256] [How Much Is a Dataset Worth? Scaling Laws, the Vendi Score, and Matrix Spectral Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29448)
*Jeff A. Bilmes,Gantavya Bhatt,Arnav M. Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了神经缩放定律目标函数与Vendi Score均满足子模性，发现Vendi Score是更广泛的矩阵谱函数（包括行列式点过程等）的特例，并提出了弱矩阵单调函数概念以扩展弱子模矩阵谱函数族。通过设计基于割线方程的更新方法，将边际增益评估的复杂度降低O(m)倍，实现了约35,000倍的实际加速，使得ImageNet-1K规模数据集的直接优化成为可能。实验表明，设施选址函数在预测子集下游性能方面表现最佳；Vendi Score在中等分数范围内具有预测性，但极端优化会导致其成为较差的下游性能代理；均匀随机采样子集在评分和性能上表现出惊人的集中性；即使在控制大小、类别平衡和训练预算等因素后，性能仍呈现从好到差的平滑变化，表明这些因素单独并不能决定数据价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据评估方法中，神经缩放定律仅通过数据集规模评估数据，而Vendi Score虽能衡量数据集价值但缺乏与子模优化的理论联系及大规模数据集上的高效优化方法。本文旨在建立两者间的理论桥梁，并实现可扩展的数据价值评估框架。

Method: 1) 理论构建：证明神经缩放定律目标与Vendi Score均为子模函数，揭示Vendi Score是矩阵谱函数（含DPP等）的特例，引入弱矩阵单调函数及弱子模矩阵谱函数族。2) 算法优化：提出基于割线方程的更新方法，避免重复特征分解，将边际增益计算的复杂度从O(m^3)降至O(m^2)，实现约35,000倍加速。3) 实验评估：在多个数据集上比较Vendi Score、DPP、设施选址等目标在固定大小、类别平衡、固定训练预算等场景下的子集选择性能。

Result: 1) 设施选址函数在预测子集下游性能方面表现最佳。2) Vendi Score在中等分数范围内具有预测性，但过度优化会导致其成为较差的下游性能代理。3) 均匀随机采样子集在评估分数和下游性能上均表现出惊人的集中性。4) 即使控制大小、类别平衡和训练预算，性能仍呈现从好到差的平滑变化范围，表明这些因素单独不能完全决定数据价值。

Conclusion: 本文建立了神经缩放定律目标与Vendi Score之间的子模性理论联系，提出了高效的优化算法，并通过大规模实验揭示了不同数据评估目标在实际应用中的优劣。设施选址函数是最佳的数据价值预测指标，而Vendi Score的极端优化可能导致误判，同时强调了数据价值的多维性。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws appraise data through dataset size, while the Vendi Score uses quantum entropy to measure dataset value.
